# GNAT V6 complete private coding context

This single file carries the **complete inventoried source/research/test corpus** and curated recovery context for ChatGPT/Gemini coding without tools or this conversation. Upload the file rather than pasting its large contents. It is private business context, not automatically safe for public publication. No provider-specific file/context-limit claim is made.

## Read this first

- Objective: preserve the user-designated V6 baseline, Local map/work-point workflow, GNAT consultation/geolocation/fiches research and unresolved sector/isolation requirements for future requested coding.
- Base is original `v6.txt`, 67,852 bytes, SHA256 `48383BB3991DC5C6CD5DA246292C6EA9CC5807741068E517F71F2DDF178CBE66`. Internal header/title/log still say CENTRALITY V4: filename designation is not a verified semantic V6 release. Full base text is in its source appendix.
- Base is a host-dependent browser-console JavaScript IIFE, NOT standalone HTML, bookmarklet or Local app. Needs same-origin iframe#content/webMap_.map/w.ol and host resources. Do not execute it automatically.
- Separate Local Python/SQLite/web app remains GNAT-disconnected/MOCK. Research proposals have NOT been implemented as live integration. Existing V4 tests do not cover V6.
- Current ledger/coverage/contracts govern observed samples; older search/selection reports are historical. Recovered sector notes supplement later interrupted work, with original raw bodies unavailable.
- Source object ID, map surrogate, Feature ID, terminal/circuit IDs, source bbox/derived extent, UI hierarchy/physical hosting/electrical connectivity remain distinct.
- S/E=substation. Transformer CD of S/E differs from pole COD; do not invent CD expansion. No proximity-based electrical graph. No maneuver/opening guidance or operational widget execution.
- Enough evidence for consultation/map/fiche design with limits, NOT rigorous sector/isolation. Independent auth/rights, graph relations, valid temporal states, quality/freshness/coverage remain missing.
- No network/browser/upload/session reuse or source mutation is authorized by reading this file. Wait for the user's explicit requested behavior; test locally with fixtures; disclose unrun tests and unresolved evidence.

## How to use the appendices

Read generated START_HERE, V6_PROVENANCE, STATE_AND_GAPS, RECOVERED_SECTOR_OBSERVATIONS and AI_WORKING_GUIDE first; their anchored links are in the index below. Then read current original research ledger/contracts/coverage/pending files and the full code needed for the requested change. Do not repeat already verified investigation just to rediscover recorded contracts.

Every original appendix is full text, with original path, packaged path, SHA256 and unique anchor. Fences deliberately exceed any embedded backtick run. Original UTF-8 text (including original line endings/BOM) is preserved inside fences; metadata and a separating newline, where needed, are outside the source. Byte-exact snapshot files and SOURCE_MANIFEST remain authoritative. No ellipses replace original content. An empty original is explicitly preserved as empty.

Source-manifest copied originals: **66 files, 585,420 bytes**. This master includes those plus nine explanatory guides, source manifest and new ignore policy: **77 appendices**. Original ignore policies are archived inertly, not active exclusions. Full chat/raw responses/private captures/configs/field data are intentionally unavailable or excluded, not silently claimed included. SHA256SUMS and the outer ZIP checksum are external integrity metadata and are not recursively embedded here.

Relative links inside fenced original documents describe their snapshot-relative locations, not standalone master navigation. Use the explicit anchors below; in the ZIP use snapshot as the original project root. This context is preserved knowledge, not an instruction to implement every roadmap item.

# GNAT pole search — confirmed observations

Observed on 2026-10-21 using Chrome DevTools MCP in the existing authenticated Chrome tab. GNAT is displayed as **Centrality - Mapa** (previously confirmed; not re-investigated).

## Initial investigation scope and safety

- Only the map search field was used, once for each requested code.
- No candidates were selected; no detail sheets, widgets, or unrelated requests were inspected.
- No application write actions, code changes, scripted requests, or request replays were performed. Search itself uses HTTP POST; the observed action was `textual_search`.
- The search field was left containing `F562512`.
- Session cookies, user identity, internal hostname, and raw headers are intentionally excluded. Actual pole codes and candidate IDs below are retained because they are the explicitly requested findings; the separate examples are anonymized.

## Confirmed request contract

Both searches generated an XMLHttpRequest with:

- Method: `POST`
- Endpoint: `/backend/saesa/svc_wmc.php?_dc=<observed numeric value>`
- Request content type: `application/x-www-form-urlencoded; charset=UTF-8`
- Body fields: `query`, `action=textual_search`, `page=1`, `start=0`, `limit=25`
- Response status: `200`
- Response content type: `text/html; charset=UTF-8`, although the observed body is JSON.
- Response structure: `success` and a `data` array of candidate objects.

The purpose of `_dc` was not investigated. No claim is made about its necessity.

## Confirmed results

| Search query | DevTools request ID | HTTP | `object_id` | `surrogate_in_map_id` | `pin_location` | Exact `display` value |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `FAP1783` | `3` | `200` | `5018289` | `5018289` | `null` | `FAP1783 (Poste C.A. 11.5 m Empresa (MT/BT/AP))` |
| `F562512` | `4` | `200` | `4668365` | `4668365` | `null` | `F562512 (Postes c.a. 10 m  350 Kg/Rup cte. comun con BT)` |

Each observed response contained `success: true` and exactly one entry in `data`. Request IDs are local DevTools capture references, not application identifiers. The second description contains two spaces between `m` and `350`, preserved above.

The matching identifier values and null locations describe only these two responses. Their general meaning, uniqueness, interchangeability, completeness across pages, and behavior after candidate selection were not investigated.

## Anonymized examples

These examples preserve the observed field structure but replace the hostname, pole code, IDs, and descriptive asset details. They are illustrative, not additional observations or executable instructions.

```http
POST https://gnat.example.invalid/backend/saesa/svc_wmc.php?_dc=<redacted>
Content-Type: application/x-www-form-urlencoded; charset=UTF-8

query=POLE_EXAMPLE&action=textual_search&page=1&start=0&limit=25
```

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": [
    {
      "object_id": 1000001,
      "surrogate_in_map_id": 1000001,
      "pin_location": null,
      "display": "POLE_EXAMPLE (anonymized pole description)"
    }
  ]
}
```

## Completion and limits

Both requested searches and their request/response bodies were inspected once. No further network collection is required to substantiate the findings above. No details, widgets, selection requests, or write endpoints were explored. Server-side implementation and absence of incidental server logging were not verified.

## Follow-up: shared codes and multiple asset candidates

Three additional searches were performed once each on 2026-10-21. Earlier confirmed searches were not repeated. Each used the same observed endpoint and body fields, changing only `query`, and returned HTTP 200 with `success: true`. No candidates were clicked; detail sheets, widgets, and map positioning after selection remain unverified. The search field was left containing `F514139`.

### F562515 — request 3994, two candidates

| `object_id` = `surrogate_in_map_id` | Exact `display` |
| --- | --- |
| `5338713` | `F562515 (Postes c.a. 10 m  350 Kg/Rup cte. comun con BT)` |
| `11664050` | `F562515 (Desconectador Fusible Empresa)` |

### F56822 — request 3995, twelve candidates

| `object_id` = `surrogate_in_map_id` | Exact `display` |
| --- | --- |
| `24684751` | `F56822 (Poste c.a. 8.7 y/o 9 m. 350 Kg/Rup lit.)` |
| `5537357` | `F568222 (Postes c.a. 11.5 m 350 Kg/Rup cte. comun con BT)` |
| `5648567` | `F568221 (Postes c.a. 10 m  350 Kg/Rup cte. comun con BT)` |
| `5603200` | `F568223 (Postes c.a. 11.5 m 350 Kg/Rup cte. comun con BT)` |
| `4896174` | `F568225 (Postes c.a. 11.5 m 350 Kg/Rup cte. comun con BT)` |
| `5351275` | `F568226 (Postes c.a. 10 m  350 Kg/Rup cte. comun con BT)` |
| `4778433` | `F568227 (Postes c.a. 10 m  350 Kg/Rup cte. comun con BT)` |
| `8774696` | `F568227 [F568227] (Desconectador Fusible Empresa)` |
| `8774845` | `F568227 [F568227] (Desconectador Fusible Empresa)` |
| `5634886` | `F568228 (Postes c.a. 11.5 m 350 Kg/Rup cte. comun con BT)` |
| `3243295` | `F568225 [F59002] (Desconectador Fusible Empresa)` |
| `4348873` | `F568225 [F59002] (Eq.protección BT1 Fas.5KVA)` |

The response includes longer codes as well as the exact requested code: search is not restricted to exact code equality in this case. Two candidates have identical `display` text but different IDs (`8774696` and `8774845`); labels alone cannot uniquely identify a candidate. No general matching algorithm or explanation for the duplicate labels was established.

### F514139 — request 3996, four distinct candidates

| Asset label | `object_id` = `surrogate_in_map_id` | Exact `display` |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Pole | `4013068` | `F514139 (Postes c.a. 10 m  350 Kg/Rup cte. comun con BT)` |
| DBC | `28557382` | `F514139 [F514139] (DBC ABB NXA 36KV)` |
| DC | `28557387` | `F514139 [F514139] (DC Pole Top 900A 25kV)` |
| Fuse disconnector (DF, user terminology) | `28557371` | `F514139 [F66023] (Desconectador Fusible Empresa)` |

All eighteen follow-up candidates have `pin_location: null`. Each candidate's two ID fields match in these observed responses.

### Interpretation: confirmed versus user-provided context

- **Confirmed by responses:** the same leading code can occur for the pole and multiple distinct equipment candidates. In particular, `F514139` returns the pole, DBC, DC, and fuse disconnector with four distinct object IDs. A search code is therefore not a unique asset identifier in these results.
- **Confirmed label distinction:** the fuse disconnector under `F514139` is displayed with bracketed code `[F66023]`, not `[F514139]`. Preserve both portions of the label rather than treating them as interchangeable.
- **User-provided domain context:** DC means desconectador cuchilla, DBC means desconectador bajo carga, and DF means desconectador fusible; equipment may be physically mounted on the same pole. The user explains that selecting a candidate opens its own information and positions the map on that equipment.
- **Not independently verified:** physical mounting, formal meaning of the leading versus bracketed codes, selection request contract, equipment coordinates, and actual post-selection map/detail behavior. Search responses alone do not prove these relationships.
- **Practical implication from confirmed evidence:** retain every candidate's object ID and full description. Do not collapse candidates by search code or display text, and do not silently choose the pole when the intended asset may be equipment.

### Additional anonymized multi-candidate response example

Synthetic values below illustrate the observed structure without reproducing real identifiers or codes:

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": [
    {"object_id": 1000001, "surrogate_in_map_id": 1000001, "pin_location": null, "display": "POLE_A (anonymized pole description)"},
    {"object_id": 1000002, "surrogate_in_map_id": 1000002, "pin_location": null, "display": "POLE_A [POLE_A] (DBC anonymized model)"},
    {"object_id": 1000003, "surrogate_in_map_id": 1000003, "pin_location": null, "display": "POLE_A [POLE_A] (DC anonymized model)"},
    {"object_id": 1000004, "surrogate_in_map_id": 1000004, "pin_location": null, "display": "POLE_A [DEVICE_B] (anonymized fuse disconnector)"}
  ]
}
```

# GNAT selection, map positioning, and object views

Date: 2026-10-21. Status: **partial investigation; no implementation**.

## Scope, evidence, and safety

The existing Chrome session was inspected through Chrome DevTools MCP. The original search candidate and information controls were used. Later UI clicks were dispatched to original DOM controls; no GNAT source was edited, backend requests replayed, or operational actions executed. Centrality Local was not inspected or modified.

The browser exploration stopped after 25 Chrome DevTools calls, including one refused request inspection and one failed serialization. Persistence and document readback followed without further browser exploration. Search was repeated only to select FAP1783 and F514139. Previously confirmed search response bodies were not collected again.

**Important limitation:** the request-detail tool was refused because it retrieves complete headers, including cookies, contrary to the user's restriction. No request details were returned by that refused call. No workaround was attempted. Consequently this report distinguishes:

- **Network-list evidence:** method, endpoint, status, and local request ID only.
- **Loaded-client evidence:** function implementations already present in page memory, selected candidate records, widget metadata, rendered text, component stores, and map view state. These are not raw HTTP response bodies.
- **Examples:** anonymized structural illustrations, not captured response bodies.

No credential, cookie, token, internal hostname, or exact geographic location is included here. No effect-unknown widget or operational tool was explicitly executed. Opening the original object view automatically initialized components and generated requests; no server implementation audit establishes their complete absence of incidental effects.

## 1. Selection and positioning

### Confirmed client call chain

The original combobox `select` listener stores the candidate, exposes its information trigger, and invokes the search/positioning trigger. Its branching logic is:

```javascript
// Observed behavior, reduced to relevant statements.
if (selected.get('object_id')) {
  Gis.zoomToObjects(selected.get('surrogate_in_map_id'));
} else {
  const point = selected.get('pin_location').coords[0];
  Gis.pinOnPoint(point.x, point.y, selected.get('display'));
}
```

The fallback branch was inspected, not exercised. `pin_location: null` does not prevent object-backed positioning.

`Gis.zoomToObjects` normalizes a scalar to an array and delegates to a proxy. The map panel exposes `zoomToObjects`, which calls `objectsToFeatures(objectIds, zoomToFeatures, this)`. The proxy's internal binding was not independently traced end-to-end.

`objectsToFeatures` builds this request in the loaded client:

```text
relative endpoint: svc_wmc.php
action: get_objects_geojson
object_ids: JSON-encoded array of map identifiers
```

The selected FAP1783 record contained `object_id=5018289`, `surrogate_in_map_id=5018289`, and `pin_location=null`. A new POST to `/backend/saesa/svc_wmc.php` appeared after selection, local request **3999**, HTTP **200**. Its body and response were not inspected, so associating its action with the client function is supported by timing and loaded code, not a captured body.

### Geometry and map rendering

The loaded implementation:

1. Receives a GeoJSON value in its success callback.
2. Uses `ol.format.GeoJSON().readFeatures(geojson, {featureProjection: mapProjection})`.
3. Uses a map view in **EPSG:3857**; does not explicitly specify `dataProjection` in the inspected call.
4. Assigns point features the `pin` style, other geometries the `default` style.
5. Highlights features and unions their geometry extents.
6. Transforms that extent to EPSG:4326 for `setExtent`.
7. Calls the OpenLayers view's `fit(bounds, {padding: [50,50,50,50]})`.

The exact GeoJSON envelope, input CRS, geometry coordinates, feature properties, empty-result envelope, and error behavior were not verified from network responses. Do not infer an input CRS solely from the map view projection.

After positioning, both inspected pole views and the DBC view had zoom 21. Exact coordinates were observed only as map view state and deliberately omitted from this document.

## 2. Opening the original technical view

The combobox information trigger uses **`object_id`**, not the map surrogate:

```javascript
Web.showObjectView(selected.get('object_id'), callerComponent);
```

The inspected function creates `Web.component.ObjectViewDlg`, containing `Web.component.ObjectViewPanel`. The panel's `loadDefinitions` builds:

```text
endpoint: /backend/saesa/Web/component/svc_object_view.php
action: get_object_definition
object_id: selected object identifier
```

The loaded success callback expects `title`, optionally `message_error`, and `widget_definition`. The latter can contain:

```text
header_widgets
tab_widgets
action_widgets
tool_widgets
```

These are confirmed consumer field names, **not a confirmed full response schema**. The raw response wrapper, absent values, nesting details and permissions fields remain unverified.

### Observed request sequence for FAP1783

| Order | Local request | Method and relative endpoint | Status | Evidence limit |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | 3998 | POST `svc_wmc.php?_dc=...` | 200 | Search necessary to select; known search body not re-inspected |
| 2 | 3999 | POST `svc_wmc.php` | 200 | After candidate selection; detail inspection refused |
| 3 | 4021 | POST `Web/component/svc_object_view.php` | 200 | After original information trigger |
| 4 | 4022 onward | GET component and localization JavaScript | 200 | Includes `AM/component/LocationPartsGrid.js` |
| 5 | 4029–4045, interleaved | POST `svc_web_services.php` and `FH/component/svc.php`, plus component scripts | 200 | Actions/bodies not inspected; do not assume all are generic widget-content requests |
| 6 | 4048 | POST `Web/component/svc_object_view.php` | 200 | Matches endpoint used by content loader; exact action not captured |
| 7 | 4049 | POST `AM/component/svc_location.php?action=list_location_parts&_dc=...` | 200 | Original Partes grid automatically active |

The interleaved sequence is not a claim of strict callback causality. Responses were successful at HTTP level; raw bodies and application-level success were not verified in this run. No fresh network list was collected for F514139 after the limit was reached.

## 3. Widget metadata and content loading

Each registered widget has `definition`, `component`, and `backendId=definition.id`. Observed definition keys:

```text
id, title, exposition, icon_class, color, container_children,
component_class, component_config, component_has_content_generator,
action_has_params, action_provider_id, action_provider_item_name
```

The inspected registry separates `headers`, `tabs`, `actions`, and `tools`. FAP1783's registered `actions` section was empty. This does not establish a system-wide permission or absence of operational behavior in other sections.

### How GNAT chooses generated content

`collectWidgetIdsWithDataGenerator` inspects **tabs, headers, and tools**, selecting definitions with `component_has_content_generator=true`. It does not include the actions section in that collection. `loadWidgetsData` builds:

```text
endpoint: /backend/saesa/Web/component/svc_object_view.php
action: get_widget_content
object_id: object identifier
widget_ids: JSON-encoded array of backend widget IDs
```

`applyWidgetContent` expects an array of items with `widget_id` and `content`. It looks up the registered component by backend widget ID; string content goes to `component.update`, otherwise data goes to `component.loadData` when supported.

**This flag identifies a loading mechanism, not guaranteed read-only behavior.** No explicit permission or universal read-only flag was confirmed. A widget with `component_has_content_generator=false` may still fetch its own data. Do not treat its title, class, lack of generator, or membership in tabs/tools as a safety authorization.

### Automatically loaded consultation data: Partes

The original view opened on **Ubicación de activos → Partes**, widget **291**, class `AM.component.LocationPartsGrid`. No extra manual widget activation was needed or performed. Its generator flag is false. Its store proxy points to `AM/component/svc_location.php?action=list_location_parts`. Its inspected `loadData` calls `store.loadPage(1, options)`.

For FAP1783, two records were present in the component store and rendered in the grid. Observed field set:

```text
id, name, class, class_display, type_display,
managed_by, managed_by_id, installed_assets
```

Confirmed displayed types were `Nodo de Baja Tensión` and `Nodo estructura BT`; both records had `class=elec_insulator`, display class `Estructura`, and installed asset counts 0 and 1 respectively. This is **component-store evidence**, not proof of the raw server envelope or pagination fields. Object scoping parameters used by that grid were not traced completely.

Anonymized illustration of the observed store records:

```json
[
  {"id": 10001, "name": "10001", "class": "elec_insulator", "class_display": "Estructura", "type_display": "Nodo de Baja Tensión", "managed_by": "COMPANY_A", "managed_by_id": 900, "installed_assets": 0},
  {"id": 10002, "name": "10002", "class": "elec_insulator", "class_display": "Estructura", "type_display": "Nodo estructura BT", "managed_by": "COMPANY_A", "managed_by_id": 900, "installed_assets": 1}
]
```

Anonymized illustration of the generated-content consumer contract (not a captured response):

```json
[
  {"widget_id": 2, "content": "<span>POLE_A</span>"}
]
```

Rendered header widgets confirmed: 267 `am.asset_location_breadcrumbs`, 2 `web.object_display_name`, 253 `web.object_type_name`, 82 `am.asset_location_info`, all with a content generator.

## 4. Pole versus equipment: F514139

Both candidates were selected using original candidate DOM elements, and each original information control was opened. No DC or DF view was opened.

| Observation | Pole | DBC equipment |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `object_id` | 4013068 | 28557382 |
| `surrogate_in_map_id` | 4013068 | 28557382 |
| `pin_location` | null | null |
| View title | Detalle de Poste F514139 | Detalle de Interruptor seccionador F514139 [F514139] |
| Type header | POSTES C.A. 10 M 350 KG/RUP CTE. COMUN CON BT | DBC ABB NXA 36KV |
| Class display | Poste | Interruptor seccionador |
| Breadcrumb | F514139 | F514139 → F514139 [F514139] |
| Map projection and zoom | EPSG:3857; 21 | EPSG:3857; 21 |

Their resulting map centers differ: DBC minus pole is approximately **(+3.8114, -7.9328) EPSG:3857 map units**. These are view-center differences, not verified raw equipment coordinates, ground distances, or mounting offsets. The breadcrumb supports a displayed hierarchy; it does not independently prove physical mounting in the field.

The same generic selection and view-loading client functions handle both records, using different IDs. No different per-type request contract was confirmed. The rendered object class, type header, and available widgets **do differ**:

- Pole-specific observed tabs: 155 `Clientes`, 156 `Métricas de cortocircuíto`.
- Equipment-specific observed tabs: 133 `Dispositivos` (`DDM.component.LocationDeviceGrid`), 142 `Red / Datos eléctricos` (`Electrical.component.NetworkingElectricalInfo`); both generator flags false.
- Common observed tabs: 291 Partes, 101 Activos instalados, 102 Unidades constructivas proyectadas, 105 Características generales, 5 IDs, 45 Adjuntos, 107 Condiciones, 138 Averías / Reparaciones, 41 Historial de cambios, 42 Bloqueos vigentes en gestiones.

Thus the UI formally distinguishes asset class/type labels and widget sets. Stable machine-level object class/type identifiers and the backend rule selecting those widgets remain pending. Identifiers happened to match within each candidate's two fields; do not generalize that equality.

## 5. Reproduction plan for Centrality Local — not implemented

1. Preserve the complete selected candidate, including separate `object_id`, `surrogate_in_map_id`, display text and optional pin location. Do not collapse by code or label.
2. For object-backed positioning, supply the **map surrogate** to the `get_objects_geojson` request's JSON array. For a pin-only candidate, follow the separate pin-location branch once its actual format and CRS are verified.
3. Parse the verified geometry response, transform into the local map projection, highlight the chosen geometry, and fit its extent with padding. Do not use `pin_location=null` to disable positioning.
4. Load the view definition using **object_id** and build its dynamic sections. Do not hardcode the pole's tabs for equipment.
5. Keep generated content and self-loading components as separate adapters. The former uses `get_widget_content`; the latter requires its own verified endpoint, scoping, pagination and record contract.
6. Use an explicit, verified consultation-only allowlist. Do not blindly execute action providers, tools, component configs, or arbitrary HTML/scripts from GNAT. A generator flag is not a read permission. A Local renderer should handle returned strings safely rather than copying GNAT's unrestricted HTML update behavior.

Steps 1–4 are supported by inspected client logic and UI observations, but not yet validated against complete network bodies. Exact backend envelopes, error handling and widget-specific scoping are required before treating a Local integration as complete. No Local code or architecture was inspected, so no specific file/module changes are prescribed.

## 6. Pending questions and next bounded investigation

- Obtain a permitted header-free request/response inspection capability, or explicit authorization for the full-header inspector. Do not bypass the current refusal.
- Confirm actual selection request body, GeoJSON response envelope, input CRS and object properties.
- Confirm object-definition and widget-content raw response envelopes and application-level success/error structures.
- Trace `LocationPartsGrid`'s object scoping and paging without executing unknown actions.
- Verify an explicitly chosen consultation widget manually; the present run only observed the automatically active Partes grid and headers.
- Determine a reliable read-only capability/permission signal. No such universal signal was established here.
- Confirm machine-level class/type IDs and compare the equipment's network requests, not only rendered metadata.
- Optionally inspect DC/DF views only under a further authorized bounded scope; do not recollect prior search evidence.

The DBC technical view was left open. No operational control was invoked. Investigation stopped without requesting or exercising broader permissions.

# GNAT electrical geometry and topology

Status: **one MT line verified; electrical connectivity unverified**, 2026-10-21. [Confirmed evidence](gnat-research/EVIDENCIA_CONFIRMADA.md) supersedes earlier unknown-line/unknown-CRS wording for the tested samples only.

## Answer first

Live checks confirm WMTS PNG MT/BT/infrastructure raster layers, ImageWMS elsewhere and a separate Vector selection overlay. A bounded object lookup returned one real MT LineString. Neither raster layers nor that line supply an electrical graph.

| Question | Verified answer | Remaining gap |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Visual MT/BT? | Live WMTS image/png visible | Not area inventory or permission to extract backing vectors |
| Vector segments? | Original click identified32327210; original get_objects_geojson returned two-vertex LineString | No BT vector test, complete line service inventory or multipart coverage |
| CRS? | Tested GeoJSON consumed as EPSG:4326; actual point transform matches EPSG:3857 view | No explicit raw crs declaration or universal source guarantee |
| Segment identity? | id32327210, display F514139 - F514140, type Tramo aéreo MT | Label does not prove endpoint node/pole/terminal association |
| Circuit? | Normal/Real circuit display Cajón [S223], three-phase MT distribution | No formal circuit/terminal graph or state semantics |
| Electrical data? | Widget142 metadata; read-write script loader | Not executed; no safe consultation contract established |
| Route between poles? | No route can be claimed | Explicit edge-terminal relationships and valid states required |

## Geometry is not connectivity

The source line bbox is not identical to vertex extent; preserve both as separate observations. Two geometry vertices are not proven pole coordinates. Feature IDs link the tested object lookup, not terminals. Never snap nearby assets into authoritative connections: crossings, shared codes and breadcrumbs do not establish continuity.

DC/DF technical views now show distinct display classes and a DF intermediate F66023 breadcrumb. This is UI hierarchy evidence only, not physical host or electrical edge semantics. See [model](gnat-research/MODELO_DATOS.md).

## Independent Local representation -> proposal

| Layer | Store separately |
| --- | --- |
| Asset | Source object ID, independent map surrogate, verified machine class/type when available |
| Geometry | Feature ID, geometry, source bbox, derived extent, CRS confidence, timestamp/provenance |
| Association | Displayed hierarchy, hosting assertion, geometric proximity and field confirmation as distinct evidence |
| Topology | Explicit edge/node/terminal IDs, endpoints, phase/voltage, feeder/circuit relation, direction, state and validity time |

Render verified geometry on an independently authorized map. Raster GNAT overlays require separate permission and remain visual aids. User context distinguishes transformer CD of S/E (substation) from pole COD; codes are not global keys.

## Calculation gates

Segment length now has a sample geometry, but implementation still needs correct projected/geodesic measurement; EPSG:3857 meters are not universally ground distances. A geometric route additionally needs explicit segment/pole endpoint associations and continuous geometry. Electrical continuity requires terminal graph, compatible phases/voltage, switch states and temporal provenance. Unknown state is not closed/connected.

## Next bounded step

Obtain a source-owner-approved read contract exposing explicit node/terminal/edge relationships, before implementing topology. Do not execute script-bearing electrical widgets to fill the gap. Area inventory, independent authentication, bulk extraction and source limits remain unverified. No point/tiles sweep or operational action was performed.

Earlier reported line8659985 and F5353/F5352 linkage remain unverified historical claims, not graph evidence. Existing synthetic line fixtures prove no GNAT network property. [Pending work](gnat-research/PENDIENTES.md) keeps standalone-CDP failure, authorization and topology gaps separate.

# Centrality Local

Visor modular Windows + Python estándar + SQLite. **GNAT está desconectado**: la ficha opcional funciona con fixtures **MOCK**, sin consultas corporativas ni cambios automáticos a sus puntos.

## Probar en Windows

1. Extraiga `centrality-local.zip` en una carpeta local.
2. Compruebe Python 3.11 o posterior: `python --version` (o `py -3 --version`). No instale paquetes pip.
3. Abra `INICIAR.bat`. Si no se abre el navegador, visite `http://127.0.0.1:8765`. Alternativa: `python iniciar.py`.
4. Importe `examples/recorrido.csv` y después `examples/aperturas.csv`: ambos tipos deben coexistir. Son datos de demostración, no coordenadas recuperadas del usuario.
5. Filtre por bloque, cambie supervisores y pruebe números/códigos independientes en **Capas**. Las aperturas usan X; sin asignar se muestra rojo, pero no es un supervisor.
6. En **GNAT**, pulse **Buscar fixture MOCK** con `F5353`. Active voluntariamente su capa. Pruebe `SINTETICO_LINEA` o `SINTETICO_MULTILINEA`; **NO** representan el tramo real F5353–F5352.

Para salir: Ctrl+C en el servidor. El paquete empieza sin base ni capturas privadas. Si ya tenía datos, mantenga una copia completa de su carpeta `datos`; con el servidor detenido, puede copiarla al nuevo directorio. No sustituya su única copia. La primera migración crea un respaldo de la BD antes de añadir columnas.

## Datos y compatibilidad

- KMZ/KML: solo puntos, nunca convertir vértices de rutas en activos. Los formatos CSV anteriores siguen disponibles.
- Recorrido v6: `ID;Numero;Subestacion;Longitud;Latitud;SupervisorColor;BloqueID`.
- Aperturas v6: `ID;Numero;Codigo;Longitud;Latitud;SupervisorColor;Bloques` (varios bloques con `|`).
- La exportación conjunta (por defecto o `kind=legacy`) usa `Tipo` para el tipo de activo y `ClasePunto` para recorrido/apertura. Se conserva el alias histórico `Clase` como tipo de activo; no se reutiliza con otro significado.
- `id` SQLite identifica selección/edición; `import_id` conserva el ID CSV; `numero` es visible y admite huecos. No se usa `Numero - 1` para localizar puntos.
- Importar coordenadas reemplaza **solo las clases presentes** (recorrido/apertura). Si ya existen puntos de esas clases, aparece confirmación antes de cambiar nada. KMZ/KML y CSV antiguos son recorrido por defecto.
- CSV sin coordenadas actualiza atributos únicamente con coincidencia única `Numero + Código`; no cambia coordenadas ni bloques existentes. Las coincidencias ambiguas no se eligen automáticamente.
- Coordenadas WGS84: longitud primero al usar GeoJSON. CSV admite punto o coma decimal y exporta `;`, coma decimal y BOM UTF-8 para Excel chileno. No lleva separador de miles.
- Un CSV dañado devuelve fila/columna y no reemplaza ningún punto. **No** se reconstruyen coordenadas corruptas por conjetura. Use el KML/KMZ original o un CSV corregido por su fuente.
- Supervisores: Verde, Naranjo, Azul; vacío/Sin asignar = rojo visual. Bloques de trabajo e identidad no son el color del supervisor.

## Cartografía y privacidad

OSM está **apagado al iniciar**. Activarlo en **Capas** envía solicitudes normales de teselas a OpenStreetMap; no hay precarga masiva ni descarga offline. Sin activarlo puede trabajar con puntos y capturas locales.

**Mosaico** conserva la importación de capturas georreferenciadas JSON/PNG. El snippet existente `bridge/exportar_vista_centrality.js` exporta únicamente la vista abierta, si su cuenta permite conservarla y el navegador lo permite. No se ha vuelto a probar dentro del portal en esta entrega. No eluda restricciones CORS ni de uso.

MT/BT/infraestructura observadas son **raster PNG WMTS**, no geometría vectorial masiva. El proveedor remoto está no disponible; no hay URL WMTS inventada ni cliente de autenticación.

## GNAT: qué puede hacer ahora

Contrato y normalización en `app/integrations/gnat.py`; UI independiente en `web/gnat-client.js`. Búsqueda exacta/ambigua de fixtures, Point del informe y líneas sintéticas, procedencia/hora, metadatos como texto seguro, capa opcional y borrado visible de ficha/capa. **Consultar GNAT** permanece deshabilitado.

No hay caché oficial SQLite, vinculación ni sobrescritura de puntos. El JSON crudo se conserva solo dentro del resultado diagnóstico en memoria; no se devuelve a la UI ni se guarda. Se difiere la importación manual de respuestas oficiales saneadas hasta acordar su formato y permiso de retención.

Para la conexión real, primero hace falta comprobar un servicio externo oficialmente autorizado; si no existe, evaluar un puente dentro del origen autorizado. Localhost/CORS/sesión **no están verificados**. No se copian cookies, tokens, contraseñas ni sesiones. Véase [contrato y diagnóstico](docs/CONTRATO_GNAT.md).

## Diagnóstico manual único (opcional)

`bridge/diagnostico_contrato_gnat.txt` contiene exactamente el mismo snippet que `.js`. Solo observa solicitudes que la interfaz oficial ya realiza; no hace consultas propias. Su panel permite detener/restaurar y exportar TXT/JSON saneado por clic. Siga el procedimiento de 30–60 segundos de [CONTRATO_GNAT.md](docs/CONTRATO_GNAT.md#una-ejecución-manual-de-30–60-segundos).

## Pruebas sin red externa

Desde la raíz:

```powershell
python -B -m unittest discover -s tests -v
node --test (Get-ChildItem tests/*.test.js | ForEach-Object FullName)
node --check web/app.js
node --check web/map.js
node --check web/gnat-client.js
node --check bridge/diagnostico_contrato_gnat.js
```

Node solo es necesario para pruebas de desarrollo, no para usar el visor. La suite usa DB/capturas temporales, HTTP loopback con puerto efímero y stubs de navegador. No abre sesiones corporativas ni descarga teselas. No sustituye una prueba visual manual en Chrome/Windows ni valida una API GNAT viva.

## Límites

Sin GNAT en vivo, exportación masiva, scraper, atlas/PDF A1, credenciales ni dependencias nuevas. El servidor escucha solo en loopback y rechaza escrituras desde otros orígenes. Si 8765 está ocupado, cierre la otra instancia. Respalde `datos` para conservar SQLite y capturas. Detalles: [arquitectura](docs/ARQUITECTURA.md), [cambios](docs/CAMBIOS.md).
````

"""Georeferenced manual viewport capture imports (NO automated scraping)."""
import base64
import json
import math
import re
import uuid
from pathlib import Path
from .config import CAPTURE_DIR

MANIFEST = CAPTURE_DIR / 'indice.json'
MAGIC = b'\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n'
MERCATOR_LIMIT = 20037508.35

def list_captures():
    if not MANIFEST.exists(): return []
    try: return json.loads(MANIFEST.read_text(encoding='utf8'))
    except (ValueError, OSError): return []

def persist(items):
    tmp = MANIFEST.with_suffix('.tmp')
    tmp.write_text(json.dumps(items,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
    tmp.replace(MANIFEST)

def add_capture(payload):
    if payload.get('format')!='centrality-local-capture-v1':
        raise ValueError('No es una captura compatible')
    bounds=payload.get('bounds3857',[])
    if len(bounds)!=4: raise ValueError('Falta extensión geográfica EPSG:3857')
    try: a,b,c,d=map(float,bounds)
    except (TypeError,ValueError): raise ValueError('Extensión inválida')
    if not all(math.isfinite(v) and -MERCATOR_LIMIT-1000<v<MERCATOR_LIMIT+1000 for v in (a,b,c,d)) or a>=c or b>=d:
        raise ValueError('Extensión fuera de rango')
    data=str(payload.get('imageDataUrl',''))
    if not data.startswith('data:image/png;base64,'): raise ValueError('Se requiere imagen PNG')
    try: binary=base64.b64decode(data.partition(',')[2],validate=True)
    except Exception: raise ValueError('PNG base64 inválido')
    if not binary.startswith(MAGIC) or len(binary)>12*1024*1024: raise ValueError('PNG inválido o demasiado grande')
    width,height=int.from_bytes(binary[16:20],'big'),int.from_bytes(binary[20:24],'big')
    if width<100 or height<100 or width>10000 or height>10000: raise ValueError('Dimensiones de imagen inválidas')
    filename=str(uuid.uuid4())+'.png'
    (CAPTURE_DIR / filename).write_bytes(binary)
    entry={'id':filename[:-4], 'archivo':filename, 'bounds3857':[a,b,c,d],
           'width':width,'height':height,'zoom':payload.get('zoom'),
           'fecha':str(payload.get('capturedAt') or '')[:40]}
    existing=list_captures()
    existing.append(entry)
    persist(existing)
    return entry

def delete_capture(cid):
    if not re.fullmatch(r'[a-f0-9\-]{36}',str(cid)):raise ValueError('Identificador inválido')
    items=list_captures()
    survivors=[x for x in items if x['id']!=cid]
    if len(items)==len(survivors):raise ValueError('Captura no encontrada')
    persist(survivors)
    (CAPTURE_DIR / (cid+'.png')).unlink(missing_ok=True)
````

````
"""Compatible local SQLite store. Coordinates are WGS84 latitude/longitude."""
import sqlite3
import json
from contextlib import closing
from contextlib import contextmanager
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from .config import DB_PATH

COLUMNS = ('numero','codigo','tipo','categoria','supervisor','latitud','longitud','descripcion','origen','clase','import_id','bloques')
SELECT_COLUMNS = 'id, ' + ', '.join(COLUMNS)
WRITE_COLUMNS = ('numero','codigo','codigo_busqueda',*COLUMNS[2:],'actualizado')
VALID_TYPES = {'poste','subestacion','sin clasificar','otro'}
VALID_SUPERVISORS = {'', 'Verde', 'Naranjo', 'Azul'}

@contextmanager
def connection():
    db = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    try:
        yield db
        db.commit()
    finally:
        db.close()

def init_db():
    with connection() as db:
        columns = {r[1] for r in db.execute('PRAGMA table_info(puntos)')}
        additions = {'clase': "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'recorrido'",
                     'import_id': "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''", 'bloques': "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'"}
        missing = additions.keys() - columns
        if columns and missing:
            backup = DB_PATH.with_name(DB_PATH.name + '.pre-' + datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S%f') + '.bak')
            with closing(sqlite3.connect(backup)) as dest:
                db.backup(dest)
            for name in sorted(missing):
                db.execute('ALTER TABLE puntos ADD COLUMN ' + name + ' ' + additions[name])
            db.commit()
        db.executescript('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS puntos (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            numero INTEGER NOT NULL,
            codigo TEXT NOT NULL,
            codigo_busqueda TEXT NOT NULL,
            tipo TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'sin clasificar',
            categoria TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
            supervisor TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
            latitud REAL NOT NULL CHECK(latitud BETWEEN -90 AND 90),
            longitud REAL NOT NULL CHECK(longitud BETWEEN -180 AND 180),
            descripcion TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
            origen TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
            actualizado TEXT NOT NULL,
            clase TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'recorrido',
            import_id TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
            bloques TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'
        );
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_codigo ON puntos(codigo_busqueda);
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_numero ON puntos(numero);
        ''')

def now_iso():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec='seconds')

def clean_code(code):
    return str(code or '').strip().upper()

def clean_record(record):
    c = str(record.get('codigo','')).strip()
    if not c:
        raise ValueError('Hay un punto sin código')
    lat = float(record['latitud']); lon = float(record['longitud'])
    if not (-90 <= lat <= 90 and -180 <= lon <= 180):
        raise ValueError('Coordenadas fuera de rango')
    tipo = str(record.get('tipo') or 'sin clasificar').strip().lower()
    if tipo not in VALID_TYPES: tipo = 'otro'
    supervisor = str(record.get('supervisor') or '').strip().capitalize()
    if supervisor not in VALID_SUPERVISORS: supervisor = ''
    kind = record.get('clase') or 'recorrido'
    if kind not in ('recorrido','apertura'): raise ValueError('Clase no válida')
    blocks = record.get('bloques') or []
    if not isinstance(blocks, list): raise ValueError('Bloques debe ser una lista')
    return (int(record.get('numero') or 0), c, clean_code(c), tipo,
            str(record.get('categoria') or '')[:180], supervisor, lat, lon,
            str(record.get('descripcion') or '')[:1000], str(record.get('origen') or '')[:180], kind, str(record.get('import_id') or '')[:180],
            json.dumps(list(dict.fromkeys(str(b).strip() for b in blocks if str(b).strip())),ensure_ascii=False), now_iso())

def decode_point(row):
    point = dict(row)
    point['bloques'] = json.loads(point['bloques'])
    return point


def list_points():
    with connection() as db:
        return [decode_point(r) for r in db.execute('SELECT '+SELECT_COLUMNS+' FROM puntos ORDER BY numero, id')]


def replace_points(records):
    normalized = [clean_record(r) for r in records]
    if not normalized: raise ValueError('El archivo no contiene puntos válidos')
    kinds = {r[10] for r in normalized}
    with connection() as db:
        db.executemany('DELETE FROM puntos WHERE clase=?', [(kind,) for kind in kinds])
        db.executemany('INSERT INTO puntos ('+','.join(WRITE_COLUMNS)+') VALUES ('+','.join('?' for _ in WRITE_COLUMNS)+')', normalized)
    return len(normalized)


def update_point(id_, changes):
    allowed = {'codigo', 'tipo', 'categoria', 'supervisor', 'descripcion'}
    edits = {key: val for key, val in changes.items() if key in allowed}
    if not edits: raise ValueError('Sin campos editables')
    if 'codigo' in edits:
        edits['codigo'] = str(edits['codigo']).strip()
        if not edits['codigo']: raise ValueError('Código vacío')
        edits['codigo_busqueda'] = clean_code(edits['codigo'])
    if 'tipo' in edits:
        if edits['tipo'] not in VALID_TYPES: raise ValueError('Tipo no válido')
    if 'supervisor' in edits:
        if edits['supervisor'] not in VALID_SUPERVISORS: raise ValueError('Supervisor no válido')
    for key in ('categoria', 'descripcion'):
        if key in edits: edits[key] = str(edits[key])[:(180 if key=='categoria' else 1000)]
    edits['actualizado'] = now_iso()
    with connection() as db:
        cur = db.execute('UPDATE puntos SET ' + ','.join(k+'=?' for k in edits) + ' WHERE id=?', list(edits.values())+[int(id_)])
        if not cur.rowcount: raise ValueError('Punto inexistente')
        r = db.execute('SELECT '+SELECT_COLUMNS+' FROM puntos WHERE id=?', (int(id_),)).fetchone()
        return decode_point(r)

def bulk_supervisor(ids, supervisor):
    if supervisor not in VALID_SUPERVISORS: raise ValueError('Supervisor no válido')
    ids = [int(x) for x in ids]
    if not ids or len(ids)>10000: raise ValueError('Seleccione puntos válidos')
    with connection() as db:
        db.executemany('UPDATE puntos SET supervisor=?, actualizado=? WHERE id=?', [(supervisor, now_iso(), i) for i in ids])
    return len(ids)

def merge_attributes(rows):
    """Update existing points by BOTH numero and exact code; keep omitted fields intact."""
    updates = 0; misses = []
    allowed = {'tipo','categoria','descripcion','supervisor'}
    with connection() as db:
        for r in rows:
            number = int(r.get('numero') or 0)
            code = clean_code(r.get('codigo'))
            matches = db.execute('SELECT id FROM puntos WHERE numero=? AND codigo_busqueda=?', (number, code)).fetchall()
            if len(matches)!=1:
                misses.append({'numero':number,'codigo':code,'coincidencias':len(matches)})
                continue
            fields = {k:r[k] for k in allowed if k in r}
            if 'tipo' in fields:
                fields['tipo']=str(fields['tipo'] or 'sin clasificar').lower()
                if fields['tipo'] not in VALID_TYPES:fields['tipo']='otro'
            if 'supervisor' in fields:
                fields['supervisor']=str(fields['supervisor'] or '').capitalize()
                if fields['supervisor'] not in VALID_SUPERVISORS:fields['supervisor']=''
            if not fields:continue
            fields['actualizado']=now_iso()
            db.execute('UPDATE puntos SET '+','.join(k+'=?' for k in fields)+' WHERE id=?', [*fields.values(),matches[0]['id']])
            updates += 1
    return {'actualizados':updates,'sin_coincidencia':misses}

def search_exact(codes, types):
    codes=[clean_code(c) for c in codes if clean_code(c)]
    types=set(types).intersection(VALID_TYPES)
    with connection() as db:
        outcomes=[]
        for code in codes[:500]:
            matches=[dict(r) for r in db.execute('SELECT id,numero,codigo,tipo,categoria,supervisor,latitud,longitud FROM puntos WHERE codigo_busqueda=? ORDER BY numero,id',(code,))]
            selected=[r for r in matches if r['tipo'] in types] if types else matches
            reason = 'encontrado' if len(selected)==1 else ('ambiguo' if len(selected)>1 else 'no encontrado')
            outcomes.append({'codigo':code,'estado':reason,'coincidencias':selected,'total_codigo':len(matches)})
        return outcomes
````

````
"""CSV/KMZ/KML readers independent from HTTP/database layers."""
import csv
import io
import math
import re
import zipfile
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from html import unescape

COLUMNS = {'numero':['numero','n','n°','nº','id_punto'],
           'codigo':['codigo','codigo_poste','codigoposte','code','poste','nombre','subestacion'],
           'tipo':['tipo','clase','tipo_activo'],
           'categoria':['categoria','category'],
           'supervisor':['supervisor','bloque','grupo','supervisorcolor'],
           'latitud':['latitud','latitude','lat'],
           'longitud':['longitud','longitude','lon','lng'],
           'descripcion':['descripcion','description','detalle'],
           'import_id':['id'], 'clase':['clasepunto','clase_punto'], 'bloques':['bloques','bloqueid']}

def plain_text(s):
    return unescape(re.sub(r'<[^>]*>', ' ', s or '')).strip()

def infer_type(desc):
    s = (desc or '').lower()
    if 'subestaci' in s or re.search(r'\bsed\b',s): return 'subestacion'
    if 'poste' in s: return 'poste'
    return 'sin clasificar'

def strip_number(name):
    s=str(name or '').strip()
    return re.sub(r'^\d+\s*(?:[-–—·.:#/]\s*|\s+)', '', s).strip() or s

def from_kml(data, source_name):
    try:
        root = ET.fromstring(data)
    except ET.ParseError as e:
        raise ValueError('KML inválido: ' + str(e))
    result = []
    for placemark in root.iter():
        if placemark.tag.rsplit('}',1)[-1] != 'Placemark': continue
        name=''; desc=''; coords=[]
        for e in placemark.iter():
            tag=e.tag.rsplit('}',1)[-1]
            if e is placemark: continue
            if tag=='name' and not name: name=e.text or ''
            if tag=='description' and not desc: desc=plain_text(e.text or '')
        # Only Point coordinates; don't turn route vertices into assets.
        for e in placemark.iter():
            if e.tag.rsplit('}',1)[-1] != 'Point': continue
            for child in e.iter():
                if child.tag.rsplit('}',1)[-1]=='coordinates' and child.text:
                    for token in child.text.split():
                        a=token.split(',')
                        row=len(result)+1
                        if len(a)<2: raise ValueError(f'Fila {row}, columna Longitud/Latitud: coordenadas KML incompletas')
                        lon=coordinate(a[0],180,row,'Longitud')
                        lat=coordinate(a[1],90,row,'Latitud')
                        coords.append((lat,lon))
        for lat,lon in coords:
            result.append({'numero':len(result)+1, 'codigo':strip_number(name) or ('Punto '+str(len(result)+1)),
                           'tipo':infer_type(desc+' '+name),'categoria':'','supervisor':'',
                           'latitud':lat,'longitud':lon,'descripcion':desc,'origen':source_name})
    return result

def parse_kmz(blob, name):
    try:
        with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(blob)) as z:
            candidate=[m for m in z.infolist() if m.filename.lower().endswith('.kml') and not m.is_dir()]
            if not candidate: raise ValueError('El KMZ no tiene archivos KML')
            candidate.sort(key=lambda i:(not i.filename.lower().endswith('doc.kml'),i.filename))
            if candidate[0].file_size>30_000_000: raise ValueError('KML interno demasiado grande')
            return from_kml(z.read(candidate[0]),name)
    except (zipfile.BadZipFile, RuntimeError, NotImplementedError, EOFError) as exc:
        raise ValueError('KMZ inválido o no compatible') from exc


def norm_head(s):
    import unicodedata
    s=unicodedata.normalize('NFKD',str(s)).encode('ascii','ignore').decode().lower().strip()
    return re.sub(r'[^a-z0-9]+','_',s).strip('_')

def coordinate(value, limit, row, column):
    text = str(value).strip()
    if not re.fullmatch(r'[+-]?(?:[0-9]+(?:[.,][0-9]*)?|[.,][0-9]+)(?:[eE][+-]?[0-9]+)?', text):
        raise ValueError(f'Fila {row}, columna {column}: coordenada inválida')
    number = float(text.replace(',', '.'))
    if not math.isfinite(number) or abs(number) > limit:
        raise ValueError(f'Fila {row}, columna {column}: coordenada fuera de rango')
    return number


def from_csv(blob, name):
    try: raw=blob.decode('utf-8-sig')
    except UnicodeDecodeError: raw=blob.decode('cp1252')
    try: dialect=csv.Sniffer().sniff(raw[:8192],delimiters=';,\t')
    except csv.Error: dialect=csv.excel; dialect.delimiter=';'
    reader=csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(raw), dialect=dialect, strict=True)
    if not reader.fieldnames: raise ValueError('CSV vacío')
    aliases = {key: {norm_head(a) for a in group} for key,group in COLUMNS.items()}
    field_map={}
    for col in reader.fieldnames:
        for canonical, choices in aliases.items():
            if norm_head(col) in choices:
                if canonical in field_map.values(): raise ValueError('Columna repetida: '+col)
                field_map[col]=canonical
                break
    mapped=set(field_map.values())
    if 'codigo' not in mapped: raise ValueError('Falta columna de código en CSV')
    if ('latitud' in mapped) != ('longitud' in mapped):
        raise ValueError('El CSV debe incluir ambas columnas Latitud y Longitud')
    headers = {norm_head(h) for h in reader.fieldnames}
    explicit = 'supervisorcolor' in headers or 'subestacion' in headers
    kind = 'apertura' if 'bloques' in headers and 'subestacion' not in headers else 'recorrido'
    hascoords = 'latitud' in mapped
    records=[]; ids=set(); numbers=set()
    try:
        for r in reader:
            row=reader.line_num
            if not any(v for v in r.values()): continue
            if None in r or any(v is None for v in r.values()):
                raise ValueError(f'Fila {row}: columnas incompletas o adicionales')
            n={field_map[k]:v.strip() for k,v in r.items() if k in field_map}
            if not n.get('codigo'): raise ValueError(f'Fila {row}, columna Código: valor vacío')
            number=n.get('numero') or str(len(records)+1)
            if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9]+',number) or not 0<int(number)<=9007199254740991:
                raise ValueError(f'Fila {row}, columna Numero: entero positivo requerido')
            n['numero']=int(number)
            n['clase']=n.get('clase') or kind
            if n['clase'] not in ('recorrido','apertura'): raise ValueError(f'Fila {row}: Clase inválida')
            identity=n.get('import_id','')
            if identity and (not re.fullmatch(r'[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,179}',identity) or (n['clase'],identity) in ids):
                raise ValueError(f'Fila {row}, columna ID: inválido o duplicado')
            if (n['clase'],n['numero']) in numbers: raise ValueError(f'Fila {row}, columna Numero: duplicado')
            ids.add((n['clase'],identity)); numbers.add((n['clase'],n['numero']))
            if not explicit: n['codigo']=strip_number(n['codigo'])
            supervisor=n.get('supervisor','')
            if supervisor == 'Sin asignar': supervisor=''
            supervisor=supervisor.capitalize()
            if supervisor not in ('','Verde','Naranjo','Azul'):
                raise ValueError(f'Fila {row}, columna SupervisorColor: color no válido')
            if 'supervisor' in n: n['supervisor']=supervisor
            blocks=n.get('bloques','').split('|')
            n['bloques']=list(dict.fromkeys(b.strip() for b in blocks if b.strip()))
            if 'bloqueid' in headers and len(n['bloques'])>1:
                raise ValueError(f'Fila {row}, columna BloqueID: admite un solo bloque')
            if hascoords:
                for column,limit in (('longitud',180),('latitud',90)):
                    n[column]=coordinate(n.get(column,''),limit,row,column.capitalize())
            n['origen']=name
            records.append(n)
    except csv.Error as exc:
        raise ValueError(f'Fila {reader.line_num}: CSV inválido ({exc})') from exc
    if not records: raise ValueError('No hay filas en CSV')
    return records,hascoords


def from_upload(filename, data):
    ext=filename.lower().rsplit('.',1)[-1]
    if ext=='kmz': return parse_kmz(data,filename),True
    if ext=='kml': return from_kml(data,filename),True
    if ext=='csv': return from_csv(data,filename)
    raise ValueError('Solo se admite KMZ, KML o CSV')
````

````
"""Offline GNAT contract. No HTTP transport or authentication is implemented."""
import copy
import json
import math
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from html.parser import HTMLParser


def timestamp():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec='seconds')


def failure(code, message):
    return {'ok': False, 'data': None, 'error': {'code': code, 'message': message}, 'consulted_at': timestamp()}


def success(data):
    return {'ok': True, 'data': data, 'error': None, 'consulted_at': timestamp()}


class TextOnly(HTMLParser):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(convert_charrefs=True)
        self.parts = []
        self.hidden = 0

    def handle_starttag(self, tag, attrs):
        if tag in ('script', 'style'): self.hidden += 1

    def handle_endtag(self, tag):
        if tag in ('script', 'style'): self.hidden = max(0, self.hidden - 1)
        if tag in ('div', 'p', 'br', 'li', 'td'): self.parts.append(' ')

    def handle_data(self, data):
        if not self.hidden: self.parts.append(data)


def metadata_text(value):
    """Extract text, not reusable HTML. UI must still use textContent."""
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return '\n'.join(metadata_text(item.get('content', '')) for item in value[:30] if isinstance(item, dict))[:8000]
    if not isinstance(value, str): return ''
    parser = TextOnly()
    parser.feed(value[:64000])
    return ' '.join(''.join(parser.parts).split())[:8000]


def validate_geometry(geometry):
    if not isinstance(geometry, dict): raise ValueError('Falta geometría GeoJSON')
    kind = geometry.get('type')
    coordinates = geometry.get('coordinates')

    def position(p):
        if not isinstance(p, list) or len(p) not in (2, 3): return False
        if not all(type(v) in (int, float) and math.isfinite(v) for v in p): return False
        return -180 <= p[0] <= 180 and -90 <= p[1] <= 90

    def line(values):
        return isinstance(values, list) and 2 <= len(values) <= 10000 and all(position(p) for p in values)

    valid = (kind == 'Point' and position(coordinates) or
             kind == 'LineString' and line(coordinates) or
             kind == 'MultiLineString' and isinstance(coordinates, list) and
             1 <= len(coordinates) <= 100 and all(line(values) for values in coordinates))
    if not valid: raise ValueError('GeoJSON inválido: se requieren coordenadas WGS84 [longitud, latitud]')
    return {'type': kind, 'coordinates': copy.deepcopy(coordinates)}


def normalize_response(body, *, http_status=200, transport_error=None, source='GNAT no validado'):
    """Validate supplied diagnostic responses; this function sends no requests."""
    if transport_error is not None:
        code = 'timeout' if isinstance(transport_error, TimeoutError) else 'connection_error'
        return failure(code, 'La consulta no pudo completarse; los puntos locales no cambiaron')
    if http_status in (401, 403): return failure('not_authorized', 'Acceso no autorizado (401/403)')
    if http_status != 200: return failure('http_error', 'Respuesta HTTP no válida')
    if body is None or body == '' or body == b'': return failure('empty_response', 'Respuesta vacía')
    if isinstance(body, (str, bytes)):
        if len(body) > 256000: return failure('invalid_response', 'Respuesta demasiado grande')
        try: body = json.loads(body)
        except (ValueError, UnicodeDecodeError): return failure('invalid_json', 'JSON inválido')
    if not isinstance(body, dict) or not isinstance(body.get('success'), bool):
        return failure('invalid_response', 'Falta el indicador success')
    if body['success'] is False: return failure('service_error', 'El servicio informó success:false')
    feature = body.get('data')
    if not isinstance(feature, dict) or feature.get('type') != 'Feature' or feature.get('id') is None:
        return failure('invalid_response', 'Falta un Feature con ID')
    identity = feature['id']
    if type(identity) not in (int, str) or not str(identity).strip() or len(str(identity)) > 180:
        return failure('invalid_response', 'ID no válido')
    properties = feature.get('properties')
    if not isinstance(properties, dict) or not isinstance(properties.get('display_name'), str):
        return failure('invalid_response', 'Falta properties.display_name')
    try: geometry = validate_geometry(feature.get('geometry'))
    except ValueError as exc: return failure('invalid_geometry', str(exc))
    return success({'id': str(identity).strip(), 'name': properties['display_name'][:180],
                    'code': properties['display_name'].strip().upper()[:180],
                    'asset_type': str(properties.get('asset_type', 'sin clasificar'))[:80],
                    'geometry': geometry, 'metadata': metadata_text(properties.get('description', '')),
                    'source': source, 'consulted_at': timestamp(),
                    'synthetic': bool(properties.get('synthetic', False)),
                    'raw_response': copy.deepcopy(body)})


def public_result(value):
    """Never dump raw diagnostic JSON/HTML to the browser or a file."""
    if isinstance(value, dict): return {k: public_result(v) for k, v in value.items() if k != 'raw_response'}
    if isinstance(value, list): return [public_result(item) for item in value]
    return value


class DisconnectedAdapter:
    def get_connection_status(self):
        return {'state': 'disconnected', 'mode': 'disconnected', 'validated': False,
                'source': 'Configuración local', 'message': 'GNAT no disponible: contrato y autorización pendientes'}

    def search_assets(self, query, asset_types=None, pagination=None):
        return failure('disconnected', 'No hay una conexión GNAT validada')

    def get_asset_geometry(self, object_id):
        return failure('disconnected', 'No hay una conexión GNAT validada')

    def get_asset_metadata(self, object_id):
        return failure('disconnected', 'No hay una conexión GNAT validada')

    def get_asset_by_id(self, object_id):
        return failure('disconnected', 'No hay una conexión GNAT validada')

    def list_available_layers(self):
        return success([])


class FixtureAdapter(DisconnectedAdapter):
    """Explicit opt-in MOCK; known point plus synthetic lines, not a live service."""
    def __init__(self):
        examples = [
            ('8659987', 'F5353', 'poste', {'type': 'Point', 'coordinates': [-72.06376236, -37.780086409]}, False),
            ('SYN-LINE', 'SINTETICO_LINEA', 'tramo', {'type': 'LineString', 'coordinates': [[-72.064, -37.780], [-72.063, -37.781]]}, True),
            ('SYN-MULTI', 'SINTETICO_MULTILINEA', 'tramo', {'type': 'MultiLineString', 'coordinates': [[[-72.064, -37.780], [-72.063, -37.781]], [[-72.062, -37.779], [-72.061, -37.780]]]}, True)
        ]
        self.assets = []
        for identity, name, kind, geometry, synthetic in examples:
            raw = {'success': True, 'data': {'id': identity, 'type': 'Feature', 'geometry': geometry,
                   'properties': {'display_name': name, 'asset_type': kind, 'synthetic': synthetic,
                                  'description': '<b>MOCK</b>: geometría sintética' if synthetic else '<b>MOCK</b>: punto del informe aportado; sin consulta actual'}}}
            self.assets.append(normalize_response(raw, source='MOCK · fixture local; no es una consulta GNAT')['data'])

    def get_connection_status(self):
        return {**super().get_connection_status(), 'mode': 'MOCK', 'message': 'Solo fixtures MOCK; GNAT sigue desconectado'}

    def search_assets(self, query, asset_types=None, pagination=None):
        if not isinstance(query, str) or len(query) > 180: return failure('invalid_query', 'Código de hasta 180 caracteres requerido')
        if asset_types is not None and (not isinstance(asset_types, list) or any(not isinstance(t, str) for t in asset_types)):
            return failure('invalid_query', 'Tipos de activo no válidos')
        # No speculative server pagination: the local fixture set is deliberately finite.
        if pagination: return failure('unavailable', 'Paginación no validada')
        code = query.strip().upper()
        matches = [copy.deepcopy(a) for a in self.assets if a['code'] == code and (not asset_types or a['asset_type'] in asset_types)]
        return success({'state': 'found' if len(matches) == 1 else 'ambiguous' if matches else 'not_found', 'matches': matches})

    def get_asset_by_id(self, object_id):
        matches = [a for a in self.assets if a['id'] == str(object_id).strip()]
        return success(copy.deepcopy(matches[0])) if len(matches) == 1 else failure('not_found', 'ID ausente del conjunto MOCK')

    def get_asset_geometry(self, object_id):
        result = self.get_asset_by_id(object_id)
        return success(result['data']['geometry']) if result['ok'] else result

    def get_asset_metadata(self, object_id):
        result = self.get_asset_by_id(object_id)
        if result['ok']: result['data'].pop('geometry', None)
        return result

- Package locator: `snapshot/app/server.py`

"""Small local-only HTTP API, Python standard library, no remote service dependency."""
import csv
import io
import json
import mimetypes
import urllib.parse
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from . import db, importers, captures
from .integrations import gnat
from .config import WEB_DIR, CAPTURE_DIR, MAX_UPLOAD, MAX_CAPTURE_UPLOAD

PORT=8765

class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    protocol_version='HTTP/1.1'

    def log_message(self, fmt, *args):
        print('[local]',fmt % args)

    def reply(self, status, data, content_type='application/json; charset=utf-8', attachment=None):
        if isinstance(data,(dict,list)):
            data=json.dumps(data,ensure_ascii=False).encode('utf8')
        elif isinstance(data,str): data=data.encode('utf8')
        if status == 403: self.close_connection = True
        self.send_response(status)
        self.send_header('Content-Type',content_type)
        self.send_header('Content-Length',str(len(data)))
        self.send_header('Cache-Control','no-store')
        self.send_header('X-Content-Type-Options','nosniff')
        self.send_header('X-Frame-Options','DENY')
        self.send_header('Content-Security-Policy',"default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; img-src 'self' data: https://tile.openstreetmap.org; connect-src 'self'; base-uri 'none'; object-src 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'")
        if attachment:
            self.send_header('Content-Disposition',f'attachment; filename="{attachment}"')
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(data)

    def json_body(self,limit=MAX_UPLOAD):
        raw=self.read_body(limit)
        try: return json.loads(raw)
        except (ValueError,UnicodeDecodeError):raise ValueError('JSON inválido')

    def read_body(self,limit=MAX_UPLOAD):
        try: length=int(self.headers.get('Content-Length',-1))
        except ValueError: raise ValueError('Tamaño inválido')
        if length<0 or length>limit:raise ValueError('Archivo/cuerpo demasiado grande')
        return self.rfile.read(length)

    def check_local_host(self):
        hostname=self.headers.get('Host','').lower()
        return hostname in {f'127.0.0.1:{PORT}',f'localhost:{PORT}'}

    def check_write_origin(self):
        origin=self.headers.get('Origin')
        if origin not in (None,f'http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}',f'http://localhost:{PORT}'):
            return False
        # Browsers can't normally send cross-site JSON, but Origin validation
        # is required too, to avoid cross-site localhost writes.
        return True

    def do_GET(self):
        if not self.check_local_host():return self.reply(403,{'error':'Host no permitido'})
        path=urllib.parse.urlparse(self.path).path
        try:
            if path=='/api/gnat/status':return self.reply(200,gnat.DisconnectedAdapter().get_connection_status())
            if path=='/api/health':return self.reply(200,{'ok':True,'version':'5.0-piloto','electrico_remoto':'no conectado'})
            if path=='/api/points':return self.reply(200,{'items':db.list_points()})
            if path=='/api/captures':return self.reply(200,{'items':captures.list_captures()})
            if path=='/api/export.csv':return self.export_csv()
            if path.startswith('/capturas/'):
                filename=path.removeprefix('/capturas/')
                if not filename.endswith('.png') or filename.count('/') or not filename.replace('-','').replace('.png','').isalnum():
                    return self.reply(404,{'error':'Archivo no encontrado'})
                target=CAPTURE_DIR / filename
                if not target.exists():return self.reply(404,{'error':'No encontrado'})
                return self.reply(200,target.read_bytes(),'image/png')
            target=WEB_DIR / (path.lstrip('/') or 'index.html')
            if target.resolve().is_relative_to(WEB_DIR.resolve()) and target.is_file():
                typ=mimetypes.guess_type(str(target))[0] or 'application/octet-stream'
                return self.reply(200,target.read_bytes(),typ + ('; charset=utf-8' if typ.startswith(('text/','application/javascript')) else ''))
            return self.reply(404,{'error':'No encontrado'})
        except Exception as exc:
            return self.reply(500,{'error':str(exc)})

    def do_POST(self):
        if not self.check_local_host() or not self.check_write_origin():
            return self.reply(403,{'error':'Origen no permitido'})
        path=urllib.parse.urlparse(self.path).path
        try:
            if path=='/api/gnat/mock':
                data=self.json_body(5000)
                if not isinstance(data,dict): raise ValueError('Se requiere un objeto JSON')
                adapter=gnat.FixtureAdapter()
                if 'object_id' in data: result=adapter.get_asset_by_id(data['object_id'])
                else: result=adapter.search_assets(data.get('query',''),data.get('asset_types'))
                return self.reply(200,gnat.public_result(result))
            if path=='/api/import':
                qs=urllib.parse.parse_qs(urllib.parse.urlparse(self.path).query)
                name=qs.get('filename',['archivo'])[0]
                if '/' in name or '\\' in name or len(name)>150:raise ValueError('Nombre de archivo inválido')
                records,with_coords=importers.from_upload(name,self.read_body())
                if with_coords:
                    kinds={r.get('clase','recorrido') for r in records}
                    existing=[p for p in db.list_points() if p['clase'] in kinds]
                    if existing and qs.get('confirm_replace') != ['1']:
                        return self.reply(409,{'error':f'La importación reemplazará {len(existing)} puntos del tipo importado. Exporte un respaldo antes de confirmar.',
                                               'requires_confirmation':True,'clases':sorted(kinds),'reemplaza':len(existing)})
                    quantity=db.replace_points(records)
                    return self.reply(200,{'modo':'reemplazo','puntos':quantity,'mensaje':'Importación completa. Reemplazó los puntos anteriores.'})
                report=db.merge_attributes(records)
                return self.reply(200,{'modo':'actualizacion','puntos':report['actualizados'],'sin_coincidencia':report['sin_coincidencia']})
            if path=='/api/batch':
                data=self.json_body(200_000)
                codes=data.get('codes',[])
                if not isinstance(codes,list) or len(codes)>500:raise ValueError('Máximo 500 códigos por consulta')
                return self.reply(200,{'items':db.search_exact(codes,data.get('types',[]))})
            if path=='/api/supervisores':
                data=self.json_body(100_000)
                return self.reply(200,{'actualizados':db.bulk_supervisor(data.get('ids',[]),data.get('supervisor',''))})
            if path=='/api/captures/import':
                payload=self.json_body(MAX_CAPTURE_UPLOAD)
                return self.reply(200,{'capture':captures.add_capture(payload)})
            return self.reply(404,{'error':'Ruta no encontrada'})
        except (ValueError,KeyError,OverflowError) as exc:
            return self.reply(400,{'error':str(exc)})
        except Exception as exc:
            return self.reply(500,{'error':'Error interno: '+str(exc)})

    def do_PATCH(self):
        if not self.check_local_host() or not self.check_write_origin():
            return self.reply(403,{'error':'Origen no permitido'})
        path=urllib.parse.urlparse(self.path).path
        try:
            if path.startswith('/api/points/'):
                num=int(path.rsplit('/',1)[-1])
                p=db.update_point(num,self.json_body(20_000))
                return self.reply(200,{'point':p})
            return self.reply(404,{'error':'Ruta no encontrada'})
        except (ValueError,KeyError,OverflowError) as exc:
            return self.reply(400,{'error':str(exc)})

    def do_DELETE(self):
        if not self.check_local_host() or not self.check_write_origin():
            return self.reply(403,{'error':'Origen no permitido'})
        path=urllib.parse.urlparse(self.path).path
        try:
            if path.startswith('/api/captures/'):
                captures.delete_capture(path.rsplit('/',1)[-1]);return self.reply(200,{'ok':True})
            return self.reply(404,{'error':'Ruta no encontrada'})
        except ValueError as exc:
            return self.reply(400,{'error':str(exc)})

    def export_csv(self):
        kind=urllib.parse.parse_qs(urllib.parse.urlparse(self.path).query).get('kind',['legacy'])[0]
        if kind not in ('route','opening','legacy'): return self.reply(400,{'error':'Tipo de exportación no válido'})
        out=io.StringIO()
        writer=csv.writer(out,delimiter=';',lineterminator='\r\n')
        if kind=='route': headers=['ID','Numero','Subestacion','Longitud','Latitud','SupervisorColor','BloqueID']
        elif kind=='opening': headers=['ID','Numero','Codigo','Longitud','Latitud','SupervisorColor','Bloques']
        else: headers=['Numero','CodigoPoste','Tipo','Categoria','Bloque','Latitud','Longitud','Descripcion','ID','ClasePunto','Bloques']
        writer.writerow(headers)
        def safe(value):
            text=str(value)
            return "'"+text if text.startswith(('=','+','-','@','\t','\r')) else text
        for p in db.list_points():
            if kind!='legacy' and p['clase']!=('recorrido' if kind=='route' else 'apertura'): continue
            lon=str(p['longitud']).replace('.',','); lat=str(p['latitud']).replace('.',',')
            identity=safe(p['import_id'] or 'local-'+str(p['id']))
            blocks=safe('|'.join(p['bloques']))
            if kind=='legacy':
                row=[p['numero'],safe(p['codigo']),p['tipo'],safe(p['categoria']),p['supervisor'],lat,lon,safe(p['descripcion']),identity,p['clase'],blocks]
            else:
                row=[identity,p['numero'],safe(p['codigo']),lon,lat,p['supervisor'] or 'Sin asignar',blocks]
            writer.writerow(row)
        return self.reply(200,'\ufeff'+out.getvalue(),'text/csv; charset=utf-8','centrality_'+kind+'.csv')

def serve():
    db.init_db()
    srv=ThreadingHTTPServer(('127.0.0.1',PORT),Handler)
    print('\nCENTRALITY LOCAL')
    print(f'Abre http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}')
    print('Datos locales: datos/puntos.sqlite3')
    print('Detener: Ctrl+C\n')
    try:srv.serve_forever()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:pass
    finally:srv.server_close()
````

  function button(label,handler){const b=document.createElement('button');b.textContent=label;b.style.margin='4px';b.addEventListener('click',handler);panel.appendChild(b);}
  function report(){return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify({format:'centrality-gnat-safe-diagnostic-v1',cors_localhost:'NO_VERIFICADO',active,samples}));}

        else try{value=JSON.parse(text);}catch{responseState=text?'invalid_json':'empty';}
      }else if(text&&typeof text==='object')value=text;
      else responseState='unavailable';
    }
    const sample={sequence:samples.length+1,...meta,status:Number.isInteger(status)?status:0,response_state:responseState,
      response_shape:value?shape(value):null,geometry_type:value?geometryType(value):'no_observado'};
    if(JSON.stringify(sample).length>6000||JSON.stringify(samples).length+JSON.stringify(sample).length>60000){stop();return;}
    samples.push(sample);counts.set(key,(counts.get(key)||0)+1);
    state.textContent='Activo · '+samples.length+' muestras. Ningún valor de negocio ni credencial se exporta.';
    if(samples.length>=24)stop();
  }
  async function readBounded(response){
    const clone=response.clone();
    if(!clone.body?.getReader)return {text:null,state:'unavailable'};
    const reader=clone.body.getReader();readers.add(reader);const chunks=[];let length=0;
    try{
      while(true){
        const chunk=await reader.read();if(chunk.done)break;
        length+=chunk.value.byteLength;
        if(length>MAX_RESPONSE){void reader.cancel().catch(()=>{});return {text:null,state:'oversize'};}
        chunks.push(chunk.value);

      const bytes=new Uint8Array(length);let offset=0;for(const chunk of chunks){bytes.set(chunk,offset);offset+=chunk.byteLength;}
      return {text:new TextDecoder().decode(bytes),state:'observed'};

    finally{readers.delete(reader);}
  }

    const meta=active?metadata(args[0],args[1]?.body):null;
    const promise=Reflect.apply(originalFetch,this,args);
    if(meta&&pending<4&&samples.length<24&&(counts.get(meta.service+':'+meta.action)||0)<3){

  if(proto){proto.open=openObserver;proto.send=sendObserver;}
  window.centralityGNATDiagnostic={stop,report};

  const geometryTypes=new Set(['Point','LineString','MultiLineString','Polygon','MultiPolygon','MultiPoint','GeometryCollection']);

  Object.assign(panel.style,{position:'fixed',bottom:'12px',left:'12px',zIndex:'2147483647',background:'#fff',color:'#142439',padding:'14px',border:'1px solid #607184',maxWidth:'340px',font:'13px Segoe UI, sans-serif',boxShadow:'0 4px 16px #0003'});
  const title=document.createElement('strong');title.textContent='Diagnóstico GNAT · pasivo';
  const state=document.createElement('p');state.textContent='Activo durante 60 s. Busque, seleccione y abra una ficha voluntariamente.';
  panel.append(title,state);document.body.appendChild(panel);
  function button(label,handler){const b=document.createElement('button');b.textContent=label;b.style.margin='4px';b.addEventListener('click',handler);panel.appendChild(b);}
  function report(){return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify({format:'centrality-gnat-safe-diagnostic-v1',cors_localhost:'NO_VERIFICADO',active,samples}));}
  function stop(){
