````
    for(const reader of readers)void reader.cancel().catch(()=>{});readers.clear();
    state.textContent='Detenido. Hooks propios restaurados; '+samples.length+' muestras saneadas en memoria. Exporte solo si desea compartirlas.';
  }
  function download(extension){
    stop();const data=JSON.stringify(report(),null,2);
    const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([data],{type:extension==='json'?'application/json':'text/plain'}));
    const link=document.createElement('a');link.href=url;link.download='diagnostico_gnat_saneado.'+extension;
    panel.appendChild(link);link.click();link.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);
  }
  button('Detener y restaurar',stop);button('Exportar TXT',()=>download('txt'));button('Exportar JSON',()=>download('json'));
  button('Cerrar y borrar',()=>{stop();samples.length=0;panel.remove();delete window.centralityGNATDiagnostic;});
  function metadata(input,body){
    try{
      const url=new URL(typeof input==='string'||input instanceof URL?input:input.url,location.href);
      const service=url.pathname.split('/').pop();
      if(url.origin!==location.origin||!services.has(service))return null;
      const fields=new Set();let action='otro_valor_omitido';
      const add=(key,value)=>{if(!requestKeys.has(key))return;fields.add(key);if(key==='action'&&typeof value==='string'&&value.length<=60&&Object.hasOwn(actions,value))action=value;};
      for(const [key,value] of url.searchParams)add(key,value);

      }

    }catch{return null;}

  function shape(value,depth=0){
    if(value===null)return 'null';
    if(depth>=4)return Array.isArray(value)?'array':typeof value;
    if(Array.isArray(value))return {type:'array',length:Math.min(value.length,100),sample:value.length?[shape(value[0],depth+1)]:[]};

    if(JSON.stringify(sample).length>6000||JSON.stringify(samples).length+JSON.stringify(sample).length>60000){stop();return;}
    samples.push(sample);counts.set(key,(counts.get(key)||0)+1);
    state.textContent='Activo · '+samples.length+' muestras. Ningún valor de negocio ni credencial se exporta.';
    if(samples.length>=24)stop();
  }
  async function readBounded(response){
    const clone=response.clone();
    if(!clone.body?.getReader)return {text:null,state:'unavailable'};
    const reader=clone.body.getReader();readers.add(reader);const chunks=[];let length=0;
    try{
      while(true){
        const chunk=await reader.read();if(chunk.done)break;
        length+=chunk.value.byteLength;
        if(length>MAX_RESPONSE){void reader.cancel().catch(()=>{});return {text:null,state:'oversize'};}
        chunks.push(chunk.value);
      }
      const bytes=new Uint8Array(length);let offset=0;for(const chunk of chunks){bytes.set(chunk,offset);offset+=chunk.byteLength;}
      return {text:new TextDecoder().decode(bytes),state:'observed'};
    }catch{return {text:null,state:'unavailable'};}
    finally{readers.delete(reader);}
  }
  function fetchObserver(...args){
    const meta=active?metadata(args[0],args[1]?.body):null;
    const promise=Reflect.apply(originalFetch,this,args);
    if(meta&&pending<4&&samples.length<24&&(counts.get(meta.service+':'+meta.action)||0)<3){

      if(extra?.action==='otro_valor_omitido'&&original.action!=='otro_valor_omitido'){meta.action=original.action;meta.operation=original.operation;}
````

````
/*
 * Puente local V5: exporta SOLO la vista ACTUAL del mapa Centrality
 * como un JSON con PNG + extensión EPSG:3857.
 * Sin llamadas a una API privada, sin lectura de cookies/tokens,
 * sin peticiones adicionales, sin recorrido masivo.
 * Requiere acceso autorizado a la cartografía y permiso de conservarla.
 * Cómo usar: Centrality > Mapas > F12 > Sources > Snippets.
 */
(async () => {
  'use strict';
  const frame=document.querySelector('iframe#content');
  const w=frame?.contentWindow, map=w?.webMap_?.map;
  if (!map) {console.error('Abre Centrality > Mapas antes de ejecutar.');return;}
  const doc=w.document;
  const view=map.getView();
  const size=map.getSize();
  if(!size?.[0]||!size?.[1])throw Error('Mapa no disponible');
  if(Math.abs(view.getRotation?.()||0)>0.000001){
    console.error('Pon el mapa con orientación norte antes de exportar.');return;
  }
  // Use view extent; no network requests triggered intentionally.
  const bounds=view.calculateExtent(size);
  let output=doc.createElement('canvas');
  output.width=Math.round(size[0]);output.height=Math.round(size[1]);
  const ctx=output.getContext('2d');
  ctx.fillStyle='#fff';ctx.fillRect(0,0,output.width,output.height);
  const viewport=map.getViewport(), canvases=[...viewport.querySelectorAll('.ol-layer canvas, canvas.ol-layer')]
    .filter(c=>c.width>0&&c.height>0);
  if(!canvases.length){console.error('No se encontraron lienzos OpenLayers para capturar.');return;}
  let composed=0;
  for(const canvas of canvases){
    const owner=canvas.closest('.ol-layer')||canvas.parentElement;
    if(w.getComputedStyle(canvas).display==='none')continue;
    if(owner&&w.getComputedStyle(owner).display==='none')continue;
    ctx.save();
    const alpha=Number.parseFloat(w.getComputedStyle(owner||canvas).opacity||'1');
    ctx.globalAlpha=Number.isFinite(alpha)?alpha:1;
    const cssTransform=w.getComputedStyle(canvas).transform;
    if(cssTransform&&cssTransform!=='none'){
      const m=new w.DOMMatrix(cssTransform);
      ctx.setTransform(m.a,m.b,m.c,m.d,m.e,m.f);
    }else{
      const r=canvas.getBoundingClientRect(),origin=viewport.getBoundingClientRect();
      ctx.setTransform(r.width/canvas.width,0,0,r.height/canvas.height,r.left-origin.left,r.top-origin.top);
    }
    try{ctx.drawImage(canvas,0,0);composed++;}catch(e){console.warn('Se omitió una capa no capturable',e.message);}
    ctx.restore();
  }
  if(!composed){console.error('No fue posible componer ninguna capa.');return;}
  let dataUrl;
  try{dataUrl=output.toDataURL('image/png');}
  catch(e){console.error('El navegador bloqueó la captura por seguridad CORS:',e.message);return;}
  if(dataUrl==='data:,'){console.error('No se generó imagen.');return;}
  const payload={format:'centrality-local-capture-v1',projection:'EPSG:3857',
    bounds3857:bounds,width:output.width,height:output.height,
    zoom:view.getZoom(),capturedAt:new Date().toISOString(),imageDataUrl:dataUrl};
  const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(payload)],{type:'application/json'});
  if(blob.size>16*1024*1024){console.error('Vista demasiado grande para piloto. Acerca o reduce ventana.');return;}
  const url=w.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a=doc.createElement('a');a.href=url;
  a.download='centrality_vista_'+new Date().toISOString().slice(0,19).replace(/[:T]/g,'-')+'.json';
  doc.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();
  w.setTimeout(()=>w.URL.revokeObjectURL(url),10000);
  console.log('Captura V5 exportada:',output.width,'x',output.height,
    'zoom',view.getZoom(),'capas canvas',composed,'. Importa el JSON en la pestaña Mosaico de Centrality Local.');
})();
````

### docs/ARQUITECTURA.md

- Origin: unchanged original workspace file

- Package locator: `snapshot/docs/ARQUITECTURA.md`

- SHA256: `0999DE9BF075718C1910F87A2D41FFFF05B07831C5FC7F032F8AA344C1BC45F6`

````
# Arquitectura: núcleo local e integración opcional

Se conserva el piloto modular. Ninguna consulta GNAT escribe en `puntos`; mapa, tabla y SQLite pueden usarse sin GNAT.

```text
KMZ/KML/CSV -> app/importers.py -> app/db.py -> app/server.py -> web/app.js
Captura autorizada JSON+PNG -> app/captures.py -> API local -> web/map.js
Estado desconectado / MOCK -> app/integrations/gnat.py -> API local -> web/gnat-client.js
                                                              -> capa opcional del mapa
Portal oficial -> snippet diagnóstico MANUAL -> TXT/JSON saneado por clic
```

## Responsabilidades

| Archivo | Límite |
|---|---|
| `app/db.py` | Puntos locales, índices, supervisores y búsquedas exactas; ningún dato GNAT |
| `app/importers.py` | CSV histórico/v6, KML y KMZ; validación previa a cualquier reemplazo |
| `app/server.py` | HTTP loopback, validación Host/Origin, CSP y rutas locales |
| `app/captures.py` | Capturas PNG + manifiesto local, sin capturador remoto |
| `app/integrations/gnat.py` | Contrato desconectado, fixtures, GeoJSON/errores/metadatos; sin cliente HTTP |
| `web/gnat-client.js` | Ficha GNAT/MOCK, estado, texto seguro, capa optativa; no importa puntos |
| `web/map.js` | Canvas WebMercator; puntos, X, capturas y geometría opcional separada |
| `bridge/diagnostico_contrato_gnat.js` | Monitor pasivo del origen oficial; no conecta localhost |

`layerProviders` describe capturas locales disponibles y WMTS no disponible. El mapa actual conserva el renderizado de capturas; no se crea un framework de proveedores ni un transportador WMTS especulativo. OSM es un fondo optativo, apagado por defecto.

## SQLite y transición reversible

La tabla de negocio sigue siendo `puntos`. Se añaden `clase` (`recorrido` por defecto), `import_id` y `bloques` (lista JSON). `tipo` sigue describiendo poste/subestación/otro; no se confunde con la clase recorrido/apertura. En el CSV conjunto, el encabezado `ClasePunto` identifica esta nueva clase; `Clase` conserva su significado histórico como alias de `Tipo`. Así pueden coexistir ambas columnas sin colisión de importación.

Al abrir un esquema anterior, primero se genera una copia SQLite consistente `puntos.sqlite3.pre-<UTC>.bak` usando la API `backup`; después se añaden solo columnas ausentes. La migración es idempotente, sin DROP, renumeración ni pérdida de campos. Las pruebas usan un esquema legado independiente y comprueban el respaldo antes del ALTER.

Cada importación valida todas las filas antes de borrar/insertar, en una transacción. Solo reemplaza las clases presentes. El API devuelve 409 y `requires_confirmation` cuando esas clases ya contienen puntos; la UI solo reenvía con `confirm_replace=1` después del sí explícito. Exportar CSV no reemplaza un respaldo completo de `datos`.

Rollback: detener el servidor, preservar los nuevos datos aparte y restaurar una **copia** del `.bak` con el código anterior. No abrir una copia activa simultáneamente con dos versiones. Para deshacer la nueva distribución sin datos añadidos, se puede volver al piloto original intacto; no se debe eliminar el original ni sus capturas.

## GNAT sin retención corporativa automática

`DisconnectedAdapter` implementa las operaciones neutrales y devuelve desconexión real. `FixtureAdapter` es una selección explícita, nunca una conexión validada. Las rutas `/api/gnat/mock` no comparten importadores ni funciones de escritura de puntos.

Los resultados contienen ID normalizado, código/nombre, tipo, GeoJSON WGS84, procedencia, hora y error estructurado. `raw_response` solo permanece en memoria del resultado diagnóstico; `public_result` lo elimina del límite HTTP. Los metadatos HTML/widgets se convierten a texto; la UI usa `textContent`, no `innerHTML`.

No se añade `gnat_cache.py` ni tabla de caché: no hay necesidad funcional ni permiso de retención demostrado. Borrar ficha elimina el estado efímero y la capa opcional. Vincular/actualizar/desvincular activos oficiales no está implementado porque no existe un vínculo real. Cualquier futura sincronización exigirá autorización y confirmación explícita, sin sobrescribir KMZ/supervisores automáticamente.

## API compatible

| Método/ruta | Resultado |
|---|---|
| GET `/api/health`, `/api/points` | Estado local / puntos con campos previos + campos aditivos |
| POST `/api/import?filename=...` | Archivo validado; 409 requiere confirmación antes de reemplazar |
| PATCH `/api/points/{id}` | Atributos por ID SQLite, no número visible |
| POST `/api/supervisores`, `/api/batch` | Asignación / búsqueda exacta local |
| GET `/api/export.csv` | CSV compatible con campos aditivos para conservar clases/IDs/bloques |
| GET `/api/export.csv?kind=route` o `opening` | Esquemas v6 independientes con coma decimal |
| GET/POST/DELETE `/api/captures...` | Rutas de capturas originales |
| GET `/api/gnat/status` | Estado desconectado, `validated:false` |
| POST `/api/gnat/mock` | Solo fixtures locales: `query`, `asset_types` o `object_id` |

No hay ruta de consulta remota, proxy abierto, credenciales, almacenamiento del navegador ni SDK externo. CSP permite imágenes OSM solo por la opción explícita; `connect-src` permanece en `'self'`.

## Verificación y límites

Pruebas Python de parsers, migración y contratos; HTTP real loopback con datos temporales; pruebas Node de mapa/ficha/monitor mediante stubs estándar. Las respuestas 401/403/timeout/success:false son casos simulados del normalizador, **no evidencia de acceso al servidor**. No se ejecutó Chrome autenticado ni el diagnóstico en GNAT. No hay atlas ni descarga masiva.
````

````
# Cambios de esta entrega

## Núcleo conservado

Se mantiene la aplicación Python/SQLite/canvas y sus rutas API; importaciones históricas, búsqueda exacta, edición/supervisores y capturas manuales. El piloto original y sus datos quedan fuera de la copia de trabajo y no se incluyen datos privados en el paquete nuevo.

## Compatibilidad v6 y mejoras v4

- Recorrido/aperturas coexisten; importación reemplaza solo su clase, con confirmación explícita si ya hay puntos.
- ID externo separado del ID SQLite y del número visible; numeración con huecos conservada.
- Bloques compartidos en aperturas y filtro independiente de supervisor; X de tamaño equivalente al punto y etiquetas de números/códigos independientes.
- Corregida la ida/vuelta de la exportación conjunta: `ClasePunto` distingue recorrido/apertura y conserva el alias legado `Clase` para tipo de activo, sin colisión con `Tipo`.
- CSV v6 de ambos tipos, separador `;`, coma decimal para Excel chileno. Coordenadas corruptas no se adivinan; rechazo completo con fila/columna.
- Migración aditiva idempotente con respaldo previo, sin borrar puntos existentes.
- KMZ inválido devuelve validación 400, no 500. OSM apagado por defecto, voluntario.
- Corregido el fallback de `INICIAR.bat`: CMD expandía `%errorlevel%` antes de la detección de Python dentro de un bloque; se usa evaluación runtime `if not errorlevel 1`.

## GNAT opcional realista

- Adaptador desconectado y fixtures explícitos; no hay conexión corporativa simulada.
- Point del informe; LineString/MultiLineString sintéticos separados, no geometría inferida de códigos.
- Ficha segura con procedencia/hora, capa optativa y borrado de sesión; consulta real deshabilitada.
- Normalización y errores de contrato probados sin transporte remoto; JSON crudo solo en memoria interna, sin caché oficial SQLite.
- Un diagnóstico pasivo manual en JS/TXT idénticos, acotado y restaurable. Acciones candidatas de fuentes guardadas se diferencian de confirmación viva.

## Pendientes explícitos

Contrato/permiso/API/sesión/CORS para conexión real; geometría real del tramo; importación manual oficial saneada; prueba visual manual en Chrome y ejecución del diagnóstico por el usuario. No se añaden módulos empresariales, scraper, atlas/PDF, autenticación ni instalaciones.

Pruebas y comandos en README; evidencias de desarrollo en `odd/tasks/centrality-gnat-integration.md` dentro del workspace (no requerido para ejecutar el paquete).
````

````
# Contexto vigente

Esta entrega conserva el piloto modular y añade compatibilidad de recorrido/aperturas e integración GNAT opcional **desconectada**. El objetivo es una versión estable y demostrable sin datos corporativos descargados ni automatización masiva.

La base local `puntos` es independiente de cualquier activo oficial. Códigos exactos no son identificadores únicos; duplicados requieren selección, los números visibles no son índices y los colores de supervisores no son bloques de trabajo.

El informe aportado describe OpenLayers EPSG:3857, raster WMTS MT/BT/infraestructura y un Point. Fuentes guardadas aportan acciones candidatas, no autorización ni contrato vivo. Ver `CONTRATO_GNAT.md` antes de cualquier trabajo remoto.

El piloto anterior y los scripts de `legacy/` son referencias históricas, no dependencias ejecutables de la app. Sus planes históricos de cobertura de 100 km/atlas no son el alcance vigente. No ejecutar scripts legacy para conseguir acceso, automatización o funciones fuera de esta entrega.

Conservar: KMZ/KML/CSV, capturas autorizadas, numeración, supervisores, operación sin GNAT y Python estándar. Pendientes: autorización y contrato real, geometría del tramo y prueba manual del diagnóstico. No inferir coordenadas de CSV dañados ni copiar cookies/sesiones.
````

````
# Contrato GNAT: evidencia, no promesas de conexión

**Estado entregado: desconectado.** Hay contrato aislado, fixtures MOCK y un diagnóstico pasivo. Ningún endpoint remoto fue invocado desde esta aplicación ni durante sus pruebas.

## Matriz de evidencia

| Tema | Observado / implementado | No observado o pendiente |
|---|---|---|
| Interfaz original | Según informe aportado: OpenLayers EPSG:3857, iframe del mismo origen, 40 capas | Independencia de iframe, cliente externo permitido |
| MT/BT/infraestructura | Informe: raster PNG WMTS; matrixSet `centrality`, niveles 0–20 | Acceso/condiciones desde localhost; vector masivo, descarga de zonas |
| Poste F5353 | Informe: ID 8659987, Point WGS84 `[-72.06376236,-37.780086409]` | Vigencia actual; exactitud de búsquedas remotas |
| Tramo F5353–F5352 | Informe: ID 8659985; identificación de objetos con descripción HTML | LineString/MultiLineString real del tramo; discrepancia 6/8 AWG sin resolver |
| Acciones | Nombres encontrados en cliente guardado, detallados abajo | Contrato vivo, autorización, parámetros obligatorios actuales |
| App local | SQLite/capturas, búsqueda exacta local, MOCK y errores probados sin red | Consultas GNAT reales, sesión/CORS de localhost |
| Importación oficial manual | Alternativa futura prioritaria si no hay conexión permitida | Formato/permiso de retención: función diferida, no usar importación de puntos |

El informe anterior observó HTTP 200 dentro del portal; **HTTP 200 no autoriza un cliente externo**. Su monitor ocultó los valores action. La lectura de fuentes locales aporta candidatos, no una nueva confirmación en vivo.

## Candidatos encontrados en fuentes locales

Referencias originales inspeccionadas, no dependencias ni archivos de esta distribución:

| Servicio / acción candidata | Fuente local | Solicitud / respuesta conocida |
|---|---|---|
| `svc_wmc.php` / `textual_search` | `Centrality - Mapa_files/map.js.descarga`, sección desde ~824 | Cliente usa búsqueda y campos `object_id`, `surrogate_in_map_id`, `pin_location`, `display`; informe registra `action,limit,page,query,start`. Forma real de cada resultado pendiente |
| `svc_wmc.php` / `get_objects_geojson` | mismo archivo, ~1546 | Cliente envía `object_ids` como JSON; informe observó `{success,data:Feature}` solo para Point |
| `svc_wmc.php` / `get_objects_info_for_point` | mismo archivo, ~1660 | `lonlat` + viewport; informe: `bounds,scale,units_per_pixel`; respuesta `data` array de `id,display,type,image,description` |
| `Web/component/svc_object_view.php` / `get_object_definition` | `ObjectViewDlg.js.descarga`, ~203 | `object_id`; informe: título, definiciones de widgets y message_error |
| mismo servicio / `get_widget_content` | mismo archivo, ~566 | `object_id,widget_ids`; contenido string/array, posiblemente HTML; cantidades de widgets variables |

`svc_web_services.php`, `svc_location.php`, `svc_condition.php` aparecen en el informe con nombres de campos; no se inventan acciones ni se consideran buscadores de postes. No hay exportación geográfica confirmada; solo se podrá evaluar una función ofrecida por la UI y autorizada. El monitor omite valores action desconocidos para evitar capturar secretos por una clasificación demasiado amplia.

## Contrato de la aplicación

Implementación Python: `app/integrations/gnat.py`.

| Operación neutra | Método | Estado actual |
|---|---|---|
| searchAssets | `search_assets(query, asset_types=None, pagination=None)` | Desconectado; MOCK exacto trim+upper, filtro y ambigüedad. No paginación remota inventada |
| getAssetGeometry | `get_asset_geometry(object_id)` | MOCK Point / líneas sintéticas; ID no conocido devuelve not_found |
| getAssetMetadata | `get_asset_metadata(object_id)` | MOCK textual, sin ejecución HTML |
| getAssetById | `get_asset_by_id(object_id)` | Composición de ficha MOCK |
| listAvailableLayers | `list_available_layers()` | Lista vacía: no confunde capas observadas con acceso disponible |
| getConnectionStatus | `get_connection_status()` | `validated:false`, desconectado; MOCK no se declara conectado |

Resultados: `{ok,data,error,consulted_at}`; activo normalizado con `id` string, `code/name`, `asset_type`, geometría WGS84, metadatos textuales, `source`, `consulted_at`, `synthetic`. El resultado interno también conserva `raw_response` en memoria; no se vuelca ni persiste. Líneas sintéticas usan IDs `SYN-LINE` y `SYN-MULTI`, no el ID del tramo real.

Errores diferenciados: desconexión, timeout, conexión, 401/403, HTTP distinto de 200, success:false, JSON inválido/vacío, campos ausentes y geometría inválida. El normalizador recibe respuestas de prueba; no ejecuta peticiones. No hay caché oficial SQLite ni actualización automática de puntos.

## Una ejecución manual de 30–60 segundos

Solo si su cuenta y organización permiten observar estas operaciones:

1. Abra Mapas en la sesión oficial ya autorizada. En DevTools seleccione el **contexto del iframe del mapa** que realiza las solicitudes; el snippet solo observa ese contexto, no otros marcos/orígenes.
2. En **Sources → Snippets**, cree un snippet y pegue íntegro `bridge/diagnostico_contrato_gnat.txt` (idéntico al JS). Ejecútelo. Aparece el panel **Diagnóstico GNAT · pasivo**.
3. Busque un código de su elección, seleccione un resultado y abra su ficha «i». Seleccione voluntariamente un tramo y abra su información. Si existe una función de exportación geográfica ofrecida por la UI, no la ejecute sin permiso; informe que existe y su etiqueta por separado.
4. En 30–60 segundos pulse **Detener y restaurar**, y luego **Exportar TXT** o **Exportar JSON**. La captura también se detiene a los 60 segundos. Revise el archivo antes de compartirlo. Use **Cerrar y borrar** para eliminarlo de memoria.
5. Entregue solo el archivo saneado, no HAR, cookies, solicitudes copiadas, fichas HTML ni capturas con credenciales.

El monitor no realiza ninguna solicitud. Solo envuelve fetch y XMLHttpRequest del contexto actual, sobre el mismo origen y los cinco nombres de servicio del informe. Retiene secuencia, servicio sin URL, acción funcional conocida, nombres de campos permitidos, HTTP, forma acotada y tipo de geometría. No registra códigos, IDs, coordenadas, valores de fichas/búsqueda, URL completa, headers, cookies, Authorization, tokens ni local/sessionStorage.

Límites: 24 muestras totales, 3 por servicio/acción, respuesta 64 KB, petición formulario inspeccionable hasta 16 KB, forma de 4 niveles/12 claves y exportación aproximada menor de 64 KB. Respuestas grandes, no JSON o no observables se marcan como tales. XHR con responseType JSON se omite (`unavailable_json_object`), porque no ofrece una cota fiable de bytes sin recorrer el objeto completo. Cuerpos Request/streams no inspeccionables no se clonan; acciones no incluidas en la lista segura se omiten. La instrumentación añade trabajo acotado, sin modificar las respuestas entregadas a la UI. Detener restaura solo sus propios hooks; no pisa herramientas que los hayan sustituido después.

**CORS localhost = NO_VERIFICADO.** Este diagnóstico no prueba autenticación externa, no reutiliza una sesión y no hace pruebas CORS. Una captura sin muestras tampoco demuestra ausencia de servicio: confirme primero el contexto correcto.

## Siguiente conexión permitida

1. Preferir API oficialmente documentada/habilitada para cliente externo con permiso explícito.
2. Si solo se permite el mismo origen, evaluar un puente mínimo allí, exclusivamente por acciones iniciadas por el usuario y resultados cuya retención está permitida.
3. Si ninguno es viable, acordar importación manual saneada como alternativa offline, separada de los puntos de trabajo.

No se implementa ninguno sin contrato/autorización. Se excluyen extracción de cookies/tokens, contraseñas guardadas, evasión CORS, Chrome inseguro, proxies abiertos, push no respaldado y descarga masiva. Una futura consulta significará petición bajo demanda con hora/error, no actualización en segundo plano.

## Pruebas realizadas

Python estándar: contrato desconectado/MOCK, coincidencia exacta/duplicada, geometrías, HTML/widgets, timeout, conexión, 401/403, success:false, JSON vacío/malformado y campos faltantes. HTTP loopback: estado y MOCK no modifican SQLite y no exponen raw_response. Node: texto inerte/XSS, estados/overlay y monitor con stubs, límites/origen/restauración. **No** se probó en el portal, no hubo tráfico externo y no existe una conexión GNAT validada.
````

````
# Independent Centrality Local integration plan

**Proposal only:** use GNAT as an explicitly authorized read-data source, not an embedded visual dependency. This phase changes no Local application code and establishes no independent authentication rights.

## Minimal architecture

| Boundary | Responsibility |
| --- | --- |
| Native Local UI | Candidate picker, local map, technical-view sections, per-item status and evidence/staleness indicators |
| Domain model | Separate object/map/feature identifiers, asset types and host associations; geometry distinct from connectivity |
| Authorized source adapter | Versioned request/response contracts, origin/action allowlist, authorization checks, timeouts/error mapping and rate policy |
| Read-widget adapters | Only individually verified consultation services; no GNAT component execution or operational controls |
| Private cache | Authorization-scoped minimal records, TTL/invalidation, access controls and provenance |

The inspector is a temporary research tool, **not** the production adapter, login mechanism, proxy, scraper or UI automation dependency. Do not extract browser sessions/tokens or assume browser authentication transfers to Local. Obtain an owner-approved authentication and read-access mechanism before implementing remote integration.

## Behavior to reproduce

1. Search once per normalized uncached code, retain all candidates and distinct IDs; require selection where ambiguous.
2. Map lookup uses selected map surrogate; technical definition uses selected object ID. Preserve returned feature ID independently.
3. Decode per-action wrappers: geometry success/data Feature or FeatureCollection; definition/content success/data; Parts data/total without success; external IDs success/data without total. Match geometry by Feature.id and content by widget_id, not position. Preserve server bbox separately. Tested consumer CRS is EPSG:4326, not a universal declaration.
4. Build native technical sections from safe metadata. Only execute an explicitly approved consultation adapter; never load remote component classes/configuration.
5. Return partial results when geometry/widget calls fail; a valid identity remains visible. No automatic operational action or unverified widget fallback.

See [geolocation acceptance rules](GEOLOCALIZACION.md) and [network representation](../GNAT_TOPOLOGIA.md). [Observed samples](EVIDENCIA_CONFIRMADA.md) satisfy successful point/line/two-ID shape evidence; synthetic fixtures alone do not. Keep IDs opaque: accept digit strings or safe integer numbers without lossy coercion, and preserve object/map/feature roles.

## Implementation gates

- Authorized independent authentication, permitted fields, cache/storage rights and expected rate budget.
- Successful sample schemas and consumer CRS are confirmed; characterize errors/login/rate-limit/partial results and future types before production use.
- Individual consultation-widget scoping/read authorization and native safe rendering; use a restricted HTML sanitizer, never embedded scripts or GNAT component execution.
- Explicit topology evidence before electrical routes; no inferred proximity connections.
- Adapter tests using approved anonymized real fixtures plus synthetic negative cases; visible provenance and incomplete-state UX.

Tradeoff: a native adapter avoids GNAT visual coupling but requires stable verified contracts and authorization maintenance. Reusing GNAT HTML/component logic would be faster superficially but imports unsafe execution, visual dependence and hidden side effects; it is not the proposed design.
````

````
# Investigation coverage

Status: **partial, bounded live bodies confirmed**, 2026-10-21. [Evidence ledger](EVIDENCIA_CONFIRMADA.md) distinguishes response, UI/live-client, transformed observed, synthetic and proposed evidence.

| Asset / feature | Observed coverage | Remaining gap |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Five known search cases | Prior E1 success/data bodies; F56822 longer/duplicate labels; F514139 separate pole/DC/DBC/DF IDs | Exactness, complete paging, negatives |
| FAP1783 geometry | Singleton Point Feature, id/label/bbox | Future types and explicit source declaration |
| F514139 pole/DBC | Separate Points plus actual two-ID FeatureCollection | Partial errors, maxima/rates; no order guarantee |
| CRS | Reader default EPSG:4326; real FAP transform matches EPSG:3857 center | Not server-wide guarantee |
| Definition | Raw root/recursive metadata; read-only/generator separate | Backend machine class IDs and server access boundary |
| Generated content | widget_id/content string/array/null; reordered response | Nonempty row schemas, safe HTML semantics |
| Parts | Scoped location_id, data/total, two rows | Paging/error completeness |
| DBC IDs5 | Verified read-only original tab; scoped five-row success/data | Independent source access, negative/error semantics |
| DC/DF technical views | Cached original UI selections; distinct display classes/types; DF three-level breadcrumb | Raw bodies not newly collected; machine classes/host semantics |
| Electrical widget142 | Script-bearing read-write loader metadata | Not executed, no topology contract |
| MT/BT/infrastructure visual layers | Live WMTS image/png visible; ImageWMS elsewhere; Vector selection | Not area inventory; no BT vector lookup |
| MT line32327210 | Original point-identify, singleton two-vertex LineString, original line view | No formal endpoint nodes/terminals or electrical route |
| Inspector | 26 prior tests + three transformed-observed contract tests; existing diagnostic checks | Standalone discovery failed, zero records; no live attachment/stop proof |
| Local integration | Native read-adapter/render/cache proposal only | No Local application changes or independent authentication |
| Error/auth/rate-limit/area cases | Not characterized | No fabricated absence, capacity or inventory guarantee |

No global extraction, point/area sweep, operational widget, load probe or unknown-effect electrical request occurred. Historical raw-unverified line8659985/F5353/F5352 is not topology evidence.

## Inspector capability gaps

Manual stdin markers are not automatic DOM/UI observation. JavaScript exceptions are not collected. HTML/business text is discarded. Current projection omits bbox/display_name/content values and exposition mode strings; external IDs and point-identify are not body-allowlisted. New transformed fixtures came from parent MCP, not observer output, and do not silently close those gaps.

## Acceptance boundary

Confirmed samples support a more concrete read-adapter design, not production readiness. Independent access/cache rights, error contracts, type semantics, widget security and topology remain blocked for corresponding features. Privacy permission is resolved for transient reception; standalone endpoint discovery is a separate technical failure. See [pending work](PENDIENTES.md).
````

````
# GNAT contract evidence

Status: **partial, updated with bounded live evidence**, 2026-10-21. Successful point, two-ID geometry, line, definition and consultation responses are confirmed; production integration and electrical topology are not. Start with [confirmed evidence](EVIDENCIA_CONFIRMADA.md).

## Evidence classes

| Key | Class | Source and limit |
| --- | --- | --- |
| E1 | Prior real response | [Search report](../GNAT_BUSQUEDA.md), five bounded codes; preserved as historical evidence |
| E2 | Prior UI / loaded-client | [Selection report](../GNAT_SELECCION_FICHAS.md); earlier gaps superseded only where E8 confirms them |
| E3 | Saved-client | Map geometry consumer 1520-1563, highlighting 1487-1513, layer handling 1016-1066/1127-1138 and point identification 1632-1770 in saved map.js; not universal server guarantees |
| E4 | Saved-config-derived | Earlier raster-layer observation; credential-bearing configuration excluded from deliverables and further inspection |
| E5 | Synthetic | [Invented geometry fixture](fixtures/geojson.synthetic.json); tooling only |
| E6 | Offline checks | Inspector and diagnostic tests; not live handshake proof |
| E7 | User domain context | Shared codes, DC/DBC/DF terminology, transformer CD versus pole COD; not a field survey |
| E8 | Current observed response / live-client / UI | [Request sequence and boundaries](EVIDENCIA_CONFIRMADA.md); transformed fixtures identify subsets |

## Contract maturity

| Contract | Confirmed for tested samples | Still unknown |
| --- | --- | --- |
| textual_search | E1 form POST and success/data candidates | Exactness, complete paging, negatives/errors/auth |
| get_objects_geojson | E8 success/data Feature for singleton points/line; two-ID FeatureCollection; IDs and bbox | Other types/counts, partial errors, maxima/rates, universal CRS declaration |
| get_object_definition | E8 root fields, recursive widget metadata, exposition/generator separation | Formal backend asset class/type IDs, server authorization guarantees |
| get_widget_content | E8 success/data entries keyed by widget_id; string/array/null; changed response order | Full nonempty-row schemas and safe HTML semantics |
| list_location_parts | E8 location_id scope, paging request, data/total without success | Complete paging and error behavior |
| list_object_external_ids | E8 object_id scope; success/data, five rows, no total | Independent access permission and error/paging guarantees |
| get_objects_info_for_point | E8 click-local success/data identification of one MT line | Area inventory and electrical endpoints |

Raw geometry HTTP envelopes are now confirmed, not merely inferred from a helper. CRS confidence is sample-scoped consumer/transform verification, not an explicit server-wide declaration.

## Safety boundary

The user authorized transient CDP reception; credential retention/publication remains prohibited. Standalone observer startup failed at exact loopback discovery with zero records, while Chrome DevTools MCP supplied E8. No alternate ports/settings or credentials were inspected for recovery. # Confirmed bounded GNAT evidence
````

````
Date: 2026-10-21. **Point and one MT line geometry, a successful two-ID batch, several response wrappers and UI read-only behavior are confirmed. Electrical connectivity, area inventory and independent access are not.**

This is the current evidence ledger. Earlier [search](../GNAT_BUSQUEDA.md) and [selection](../GNAT_SELECCION_FICHAS.md) reports are preserved historical snapshots, not silently rewritten.

## Collection and provenance

The parent observed original authorized Chrome UI/client methods through Chrome DevTools MCP. Missing bodies were recovered from already-generated calls, without repeated searches. New bounded geometry work used two known IDs, one original point-identify click on a visible segment, and one line lookup; no sweep, load/capacity probe, operational action or unknown widget was executed. Local request references below are session-scoped, not globally unique or replay tokens.

The standalone Node observer attempted a two-second startup once: ACL initialization succeeded, zero records, exit 1; exact loopback /json/list returned non-success HTTP (numeric status not retained). No target attachment or live clean shutdown was established. No alternate port/browser-setting changes were attempted. MCP evidence is **not** proof the standalone observer works live.

## Response sequence

All listed response bodies were observed as successful samples. HTTP200 was explicitly retained for 4021/4048/4111/4116/4117; do not infer unrecorded status codes for other entries.

| Reference | Original action and scope | Confirmed response |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 3999 | get_objects_geojson [5018289], FAP1783 | success/data Feature, Point, id/properties.display_name/bbox; no crs |
| 4021 | get_object_definition object_id=5018289 | success/data; title/widget_definition/message_error keys; recursive widget metadata |
| 4048 | get_widget_content, same object, bounded known widget list | success/data array of widget_id/content; string/array/null; response order differs |
| 4049 | list_location_parts location_id=5018289, page1/start0/limit100 | data two rows, total2; no success field |
| 4059 | get_objects_geojson [4013068], F514139 pole | singleton Point Feature with id/label/bbox |
| 4075 | get_objects_geojson [28557382], F514139 DBC | separate singleton Point Feature; distinct ID and coordinate |
| 4110 | Original verified read-only IDs5 tab on DBC28557382 | list_object_external_ids success/data five rows; no total; all row read_only=false |
| 4111 | Original zoomToObjects [4013068,28557382] | **Actual server two-ID success/data FeatureCollection**, two independently identified Point features |
| 4116 | Original onMapClick, visible blue F514139 segment, no pick/drawing mode | get_objects_info_for_point success/data one row: id32327210, display F514139 - F514140, type Tramo aéreo MT, description string |
| 4117 | Original zoomToObjects [32327210] | success/data Feature, LineString with two vertices, id/label/bbox; no node/terminal IDs |

4116 description UI showed medium-voltage distribution cable, three phases and Normal/Real circuit display Cajón [S223]. These are displayed attributes, not a terminal graph. Maintenance/operator details are excluded. The consumer references image, but **image was not verified in the body**.

## Geometry and CRS

Loaded OpenLayers GeoJSON reader dataProjection.getCode() returned EPSG:4326, consistent with the original fallback. Actual fromLonLat transformation of the observed FAP coordinate into EPSG:3857 exactly matched the prior map center. Thus GNAT's consumer interprets the tested samples as longitude/latitude EPSG:4326 with a real transform consistency check. Raw responses contain no explicit crs; this is not a universal guarantee for future types/contracts.

Feature.id supports matching the tested objects, independently of response order. The two-ID success proves neither maxima, ordering guarantees, partial-error behavior nor complete inventory.

The returned line bbox extends beyond the coordinate-vertex latitude extent. Preserve server bbox separately from any derived geometry extent; do not overwrite it or call the vertices pole/terminal coordinates. One MT line is verified; BT vector geometry was not tested.

## Definition, UI and widget evidence

Definition exposition includes read-only/read-write; initializeTab calls setReadOnly(true) for read-only tabs supporting that method. IDs5 and Attachments45 are read-only/non-generator. Header267/2/253/82 are read-write/generator=true. Row read_only=false is distinct from global tab readOnly=true. This is an observed UI gate, not server-side authorization proof.

IDs component has add/edit/remove controls in its implementation, but the tested initialized tab was read-only. Only that verified tab was manually selected. Widget142 electrical loader has scripts:true and read-write exposition; **not executed**. All other unknown-effect tools remain excluded.

| UI-only live case | Confirmed display | Limit |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Line32327210 | Cable; TRAMO AÉREO MT; Lineal | Display class/type, not backend machine type IDs |
| DC28557387 | Seccionador; DC POLE TOP 900A 25KV; Puntual; F514139 -> F514139 [F514139] | Cached original candidate/info controls; no new raw DC body |
| DF28557371 | Interruptor-seccionador con fusibles; DESCONECTADOR FUSIBLE EMPRESA; Puntual; F514139 -> F66023 -> F514139 [F66023] | Intermediate breadcrumb is real UI evidence, not proof of hosting or electrical connection |

DC/DF centers differ in EPSG:3857; exact coordinates are not published. Formal machine class codes remain unverified. No device/electrical widget was activated for these cases.

## Live layers and privacy

Safe live class/format checks confirmed visible MT/BT/infrastructure WMTS image/png; selection Vector; ImageWMS elsewhere. Raster visual delivery is distinct from object-vector lookup, not a network inventory contract.

Transient CDP reception was explicitly authorized, not credential publication. One earlier saved-config snippet and a later broader WMS source-parameter read exposed sensitive fields transiently in tool context; inspection stopped/was narrowed to safe class/format projections. No credential values, source URLs, cookies or headers are included here. This does not claim zero historical exposure or bypass earlier full-header inspection refusal.

## Reproducible local evidence

[Transformed observed catalog](fixtures/contracts.observed.anonymized.json) remaps IDs, translates all coordinates/bboxes consistently, replaces labels/organization/date values, and explicitly records partial subsets. It is **not an untouched server response**, original geolocation dataset, standalone capture or invented live evidence. Point/line geometry shape and wrapper distinctions derive from real responses. Unknown nonempty widget rows and uncollected scalar values are omitted rather than fabricated. Batch bboxes are rounded to available coordinate precision.

[Contract tests](tests/observed-contracts.test.cjs) exercise existing inspector projection/schema against this catalog; they do not contact GNAT. Initial RED: three explicit missing-fixture assertions, not missing-module errors; GREEN after catalog creation. The [synthetic fixture](fixtures/geojson.synthetic.json) remains separate.

Inspector limitations remain: bbox/display_name/content values and exposition mode strings are not retained by its current projection; external-ID and point-identify bodies are not allowlisted; schema may collapse unknown keys. Manual stdin correlation, no automatic UI changes/JavaScript exceptions, and dropped HTML/business text remain capability gaps. No production code changed to hide these gaps.
````

````
# Local consultation flows and evidence

These flows describe inspected client behavior and proposed independent adapters. They do not implement GNAT replay or authorize new server actions.

## Selection and map positioning

1. E1 search returns candidates; preserve every object ID, map surrogate and display label.
2. E2 selection uses `surrogate_in_map_id` for object-backed positioning. The separate pin-only branch remains untested.
3. E2/E3 `objectsToFeatures` serializes a nonempty map-ID array for `get_objects_geojson`.
4. Consumer reads GeoJSON into the map projection, highlights features and fits their combined extent. Map view is EPSG:3857; tested samples use reader EPSG:4326 and a real point transform matched the map center (E8). No universal server declaration follows.
5. E8 confirms success/data Feature for singleton points/line and FeatureCollection for two IDs. Local must decode both, match by Feature.id and retain server bbox separately before transformation. Do not fabricate longitude/latitude from null pins or map centers.

## Technical view and widgets

1. E2 information trigger uses `object_id` to obtain the object definition.
2. Render native Local headers/tabs from safe definition fields; reject executable configuration.
3. A verified generated-content widget uses object ID plus backend widget ID(s); self-loading widgets need separate contracts.
4. Handle partial widget failures independently. A loading flag is not a permission flag. E8 confirms exposition read-only drives setReadOnly(true); row read_only=false is a different layer.

Observed FAP1783 sequence: search → positioning POST → definition POST → interleaved scripts/services → additional view-service POST → Partes request. Endpoint timing does not prove exact action or callback causality for every request. See the [original ordered network list](../GNAT_SELECCION_FICHAS.md).

## Code-list geolocation — proposed

Normalize/validate input → consult authorization-scoped cache → bounded serial searches → expose ambiguous candidates → retrieve selected map geometry → verify CRS → return per-input evidence/status → optionally load explicitly requested technical metadata. Detailed acceptance conditions: [GEOLOCALIZACION](GEOLOCALIZACION.md).

## Point identify versus inventory

E3 click handling transforms the clicked location to EPSG:4326 and requests `get_objects_info_for_point`, with viewport-scale context. The result consumer reads `id/display/description/type/image` (image not verified in the captured body); choosing original information opens `Web.showObjectView(id)`.

This is click-local identification, not an authorized area enumeration, exact feature geometry response, terminal linkage or global inventory. Do not sweep points/tiles to reconstruct an inventory.

## Current evidence boundary

Transient reception permission is resolved. Standalone discovery failed with zero records; no live observer attachment was verified. Parent MCP recovered existing bodies and bounded original client reads: two-ID geometry, click-local line identification and one line lookup. See [sequence](EVIDENCIA_CONFIRMADA.md). No area sweep or widget142 execution. New fixtures are transformed observed samples; synthetic fixtures remain distinct.
````

````
# Code-list geolocation specification

Status: **proposal with confirmed sample contracts**, not an implemented Local batch feature. [Confirmed evidence](EVIDENCIA_CONFIRMADA.md) includes three points, one MT line and a successful two-ID batch; no bulk extraction or inventory.

## Goal and input

Accept an ordered list of user-authorized pole/asset codes and return per-input candidates, separately identified selected assets, verified geometries, geographic coordinates **only when CRS is established**, and minimal metadata/provenance. Local owns the UI; GNAT access requires an independently approved read/authentication mechanism.

Preserve original text, normalize only documented whitespace/case policy, bound list size locally, and coalesce repeated lookup work without losing input positions. Reject malformed inputs without remote requests. Initial product list-size cap must be explicitly configured; no GNAT capacity is claimed.

## Evidence-backed lookup sequence

1. E1 `textual_search` uses `query/action/page/start/limit`; observed successful wrapper is `success/data`. Search is not exact-only (F56822).
2. Retain every distinct candidate object ID, independent map surrogate and label. Shared codes and duplicate labels are not duplicates.
3. Exact-match classification requires a verified structured code field or separately verified matching rule. Label prefixes alone remain heuristic. Expose uncertain matching; do not silently choose a pole or equipment.
4. For an explicitly selected object-backed candidate, E2/E3 map lookup sends its map surrogate in JSON `object_ids`; technical metadata uses object ID. E8 confirms success/data with singleton Feature or two-ID FeatureCollection for tested samples.
5. Validate returned feature identity/geometry/CRS; record missing, partial and mismatched results individually. Never assume response order corresponds to input order.

## Per-input statuses — proposed Local semantics

| Status | Meaning; evidence needed |
| --- | --- |
| `resolved` | Explicitly resolved identity and validated geometry; lon/lat may still be withheld if source CRS unverified |
| `ambiguous` | Multiple eligible assets or unresolved exactness/type; user choice required |
| `no_exact_match` | Successful characterized response, but verified matching rule finds no exact candidate; longer-code matches may exist |
| `nonexistent` | Only an authoritative characterized absence/exact-query contract with complete applicable paging; **not currently verified** |
| `match_unverified` | Response lacks verified structured code/type evidence; do not infer exactness from display alone |
| `partial_geometry_failure` | Identity resolved, geometry call/feature mapping failed, absent or unsupported; retain candidate |
| `auth_required`, `forbidden` | Characterized authentication/authorization failure; never report as absence |
| `network_error`, `rate_limited`, `source_error`, `invalid_response` | Keep failure category and safe retry state; no invented empty success |
| `invalid_input`, `cancelled` | Local validation/cancellation; no claim about GNAT inventory |

Server negative/error envelopes are unknown. Until verified, return safe generic source/invalid-response outcomes rather than guessing an error class from HTTP 200 or an HTML login page.

## Geometry and reference system

- Client map uses EPSG:3857. Loaded reader dataProjection is EPSG:4326, and actual FAP fromLonLat transformation matches the prior view center. Tested samples are therefore interpreted as lon/lat EPSG:4326 by GNAT; no raw crs declaration or universal server guarantee was observed.
- Keep raw verified geometry plus declared/verified CRS separately from derived WGS84 longitude/latitude. Publish geographic coordinates only after a confirmed transformation; validate finite values and applicable ranges.
- Point: return actual point coordinate after CRS verification. MultiPoint: retain all points; no silent first-point choice. LineString/MultiLineString/Polygon: return geometry and type, not a claimed pole coordinate; an optional representative point must be labeled derived with its method.
- `pin_location:null`, empty geometry, no returned feature and failed lookup are different conditions. Geometryless objects are not automatically nonexistent.
- Feature.id matches the tested requested IDs; match by ID, not order. Preserve source bbox separately from derived extent: the line bbox exceeds vertex latitude extent. [Transformed observed fixtures](fixtures/contracts.observed.anonymized.json) characterize samples; the [synthetic fixture](fixtures/geojson.synthetic.json) remains tooling-only.

## Batching, request budget and retries — proposed policy, not GNAT limits

E8 verifies actual server success for two known IDs in one request. Maximum IDs/body/page sizes, order guarantees, partial failures and rate remain unknown. This is not capacity evidence; no further load probing.

Start future integration with concurrency **1**, configurable minimum spacing (proposed 500 ms), bounded timeouts and at most two retries with exponential backoff/jitter only for verified idempotent reads and transient failures. Honor `Retry-After` when exposed; stop on auth/forbidden/unknown effects. Add cancellation, a total request budget and partial progress. Do not auto-retry malformed/ambiguous responses or perform unbounded paging. Operator/source-owner limits override these local defaults.

## Cache and result contract — proposal

Cache key: source environment + user/authorization scope + contract/schema version + action + normalized inputs, with object ID and map surrogate distinct; geometry entries also include source CRS/version. Deduplicate lookups, not assets. Never share cached data across unauthorized users/scopes.

Proposed TTLs: search/metadata 5 minutes; geometry 15 minutes when cache rights permit; characterized negative exact result 30 seconds. TTLs are Local policy, not source freshness guarantees. Mark age/stale status, invalidate on authorization/contract changes, allow explicit bounded refresh, and never cache auth failures as negative inventory. Prefer private encrypted storage where approved, minimal fields, access controls and retention/deletion policy; no browser credentials in cache.

Illustrative Local result only, **synthetic; not a GNAT response**:

```json
{"inputCode":"POLE_A","status":"ambiguous","candidates":[{"objectId":"1001","mapSurrogateId":"9001","assetTypeStatus":"unverified"},{"objectId":"1002","mapSurrogateId":"9002","assetTypeStatus":"unverified"}],"geometry":null,"geographicCoordinates":null,"sourceCrsStatus":"unverified","evidenceClass":"synthetic"}
```

## Acceptance before implementation

Use only the five known code cases initially: FAP1783/F562512/F562515/F56822/F514139. Real point consumer-CRS consistency and two-ID success are now verified. Partial failures remain pending; preserve shared-code assets and nonexact/duplicate labels. A new nonexistent test needs one explicitly authorized bounded safe code. Area inventory remains unavailable until a verified read contract and authorized bounded area exist; point-identify, raster tiles and viewport bounds do not confer inventory permission.
````

````
# Proposed Local evidence-preserving data model

This is a **proposal**, not a recovered GNAT database schema. Existing evidence and gaps are in [CONTRATOS](CONTRATOS.md).

| Entity | Required separation and provenance |
| --- | --- |
| QueryInput | Original code, normalized code, request order and per-input outcome; do not silently change a code |
| SearchCandidate | Source, object ID, independent map surrogate, raw display label, exact-match confidence, unverified pin location, evidence reference |
| AssetIdentity | Source-scoped object ID as opaque identifier; verified machine class/type when available; display labels separately |
| GeometryObservation | Map surrogate, returned feature ID independently, geometry, original server bbox, separate derived extent, source CRS confidence, timestamp and response reference; tested Feature.id matching does not establish universal ID equivalence |
| ObjectDefinition | Object ID, definition version/hash, separate widget sections; reject unknown executable behavior |
| WidgetObservation | Object ID + backend widget ID, verified read adapter, content schema, observation/error status |
| AssetAssociation | Parent/child or host/equipment relationship, relation type and evidence class; displayed breadcrumb is not field verification |
| NetworkConnection | Explicit source terminal/node/edge relationship, phase/state/direction and provenance; not proximity-based |

## Domain constraints

- A code is not a primary key. F514139 returned pole/DC/DBC/DF candidates; F56822 included longer codes and duplicate display labels with distinct IDs (E1).
- `object_id` selects the technical view; `surrogate_in_map_id` supplies the map lookup in inspected client logic (E2). Matching example values do not make the fields aliases.
- Preserve leading/bracketed codes as separate observed label portions until their formal semantics are verified. Do not reinterpret `[F66023]` as corruption automatically.
- User context: S/E means substation; transformer CD of S/E and pole COD represent different identifiers. System-versus-field discrepancy is a separate possibility, not the default explanation.
- Formal machine type IDs are pending. UI class labels now distinguish pole/DBC/DC/DF/line, but label parsing alone must not authorize type-dependent behavior.
- `pin_location:null` is not proof of missing geometry; geometry lookup is a separate observation.

## Illustrative synthetic record

Not a GNAT response; schema names below belong to the proposed Local model.

```json
{"inputCode":"POLE_A","objectId":"1001","mapSurrogateId":"9001","geometryStatus":"unverified","sourceCrs":null,"assetTypeStatus":"unverified","evidenceClass":"synthetic"}
```

DC28557387 displays Seccionador, DC POLE TOP 900A 25KV; DF28557371 displays Interruptor-seccionador con fusibles, DESCONECTADOR FUSIBLE EMPRESA. DF breadcrumb is F514139 -> F66023 -> F514139 [F66023]. Preserve that intermediate display association, not an inferred physical host/electrical connection or formal bracket-code meaning. These are UI-only observations, no new raw DC/DF bodies.

IDs are modeled as opaque strings to avoid numeric precision/coercion errors. Normalize digit strings or safe integer numbers only; reject precision-lost numbers, never merge identifier roles. Never equate a search candidate ID, point-identify `id`, returned feature ID, terminal ID or circuit ID without explicit linking evidence. Geometry and electrical connectivity remain distinct tables even when their coordinates coincide.
````

````
# Passive GNAT research inspector

This standalone Node 24 observer records bounded, privacy-projected evidence from the authorized Chrome page. It never searches GNAT, navigates, opens widgets, intercepts requests, injects code, reads storage, or changes application settings. Operate the original interface separately, and only for authorized read-only cases.

**Status:** offline observer verified; standalone startup failed at exact loopback discovery, zero records, no live attachment. Parent Chrome DevTools MCP separately confirmed bounded response contracts. See [current evidence](EVIDENCIA_CONFIRMADA.md); transformed observed fixtures are distinct from synthetic tests.

## Quick path

```powershell
node --test gnat-research/tests/*.test.cjs
node gnat-research/inspector/cli.cjs --help
# Run only after authorizing this browser session and validating the observer:
node gnat-research/inspector/cli.cjs observe --origin https://st.saesa.cl:8082 --endpoint http://127.0.0.1:9222 --duration 180
```

The observer requires exactly one matching page, or an explicit `--target <page-id>` when multiple matching pages exist. It rejects non-loopback debugging endpoints, unexpected origins, credentials in URLs, and cross-port debugger WebSockets. It does not start Chrome or enable remote debugging. No automatic requests are sent to GNAT.

Captures are created under `gnat-research/private/` and ignored by Git. Before connecting, Windows ACLs must prove current-user ownership and protected current-user-only access; unverified storage fails closed. Newly created directories/files receive restrictive ACLs; existing directories are checked, not relaxed. POSIX uses owner-only modes. Symlinked ancestors are rejected.

While observing, type `select_pole_1`, `select_equipment_1`, `open_view_1`, or `consult_widget_1` on stdin to mark an authorized manual action. Other supported prefixes are `search`, `batch`, `area`, and `topology`; append `_1` through `_999`. `stop`, Ctrl+C, or the duration limit ends collection and prints a value-free schema summary. Markers indicate timing, **not proven causality**. Incremental progress counts print every 15 seconds. The collector never initiates the marked action.

```powershell
node gnat-research/inspector/cli.cjs summary gnat-research/private/<capture-file>.jsonl
```

## Privacy and evidence boundary

| Layer | Retention policy |
| --- | --- |
| CDP transport | Chrome necessarily sends complete protocol frames transiently, which can include headers, cookies, URL parameters, or request bodies. This observer cannot promise those bytes never enter process memory. Do not use it when transient reception itself is forbidden. |
| Protocol ingress | JSON reviver discards header/cookie/auth/token/storage/personal-data key families before events reach the collector. No ExtraInfo events, cookie commands, raw event logging, HAR exports, console evaluation, or interception. `header_widgets` is a business metadata key, not an HTTP header. |
| Requests | Only exact authorized origin, Fetch/XHR and GET/POST. Static known endpoint/action names, local correlation numbers and a small numeric/asset-code parameter allowlist survive. URLs, request IDs and arbitrary text do not survive. Unknown actions/endpoints become `unknown`; no body retrieval. |
| Responses | Request and final response path/origin plus safe query identity must match. Duplicate/conflicting action parameters and unknown query parameters fail closed. Only successful allowlisted observed actions labelled `application/json`, `application/geo+json`, or `text/html` (optional charset parameters) are eligible for bounded JSON parsing. GNAT is known to label JSON as HTML. Actual HTML/malformed JSON is skipped, not retained; unknown actions/endpoints never trigger body retrieval. No raw body is persisted. |
| Projection | Preserve numeric object/map/widget IDs independently, finite coordinates, GeoJSON geometry types, selected class codes and widget generator booleans. Arbitrary descriptions, titles, HTML/content, names, owner/contact information and unknown business values are omitted. Unknown fields are collapsed in schema-only evidence. |
| Files and errors | Strict record projection, bounded private JSONL, generic error categories. No raw exception, server error text, credentials or real secrets in fixtures. Exact IDs and coordinates are still private business data, not publication-ready anonymized examples. |

The prior full-header network inspector refusal remains in force. The user authorized transient CDP reception, not secret retention/publication. One bounded standalone startup failed at discovery; no browser settings or alternate ports were changed. MCP observations do not prove observer attachment.

## Allowlisted observed contracts

These names come from earlier research; allowlisting permits passive response inspection, not independent invocation or a claim that every server implementation is side-effect-free.

| Endpoint | Observed action eligible for a bounded projected body |
| --- | --- |
| `/backend/saesa/svc_wmc.php` | `textual_search`, `get_objects_geojson` |
| `/backend/saesa/Web/component/svc_object_view.php` | `get_object_definition`, `get_widget_content` |
| `/backend/saesa/AM/component/svc_location.php` | `list_location_parts` |

Unsupported endpoints/actions retain only coarse request/response metadata. Do not expand the allowlist without a verified read contract. This tool does not classify widgets as safe or authorize opening them.

## Bounds and limitations

- Session: default 180 seconds, maximum 1800; 10,000 events/records, 100 pending network requests, 30-second request age, 32 pending CDP commands, 5-second command timeout.
- Network buffers: 128 KiB total, 64 KiB per resource, 4 KiB request post data. Eligible bodies: at most 64 KiB reported encoded length and 64 KiB decoded UTF-8; base64 representation separately bounded. Compressed responses can transiently exceed their encoded length before rejection. Protocol frames above 256 KiB are discarded after WebSocket receipt.
- Output: 8 MiB total, 128 KiB per JSONL line; 100 markers; bounded schema depth/fields, arrays and coordinate nesting. Request parameter arrays are retained only when all entries are numeric and the original length is at most two; otherwise the entire parameter is omitted, not truncated. Body arrays retain at most 200 projected entries and drop unsafe values, so neither array lengths nor indexes prove original cardinality/order. Explicit source nulls remain null; dropped values do not become invented nulls. Missing/omitted fields are not full-request evidence; skipped bodies are not negative business results.
- No guarantee of collection before attachment, request completeness, retry success, server CRS, widget readability, topology, or absence of incidental server effects. Empty/missing values cannot be interpreted as nonexistent coordinates or assets.
- `object_id` and `surrogate_in_map_id` are retained as distinct fields. Coordinates are preserved without assigning a CRS or deriving connectivity.
- HTML widget content and free-form descriptions are schema-only. Inspecting safe business text needs a separate reviewed projection, not raw capture. Raster/WMS images and script bodies are intentionally not collected.
- Offline summaries report field types, null/type variation, presence/repetition counts and sanitized errors. Presence counts are per observed body, not proof of server optionality. Correlation IDs remain in JSONL; timing alone is not causal proof.
- The synthetic protocol peer tests event/reply/error behavior without a live WebSocket handshake. Actual Chrome interoperability, live clean stop, operating-system memory/swap and non-Windows file permissions remain separate checks.

## Verification

Tests cover privacy ingress, geometry/ID preservation, off-origin redirects, response-path mismatch, unknown actions, MIME/status/size gates, malformed and base64 JSON, pending/late/error cleanup, command/target restrictions, private Windows ACL output, CLI argument privacy, offline schemas and oversized lines. `fixtures/geojson.synthetic.json` is invented data, not a GNAT response.

No application source, original research document, browser state or remote inventory is changed. No global inventory, area enumeration, operational widget, batch load test, or independent GNAT request is implemented.

## Research specifications — partial, bounded live evidence

Start with [contracts and evidence](CONTRATOS.md), then [coverage](COBERTURA.md) and [pending decisions](PENDIENTES.md). Technical catalogs: [endpoints](ENDPOINTS.md), [widgets](WIDGETS.md), [data model](MODELO_DATOS.md), [flows](FLUJOS.md). Implementation proposals: [independent Local integration](CENTRALITY_IMPLEMENTACION.md), [code-list geolocation](GEOLOCALIZACION.md), [electrical geometry/topology](../GNAT_TOPOLOGIA.md).

Current checks: 29 inspector/contract tests and three existing diagnostic tests pass. Standalone discovery returned non-success HTTP; zero records, exit1, no attachment/clean-stop proof. MCP confirms point/line geometry, two-ID success and consultation wrappers. Earlier saved-config and later broader WMS-parameter inspections transiently exposed sensitive fields to tool context; stopped/narrowed, no credential values published. No zero-historical-exposure claim.

Observer still omits bbox/display_name/content values and exposition mode strings; point-identify/external-ID bodies are not allowlisted. Fixtures are curated transformed evidence from parent MCP, not observer output. Automatic UI correlation and JavaScript exceptions remain unimplemented.
````

````
# Technical-view widget catalog

Definition/body evidence now supplements UI/loaded-client evidence. [E8 provenance](EVIDENCIA_CONFIRMADA.md) is sample-scoped; no universal read authorization exists.

## Definition and permissions

Observed keys: id, title, exposition, icon_class, color, container_children, component_class, component_config, component_has_content_generator, action_has_params, action_provider_id, action_provider_item_name. Containers recurse through container_children and may have null component_class/generator. Actions were empty in the tested definition. Some grid config has dataType; values were not collected.

Loaded initializeTab applies setReadOnly(true) when exposition is read-only and the component supports it. Generator is a **separate loading flag**, not permission. Read-write or row read_only=false is not authority to mutate or proof of server security.

| IDs | Component / role | Observed mode and limit |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 267/2/253/82 | Breadcrumb/display/type/location headers | read-write, generator=true; response strings, never execute HTML |
| 5 | Acs.ExternalIdGrid / IDs | read-only, generator=false; original tab global readOnly=true; scoped body confirmed |
| 45 | Attachments | read-only, generator=false; no new content contract |
| 291 | AM.component.LocationPartsGrid / Parts | Self-loading; scoped two-row body confirmed |
| 133 | DDM.component.LocationDeviceGrid / Devices | DBC-specific metadata; not manually invoked |
| 142 | Electrical.component.NetworkingElectricalInfo | read-write; loader elec_web_object_view.php has scripts:true/autoLoad:false; **not executed** |

Common tabs: 291,101,102,105,5,45,107,138,41,42; line/DC/DF also have 142. Pole has 155/156; DBC has Devices133. Current line, DC and DF views lack 133/155/156. Except IDs5/Attachments45, observed tab exposition is read-write. Metadata does not establish side-effect safety.

## Generated-content sample

Request 4048 asked for [102,105,155,156,138,41,42,267,2,253,82,1,6,7]; return order was [2,253,267,82,41,42,138,156,102,155,105,1,6,7]. Content: strings for 2/253/267/82/1/6; arrays for 41/42/138/156/102/155/105; null for 7. Nonempty row schemas were not collected. The fixture contains only three known entries, not invented rows.

## Native Local rendering -> proposal

Separate definition and self-loading-store adapters. Use individually verified read services with independent authorization; never instantiate GNAT classes, execute component_config/action providers, or load widget142 scripts. Render structured data natively and HTML only through a restricted sanitizing renderer. Preserve per-widget failures without discarding asset identity. The UI read-only gate is evidence, not the Local security boundary.
````

````
#!/usr/bin/env node
'use strict';
const fs = require('node:fs');
const path = require('node:path');
const { Collector, Protocol, Summary, parseFrame, sanitizeRecord, validateOrigin, validateEndpoint, selectTarget, openPrivateWriter } = require('./core.cjs');
const HELP = `Passive GNAT inspector (Node 24; no dependencies)

Offline:
  node gnat-research/inspector/cli.cjs --help
  node gnat-research/inspector/cli.cjs summary <sanitized.jsonl>
Observe (requires explicit authorization for this Chrome session):
  node gnat-research/inspector/cli.cjs observe --origin https://st.saesa.cl:8082 --endpoint http://127.0.0.1:9222 --duration 180 [--target <page-id>]

Duration: 1..1800 seconds. No browser navigation or GNAT requests are generated.
Type a marker on stdin, e.g. select_pole_1, open_view_1, consult_widget_1.
Allowed markers: search/select_pole/select_equipment/open_view/consult_widget/batch/area/topology plus _1.._999.
Type stop or press Ctrl+C to stop. Output is a private bounded JSONL capture;
stdout gives incremental counts and a final value-free schema summary.
Unknown endpoint/actions have metadata only. No raw events, headers or errors.
`;

async function boundedText(stream, limit) {
  let size = 0; const chunks = [];
  for await (const chunk of stream) {
    size += chunk.length;
    if (size > limit) throw Error('input_limit');
    chunks.push(Buffer.from(chunk));
  }
  return Buffer.concat(chunks).toString('utf8');
}
async function summarize(file) {
  const summary = new Summary(); let buffer = '';
  const stream = fs.createReadStream(file, { highWaterMark: 16384, encoding: 'utf8' });
  for await (const chunk of stream) {
    buffer += chunk;
    let newline;
    while ((newline = buffer.indexOf('\n')) !== -1) {
      const line = buffer.slice(0, newline); buffer = buffer.slice(newline + 1);
      if (Buffer.byteLength(line) > 131072) throw Error('input_limit');
      if (line) { const record = parseFrame(line); if (!record) throw Error('invalid_record'); summary.add(sanitizeRecord(record)); }
    }
    if (Buffer.byteLength(buffer) > 131072) throw Error('input_limit');
  }
  if (buffer) { const record = parseFrame(buffer); if (!record) throw Error('invalid_record'); summary.add(sanitizeRecord(record)); }
  process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(summary.snapshot(), null, 2) + '\n');
}
function options(args) {
  const out = {};
  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 2) {
    const key = args[i];
    if (!['--origin', '--endpoint', '--duration', '--target'].includes(key) || !args[i + 1] || out[key]) throw Error('invalid_arguments');
    out[key] = args[i + 1];
  }
  validateOrigin(out['--origin']); const endpoint = validateEndpoint(out['--endpoint']);
  const duration = Number(out['--duration'] || 180);
  if (!Number.isInteger(duration) || duration < 1 || duration > 1800) throw Error('invalid_duration');
  if (out['--target'] && !/^[A-Za-z0-9-]{1,128}$/.test(out['--target'])) throw Error('invalid_target');
  return { origin: out['--origin'], endpoint, duration, target: out['--target'] };
}
async function observe(config) {
  // Create and verify private storage before contacting even the loopback service.
  const writer = openPrivateWriter(path.resolve(__dirname, '../private'));
  const summary = new Summary(); let protocol, collector, timer, tick, input = '';
  let finish;
  const done = new Promise(resolve => { finish = resolve; });
  const stop = () => finish();
  const onInput = chunk => {
    input += chunk.toString('utf8');
    if (input.length > 256) { input = ''; process.stderr.write('Marker rejected: input limit.\n'); return; }
    let n;
    while ((n = input.indexOf('\n')) !== -1) {
      const line = input.slice(0, n).trim(); input = input.slice(n + 1);
      if (line === 'stop') { stop(); return; }
      try { const record = sanitizeRecord({ kind: 'marker', at: Date.now(), marker: line }); writer.write(record); summary.add(record); } catch { process.stderr.write('Marker rejected.\n'); }
    }
  };
  try {
    const response = await fetch(new URL('/json/list', config.endpoint), { redirect: 'error', credentials: 'omit', signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) });
    if (!response.ok) throw Error('debug_target_unavailable');
    const targets = parseFrame(await boundedText(response.body, 65536));
    if (!Array.isArray(targets)) throw Error('debug_target_unavailable');
    const target = selectTarget(targets, config.origin, config.target);
    const socketUrl = validateEndpoint(target.webSocketDebuggerUrl, true);
    if (socketUrl.port !== config.endpoint.port) throw Error('debug_target_unavailable');
    const socket = new WebSocket(socketUrl);
    await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const timeout = setTimeout(() => { socket.close(); reject(Error('debug_connection_failed')); }, 5000);
      socket.addEventListener('open', () => { clearTimeout(timeout); resolve(); }, { once: true });
      socket.addEventListener('error', () => { clearTimeout(timeout); reject(Error('debug_connection_failed')); }, { once: true });
    });
    protocol = new Protocol(socket, event => collector.handle(event));
    socket.addEventListener('close', stop);
    collector = new Collector({ origin: config.origin, send: (method, params) => protocol.send(method, params), emit: record => {
      const safe = sanitizeRecord(record); writer.write(safe); summary.add(safe);
      if (record.kind === 'limit') stop();
    } });
    await protocol.send('Network.enable', { maxTotalBufferSize: 131072, maxResourceBufferSize: 65536, maxPostDataSize: 4096 });
    timer = setTimeout(stop, config.duration * 1000);
    tick = setInterval(() => process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify({ progress_records: summary.records }) + '\n'), 15000);
    process.on('SIGINT', stop); process.on('SIGTERM', stop);
    process.stdin.on('data', onInput); process.stdin.resume();
    process.stdout.write('Observer attached; no GNAT requests generated.\n');
    await done;
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer); clearInterval(tick);
    process.removeListener('SIGINT', stop); process.removeListener('SIGTERM', stop);
    process.stdin.removeListener('data', onInput); process.stdin.pause();
    collector?.stop();
    if (protocol && !protocol.closed) { try { await protocol.send('Network.disable'); } catch {} protocol.close(); }
    writer.close();
    process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(summary.snapshot(), null, 2) + '\n');
  }
}
async function main(args) {
  if (args.length === 1 && args[0] === '--help') { process.stdout.write(HELP); return; }
  if (args[0] === 'summary' && args.length === 2) return summarize(args[1]);
  if (args[0] === 'observe') return observe(options(args.slice(1)));
  throw Error('invalid_arguments');
}
main(process.argv.slice(2)).catch(() => { process.stderr.write('Inspector stopped: validation, privacy, input or connection check failed. No raw diagnostics retained.\n'); process.exitCode = 1; });
````

````
'use strict';
const fs = require('node:fs');
const path = require('node:path');
const { execFileSync } = require('node:child_process');
const LIMIT = 65536;
const SENSITIVE = /header|cookie|authorization|credential|password|secret|token|session|storage|email|phone|address|owner|user|person/i;
const sensitive = key => key !== 'header_widgets' && SENSITIVE.test(key);
const ENDPOINTS = {
  '/backend/saesa/svc_wmc.php': ['textual_search', 'get_objects_geojson'],
  '/backend/saesa/Web/component/svc_object_view.php': ['get_object_definition', 'get_widget_content'],
  '/backend/saesa/AM/component/svc_location.php': ['list_location_parts']
};
const NUMBERS = new Set(['id', 'object_id', 'surrogate_in_map_id', 'widget_id', 'managed_by_id', 'installed_assets', 'total', 'count', 'action_provider_id']);
const BOOLEANS = new Set(['success', 'component_has_content_generator', 'action_has_params']);
const CONTAINERS = new Set(['data', 'items', 'results', 'features', 'properties', 'geometry', 'geometries', 'widget_definition', 'header_widgets', 'tab_widgets', 'action_widgets', 'tool_widgets', 'container_children', 'pin_location', 'coords', 'crs']);
const STRINGS = new Set(['class', 'component_class', 'type', 'code', 'name', 'display', 'exposition']);
const GEOMETRIES = new Set(['FeatureCollection', 'Feature', 'Point', 'MultiPoint', 'LineString', 'MultiLineString', 'Polygon', 'MultiPolygon', 'GeometryCollection']);
const SCHEMA_KEYS = new Set([...NUMBERS, ...BOOLEANS, ...CONTAINERS, ...STRINGS, 'coordinates', 'x', 'y', 'title', 'description', 'content', 'message_error', 'class_display', 'type_display', 'component_config', 'extra', 'installed_assets', 'name']);

function parseFrame(text) {
  if (typeof text !== 'string' || Buffer.byteLength(text) > 262144) return null;
  try { return JSON.parse(text, (key, value) => sensitive(key) ? undefined : value); } catch { return null; }
}
function numeric(value) { return typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) || typeof value === 'string' && /^\d{1,16}$/.test(value); }
function coordinates(value, depth = 0) {
  if (depth > 6 || !Array.isArray(value) || value.length > 2048) return undefined;
  if (value.every(x => typeof x === 'number' && Number.isFinite(x))) return value;
  const result = value.map(x => coordinates(x, depth + 1));
  return result.every(x => x !== undefined) ? result : undefined;
}
function project(value, depth = 0) {
  if (depth > 12) return undefined;
  if (value === null || numeric(value)) return value;
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.slice(0, 200).map(x => project(x, depth + 1)).filter(x => x !== undefined);
  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object') return undefined;
  const out = {};
  for (const [key, item] of Object.entries(value).slice(0, 300)) {
    if (sensitive(key)) continue;
    if (item === null && (NUMBERS.has(key) || CONTAINERS.has(key))) out[key] = null;

    else if (BOOLEANS.has(key) && typeof item === 'boolean') out[key] = item;
    else if (key === 'coordinates') { const coords = coordinates(item); if (coords) out[key] = coords; }
    else if ((key === 'x' || key === 'y') && typeof item === 'number' && Number.isFinite(item)) out[key] = item;
    else if (STRINGS.has(key) && typeof item === 'string' && item.length <= 96) {

function schema(value, out = {}, key = '$', depth = 0, budget = { n: 0 }) {

class Collector {
````
