'use strict';
const { test } = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const fs = require('node:fs');
const os = require('node:os');
const path = require('node:path');
let api = {};
try { api = require('../inspector/core.cjs'); } catch (error) { if (error.code !== 'MODULE_NOT_FOUND') throw error; }
const origin = 'https://st.saesa.cl:8082';
const endpoint = origin + '/backend/saesa/svc_wmc.php';
const request = (id, action = 'get_objects_geojson', url = endpoint) => ({ method: 'Network.requestWillBeSent', params: { requestId: id, type: 'XHR', request: { url, method: 'POST', postData: 'action=' + action + '&object_ids=%5B101%5D', headers: { Cookie: 'SYNTHETIC_SECRET' } } } });
const response = (id, extra = {}) => ({ method: 'Network.responseReceived', params: { requestId: id, type: 'XHR', response: { url: endpoint, status: 200, mimeType: 'application/json', ...extra } } });
function setup() {
  assert.equal(typeof api.Collector, 'function', 'passive collector must exist');
  const records = [], commands = [];
  const collector = new api.Collector({ origin, emit: record => records.push(record), send: async (method, params) => { commands.push({ method, params }); return { body: JSON.stringify({ type: 'FeatureCollection', features: [{ type: 'Feature', id: 101, geometry: { type: 'Point', coordinates: [-72, -40] }, properties: { object_id: 101, class: 'elec_pole', owner_name: 'Person Private', token: 'SYNTHETIC_SECRET' } }] }) }; } });
  return { collector, records, commands };
}
test('ingress removes sensitive keys recursively and never returns raw invalid frames', () => {
  assert.equal(typeof api.parseFrame, 'function', 'privacy ingress must exist');
  assert.deepEqual(api.parseFrame('{"id":1,"result":{"headers":{"Cookie":"SYNTHETIC_SECRET"},"nested":{"access_token":"SYNTHETIC_SECRET","object_id":12}}}'), { id: 1, result: { nested: { object_id: 12 } } });
  assert.equal(api.parseFrame('SYNTHETIC_SECRET'), null);
});
test('authorized geometry preserves safe IDs/coordinates but not personal fields', async () => {
  const { collector, records, commands } = setup();
  await collector.handle(request('secret-raw-id'));
  await collector.handle(response('secret-raw-id'));
  await collector.handle({ method: 'Network.loadingFinished', params: { requestId: 'secret-raw-id', encodedDataLength: 300 } });
  assert.equal(commands.length, 1);
  assert.equal(commands[0].method, 'Network.getResponseBody');
  const capture = records.find(x => x.kind === 'body');
  assert.deepEqual(capture.data.features[0].geometry, { type: 'Point', coordinates: [-72, -40] });
  assert.equal(capture.data.features[0].properties.object_id, 101);
  assert.equal(capture.data.features[0].properties.owner_name, undefined);
  assert.doesNotMatch(JSON.stringify(records), /SYNTHETIC_SECRET|Person Private|secret-raw-id/);
  assert.equal(collector.size, 0);
});
test('off-origin redirect discards request and cannot retrieve body', async () => {
  const { collector, records, commands } = setup();
  await collector.handle(request('r'));
  await collector.handle(request('r', 'get_objects_geojson', 'https://other.invalid/private?token=SYNTHETIC_SECRET'));
  await collector.handle(response('r'));
  await collector.handle({ method: 'Network.loadingFinished', params: { requestId: 'r', encodedDataLength: 10 } });
  assert.equal(commands.length, 0);
  assert.doesNotMatch(JSON.stringify(records), /other.invalid|SYNTHETIC_SECRET/);
});
test('unknown action or endpoint records metadata only', async () => {
  for (const req of [request('r', 'delete_asset'), request('r', 'get_objects_geojson', origin + '/backend/saesa/unknown.php')]) {
    const { collector, records, commands } = setup();
    await collector.handle(req); await collector.handle(response('r'));
    await collector.handle({ method: 'Network.loadingFinished', params: { requestId: 'r', encodedDataLength: 10 } });
    assert.equal(commands.length, 0);
    assert.equal(records[0].kind, 'request');
    assert.equal(records[0].action, 'unknown');
  }
});
test('oversized, unknown MIME and non-success responses are not retrieved', async () => {
  for (const [extra, size] of [[{}, 65537], [{ mimeType: 'image/png' }, 10], [{ status: 302 }, 10], [{ url: 'https://other.invalid/' }, 10]]) {
    const { collector, commands } = setup();
    await collector.handle(request('r')); await collector.handle(response('r', extra));
    await collector.handle({ method: 'Network.loadingFinished', params: { requestId: 'r', encodedDataLength: size } });
    assert.equal(commands.length, 0);
  }
});
test('invalid JSON and oversized decoded bodies are schema-only failures', async () => {
  for (const body of ['not-json SYNTHETIC_SECRET', 'x'.repeat(65537)]) {
    const { collector, records } = setup(); collector.send = async () => ({ body });
    await collector.handle(request('r')); await collector.handle(response('r'));
    await collector.handle({ method: 'Network.loadingFinished', params: { requestId: 'r', encodedDataLength: 10 } });
    assert.equal(records.at(-1).kind, 'skipped');
    assert.doesNotMatch(JSON.stringify(records), /SYNTHETIC_SECRET|xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx/);
    assert.equal(collector.size, 0);
  }
});
test('network errors and late events are sanitized and cleanup is bounded', async () => {
  const { collector, records, commands } = setup();
  await collector.handle(request('r'));
  await collector.handle({ method: 'Network.loadingFailed', params: { requestId: 'r', errorText: 'SYNTHETIC_SECRET' } });
  await collector.handle(response('r'));
  await collector.handle({ method: 'Network.loadingFinished', params: { requestId: 'r', encodedDataLength: 1 } });
  assert.equal(commands.length, 0); assert.equal(collector.size, 0);
  assert.doesNotMatch(JSON.stringify(records), /SYNTHETIC_SECRET/);
  for (let i = 0; i < 101; i++) await collector.handle(request(String(i)));
  assert.ok(collector.size <= 100);
  collector.stop(); assert.equal(collector.size, 0);
});
test('body and marker projection cannot retain arbitrary text or HTML', () => {
  assert.equal(typeof api.project, 'function', 'business projection must exist');
  const result = api.project({ title: '<p>Private Person</p>', content: '<b>SYNTHETIC_SECRET</b>', description: 'Private Person', object_id: 101, widget_definition: { tab_widgets: [{ id: 3, component_class: 'AM.component.PartsGrid', component_has_content_generator: false }] } });
  assert.equal(result.object_id, 101);
  assert.equal(result.widget_definition.tab_widgets[0].component_class, 'AM.component.PartsGrid');
  assert.doesNotMatch(JSON.stringify(result), /Private Person|SYNTHETIC_SECRET/);
});
test('target selection rejects ambiguity and all non-loopback CDP endpoints', () => {
  assert.equal(typeof api.selectTarget, 'function', 'target validation must exist');
  const page = { id: '1', type: 'page', url: origin + '/backend/saesa/centrality.php', webSocketDebuggerUrl: 'ws://127.0.0.1:9222/devtools/page/1' };
  assert.equal(api.selectTarget([page], origin).id, '1');
  assert.throws(() => api.selectTarget([page, { ...page, id: '2' }], origin));
  assert.throws(() => api.validateEndpoint('http://remote.invalid:9222'));
  assert.throws(() => api.validateEndpoint('http://127.0.0.1:9222/?token=bad'));
  assert.throws(() => api.selectTarget([{ ...page, webSocketDebuggerUrl: 'ws://remote.invalid/devtools/page/1' }], origin));
});
test('protocol transport rejects mutation and sensitive commands before send', async () => {
  assert.equal(typeof api.Protocol, 'function', 'safe protocol transport must exist');
  const wire = { send() { throw Error('must not send'); }, close() {} };
  const protocol = new api.Protocol(wire);
  for (const method of ['Runtime.evaluate', 'Network.getAllCookies', 'Fetch.enable', 'Page.navigate', 'Network.setCacheDisabled']) await assert.rejects(protocol.send(method, {}));
  protocol.close(); assert.equal(protocol.pending.size, 0);
});
test('summary counts repeats, nulls and type variations without retaining values', () => {
  assert.equal(typeof api.Summary, 'function', 'offline summary must exist');
  const s = new api.Summary();
  s.add({ kind: 'body', endpoint: '/safe.php', action: 'a', data: { object_id: null } });
  s.add({ kind: 'body', endpoint: '/safe.php', action: 'a', data: { object_id: 1, extra: true } });
  const out = s.snapshot(); assert.equal(out.records, 2);
  assert.deepEqual(out.groups[0].schema['$.object_id'], ['null', 'number']);
  assert.equal(out.groups[0].present['$.extra'], 1);
});
test('private writer refuses symlinks and serializes only sanitized records', () => {
  assert.equal(typeof api.openPrivateWriter, 'function', 'private writer must exist');
  if (process.platform === 'win32') return; // Windows ACL integration is verified separately on this host.
  const dir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'gnat-offline-'));
  try {
    const writer = api.openPrivateWriter(path.join(dir, 'private'));
    writer.write({ kind: 'marker', marker: 'select_1', cookie: 'SYNTHETIC_SECRET' }); writer.close();
    assert.doesNotMatch(fs.readFileSync(writer.file, 'utf8'), /SYNTHETIC_SECRET/);
    assert.equal(fs.statSync(writer.file).mode & 0o077, 0);
    fs.symlinkSync(path.join(dir, 'private'), path.join(dir, 'linked'));
    assert.throws(() => api.openPrivateWriter(path.join(dir, 'linked')));
  } finally { fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
});
test('widget headers survive privacy ingress while actual HTTP headers do not', () => {
  const result = api.parseFrame('{"header_widgets":[{"id":7}],"responseHeaders":{"Cookie":"SYNTHETIC_SECRET"}}');
  assert.deepEqual(result.header_widgets, [{ id: 7 }]);
  assert.equal(result.responseHeaders, undefined);
});
test('same-origin response path mismatch cannot retrieve another endpoint body', async () => {
  const { collector, commands } = setup();
  await collector.handle(request('r'));
  await collector.handle(response('r', { url: origin + '/backend/saesa/private.php' }));
  await collector.handle({ method: 'Network.loadingFinished', params: { requestId: 'r', encodedDataLength: 10 } });
  assert.equal(commands.length, 0);
});
test('POST with query action retains safe parameters but never token values', async () => {
  const { collector, records, commands } = setup();
  const req = request('r');
  req.params.request.url = origin + '/backend/saesa/AM/component/svc_location.php?action=list_location_parts';
  req.params.request.postData = 'object_id=101&token=SYNTHETIC_SECRET';
  await collector.handle(req);
  assert.equal(records[0].action, 'list_location_parts');
  assert.deepEqual(records[0].params, { object_id: '101' });
  assert.doesNotMatch(JSON.stringify(records), /SYNTHETIC_SECRET/);
  assert.equal(commands.length, 0);
});
test('base64 response decoded limit and expired request prevent retention', async () => {
  const { collector, records, commands } = setup();
  collector.send = async () => ({ body: Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ object_id: 201 })).toString('base64'), base64Encoded: true });
  await collector.handle(request('r')); await collector.handle(response('r'));
  await collector.handle({ method: 'Network.loadingFinished', params: { requestId: 'r', encodedDataLength: 10 } });
  assert.equal(records.at(-1).data.object_id, 201);
  await collector.handle(request('late')); collector.now = () => Date.now() + 31000;
  await collector.handle({ method: 'Network.loadingFinished', params: { requestId: 'late', encodedDataLength: 10 } });
  assert.equal(collector.size, 0); assert.equal(commands.length, 0);
});
test('known JSON action labelled text/html retains only valid bounded JSON projection', async () => {
  const { collector, records, commands } = setup();
  await collector.handle(request('r'));
  await collector.handle(response('r', { mimeType: 'text/html; charset=UTF-8' }));
  await collector.handle({ method: 'Network.loadingFinished', params: { requestId: 'r', encodedDataLength: 300 } });
  assert.equal(commands.length, 1);
  assert.equal(records.at(-1).kind, 'body');
  assert.equal(records.at(-1).data.features[0].properties.object_id, 101);
  assert.doesNotMatch(JSON.stringify(records), /SYNTHETIC_SECRET|Person Private/);
});
test('text/html action containing HTML is skipped and unknown actions never retrieve it', async () => {
  const { collector, records } = setup();
  collector.send = async () => ({ body: '<html>SYNTHETIC_SECRET Private Person</html>' });
  await collector.handle(request('r'));
  await collector.handle(response('r', { mimeType: 'text/html' }));
  await collector.handle({ method: 'Network.loadingFinished', params: { requestId: 'r', encodedDataLength: 60 } });
  assert.equal(records.at(-1).kind, 'skipped');
  assert.doesNotMatch(JSON.stringify(records), /SYNTHETIC_SECRET|Private Person/);
  const unknown = setup();
  await unknown.collector.handle(request('u', 'unknown_action'));
  await unknown.collector.handle(response('u', { mimeType: 'text/html' }));
  await unknown.collector.handle({ method: 'Network.loadingFinished', params: { requestId: 'u', encodedDataLength: 10 } });
  assert.equal(unknown.commands.length, 0);
});
test('scalar ID arrays preserve IDs without manufacturing nulls from dropped values', () => {
  const result = api.project({ header_widgets: [7, '8', null, 'Private Person', { id: 9 }] });
  assert.deepEqual(result.header_widgets, [7, '8', null, { id: 9 }]);
  assert.equal(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(result)).header_widgets.filter(x => x === null).length, 1);
});
test('schema sanitation retains recognized union types while dropping unsupported types', () => {
  const result = api.sanitizeRecord({ kind: 'body', endpoint: '/backend/saesa/svc_wmc.php', action: 'get_objects_geojson', schema: { '$.object_id': ['null', 'number', 'undefined'], '$.extra': ['undefined'] } });
  assert.deepEqual(result.schema['$.object_id'], ['null', 'number']);
  assert.equal(result.schema['$.extra'], undefined);
});
test('duplicate or conflicting action parameters cannot authorize body retrieval', async () => {
  for (const [query, form] of [
    ['?action=get_objects_geojson&action=delete_asset', 'object_ids=%5B101%5D'],
    ['', 'action=get_objects_geojson&action=delete_asset'],
    ['?action=delete_asset', 'action=get_objects_geojson'],
    ['?action=get_objects_geojson', 'action=get_objects_geojson']
  ]) {
    const { collector, commands } = setup();
    const req = request('r'); req.params.request.url += query; req.params.request.postData = form;
    await collector.handle(req); await collector.handle(response('r', { url: endpoint + query }));
    await collector.handle({ method: 'Network.loadingFinished', params: { requestId: 'r', encodedDataLength: 10 } });
    assert.equal(commands.length, 0);
  }
});
test('changed response query cannot inherit original request read authorization', async () => {
  for (const query of ['?action=delete_asset', '?action=get_objects_geojson', '?object_id=202', '?unverified=1', '?action=get_objects_geojson&action=delete_asset']) {
    const { collector, commands } = setup();
    await collector.handle(request('r'));
    await collector.handle(response('r', { url: endpoint + query }));
    await collector.handle({ method: 'Network.loadingFinished', params: { requestId: 'r', encodedDataLength: 10 } });
    assert.equal(commands.length, 0);
  }
});

'use strict';
const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const fs = require('node:fs');
const path = require('node:path');
const { project, schema } = require('../inspector/core.cjs');
const filename = path.join(__dirname, '../fixtures/contracts.observed.anonymized.json');
function evidence() {
  assert.ok(fs.existsSync(filename), 'anonymized observed contract catalog must exist');
  return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(filename, 'utf8'));
}

test('observed singleton and two-ID geometry retain independent IDs and coordinate nesting', () => {
  const f = evidence();
  assert.equal(f.provenance.class, 'transformed_observed_response');
  const single = project(f.point);
  const batch = project(f.batch);
  assert.equal(single.data.type, 'Feature');
  assert.equal(single.data.id, 1001);
  assert.deepEqual(single.data.geometry.coordinates, [-10.915995049, -18.932875695]);
  assert.equal(batch.data.type, 'FeatureCollection');
  const byId = new Map(batch.data.features.slice().reverse().map(x => [String(x.id), x]));
  assert.deepEqual([...byId.keys()].sort(), ['1002', '1003']);
  assert.deepEqual(byId.get('1003').geometry.coordinates, [-9.999965762, -20.000055628]);
  assert.equal(byId.get('1002').geometry.type, 'Point');
});

test('observed line stays a LineString and retained source bbox is not replaced by vertex extent', () => {
  const f = evidence();
  assert.deepEqual(project(f.line).data.geometry, {
    type: 'LineString', coordinates: [[-9.999931577, -20.000079709], [-9.999151261, -20.000611599]]
  });
  assert.ok(f.line.data.bbox[1] < Math.min(...f.line.data.geometry.coordinates.map(x => x[1])));
  assert.ok(f.line.data.bbox[3] > Math.max(...f.line.data.geometry.coordinates.map(x => x[1])));
  assert.equal(Object.hasOwn(project(f.line).data, 'bbox'), false, 'observer currently omits bbox');
});

test('observed wrappers and content variants survive schema discovery without inventing a success field', () => {
  const f = evidence();
  assert.equal(Object.hasOwn(f.parts, 'success'), false);
  assert.equal(project(f.parts).total, 2);
  assert.equal(project(f.definition).success, true);
  const types = schema(f.widgetContent);
  assert.deepEqual(types['$.data[].content'], ['array', 'null', 'string']);
  assert.equal(f.externalIds.success, true);
  assert.equal(Object.hasOwn(f.externalIds, 'total'), false);
  assert.equal(f.definition.data.widget_definition.tab_widgets[0].exposition, 'read-only');
  assert.equal(f.externalIds.data[0].read_only, false, 'row flag is separate from read-only tab');
});
````

'use strict';
const { test } = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const fs = require('node:fs');
const path = require('node:path');
const { spawnSync } = require('node:child_process');
const { EventEmitter } = require('node:events');
const api = require('../inspector/core.cjs');
const cli = path.resolve(__dirname, '../inspector/cli.cjs');
// A fake CDP peer avoids attaching any real browser during development.
class Peer extends EventEmitter {
  addEventListener(name, fn) { this.on(name, fn); }
  send(text) {
    const { id, method } = JSON.parse(text);
    if (method === 'Network.getResponseBody') queueMicrotask(() => this.emit('message', { data: JSON.stringify({ id, result: { body: '{"object_id":101,"headers":{"Cookie":"SYNTHETIC_SECRET"}}' } }) }));
    else queueMicrotask(() => this.emit('message', { data: JSON.stringify({ id, result: {} }) }));
  }
  close() { this.emit('close'); }
}
test('fake CDP response resolves safely and disconnect rejects outstanding work', async () => {
  const peer = new Peer(); const protocol = new api.Protocol(peer);
  const result = await protocol.send('Network.getResponseBody', { requestId: 'r' });
  assert.equal(api.parseFrame(result.body).object_id, 101);
  assert.equal(api.parseFrame(result.body).headers, undefined);
  peer.send = () => {};
  const waiting = protocol.send('Network.getResponseBody', { requestId: 'late' });
  protocol.close(); await assert.rejects(waiting, /cdp_closed/);
  peer.emit('message', { data: '{"id":2,"result":{"body":"SYNTHETIC_SECRET"}}' });
  assert.equal(protocol.pending.size, 0);
});
test('private JSONL rejects arbitrary record values on the actual host', () => {
  const root = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(__dirname, 'private-test-'));
  try {
    const writer = api.openPrivateWriter(path.join(root, 'private'));
    writer.write({ kind: 'marker', marker: 'select_pole_1', cookie: 'SYNTHETIC_SECRET', extra: 'Private Person' });
    writer.write({ kind: 'body', endpoint: '/untrusted/PrivatePerson', action: 'SYNTHETIC_SECRET', data: { object_id: 101, title: 'Private Person', content: '<b>Private Person</b>' } });
    writer.close();
    const text = fs.readFileSync(writer.file, 'utf8');
    assert.doesNotMatch(text, /SYNTHETIC_SECRET|Private Person|PrivatePerson/);
    const records = text.trim().split('\n').map(JSON.parse);
    assert.equal(records[0].marker, 'select_pole_1');
    assert.equal(records[1].data.object_id, 101);
  } finally { fs.rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
});
test('CLI help is offline and unsafe command errors do not echo arguments', () => {
  const help = spawnSync(process.execPath, [cli, '--help'], { encoding: 'utf8' });
  assert.equal(help.status, 0); assert.match(help.stdout, /observe/); assert.match(help.stdout, /summary/);
  const bad = spawnSync(process.execPath, [cli, 'observe', '--origin', 'https://private.invalid/?token=SYNTHETIC_SECRET'], { encoding: 'utf8' });
  assert.equal(bad.status, 1); assert.doesNotMatch(bad.stdout + bad.stderr, /SYNTHETIC_SECRET|private.invalid/);
});
test('offline fixture summary preserves schema and rejects oversized lines', () => {
  const root = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(__dirname, 'summary-test-'));
  try {
    const file = path.join(root, 'synthetic.jsonl');
    fs.writeFileSync(file, JSON.stringify({ kind: 'body', endpoint: '/backend/saesa/svc_wmc.php', action: 'get_objects_geojson', data: JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.resolve(__dirname, '../fixtures/geojson.synthetic.json'), 'utf8')) }) + '\n');
    const run = spawnSync(process.execPath, [cli, 'summary', file], { encoding: 'utf8' });
    assert.equal(run.status, 0); assert.match(run.stdout, /coordinates/); assert.doesNotMatch(run.stdout, /-72|-40/);
    fs.writeFileSync(file, 'x'.repeat(131073));
    const large = spawnSync(process.execPath, [cli, 'summary', file], { encoding: 'utf8' });
    assert.equal(large.status, 1); assert.ok(large.stderr.length < 200);
  } finally { fs.rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
});

````
"""Iniciar visor local. No instala paquetes, no pide credenciales."""
import threading
import time
import webbrowser
from app.server import serve, PORT

def open_later():
    time.sleep(0.8)
    webbrowser.open(f'http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}')

if __name__=='__main__':
    threading.Thread(target=open_later,daemon=True).start()
    serve()
````

/*
 * CENTRALITY - PUNTOS KMZ/KML POR BLOQUES (VERDE / NARANJO / AZUL)
 * Ejecutar en la consola del navegador con el mapa de Centrality abierto.
 * Lee archivos locales. No envia archivos ni cambia datos de Centrality.
 * Los colores son temporales; puedes exportar/importar el reparto como CSV.
 */
(() => {
  "use strict";

  const frame = document.querySelector("iframe#content");
  if (!frame) {
    console.error("Abre primero el modulo Mapas de Centrality.");
    return;
  }

  let w, map, ol, doc;
  try {
    w = frame.contentWindow;
    map = w.webMap_?.map;
    ol = w.ol;
    doc = w.document;
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("No es posible acceder al mapa de Centrality:", e);
    return;
  }

  if (!map || !ol?.layer?.Vector || !ol?.source?.Vector ||
      !ol?.format?.KML || !ol?.Feature || !ol?.geom?.Point ||
      !ol?.style?.Style || !ol?.style?.Circle ||
      !ol?.style?.Text || !ol?.style?.Fill || !ol?.style?.Stroke) {
    console.error("El mapa no expone los componentes necesarios de OpenLayers.");
    return;
  }

  try { w.__superponerKMZ?.cerrar?.(); } catch (e) {
    console.warn("No se pudo retirar el panel anterior:", e);
  }

  const COLORES = Object.freeze({
    Verde: "#198754",
    Naranjo: "#ef8b16",
    Azul: "#2469d8"
  });
  const BLOQUES = Object.keys(COLORES);
  let puntos = [];
  let capa = null;
  let seleccionado = new Set();
  let enfocado = -1;
  let etiquetas = "cerca";
  let cargaId = 0;
  let abierto = true;
  let nombreArchivo = "puntos";
  let textoAviso = "";
  const cacheEstilos = new WeakMap();

  // Una sola interfaz, dentro del iframe que contiene el mapa.
  const panel = doc.createElement("div");
  panel.style.cssText = [
    "position:fixed", "top:12px", "right:12px",
    "z-index:2147483647", "width:450px",
    "max-width:calc(100vw - 24px)", "max-height:calc(100vh - 24px)",
    "overflow:auto", "box-sizing:border-box", "padding:14px",
    "background:#fff", "color:#17212b", "font:13px Arial,sans-serif",
    "border:1px solid #bdc7d0", "border-radius:10px",
    "box-shadow:0 7px 30px #0004"
  ].join(";");

  const crear = (padre, tag, texto, css) => {
    const el = doc.createElement(tag);
    if (texto !== undefined && texto !== null) el.textContent = texto;
    if (css) el.style.cssText = css;
    padre.appendChild(el);
    return el;
  };

  const ESTILO_CONTROL =
    "font:12px Arial,sans-serif;box-sizing:border-box;border:1px solid #abb6c1;" +
    "border-radius:5px;padding:7px;background:#fff;color:#17212b;";
  const ESTILO_BOTON = ESTILO_CONTROL + "cursor:pointer;background:#f5f7fa;";
  const btn = (padre, titulo, cssExtra = "") => {
    const el = crear(padre, "button", titulo, ESTILO_BOTON + cssExtra);
    el.type = "button";
    return el;
  };
  const fila = (padre, cssExtra = "") =>
    crear(padre, "div", null, "display:flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;flex-wrap:wrap;" + cssExtra);
  const titulo = fila(panel, "justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:8px;");
  const tituloTexto = crear(titulo, "strong", "Puntos KMZ · Supervisores", "font-size:15px;");
  const ocultar = btn(titulo, "Contraer", "padding:5px 8px;");

  crear(panel, "div", "Importa tu archivo KMZ o KML:", "margin-bottom:5px;");
  const archivo = crear(panel, "input", null,
    "display:block;width:100%;margin:4px 0 7px 0;font-size:12px;");
  archivo.type = "file";
  archivo.accept = ".kmz,.kml";

  const estado = crear(panel, "div", "Esperando archivo...",
    "margin:7px 0;color:#314355;font-size:12px;");

  const controles = crear(panel, "div");
  const busqueda = crear(controles, "input", null,
    ESTILO_CONTROL + "display:block;width:100%;margin:8px 0 6px 0;");
  busqueda.type = "search";
  busqueda.placeholder = "Buscar N° o código de poste...";
  busqueda.setAttribute("aria-label", "Buscar punto por numero o codigo");
  const barraFiltros = fila(controles, "margin:0 0 7px 0;");
  const bloqueFiltro = crear(barraFiltros, "select", null,
    ESTILO_CONTROL + "flex:1;min-width:100px;");
  ["Todos los bloques", ...BLOQUES].forEach((v, i) => {
    const opt = crear(bloqueFiltro, "option", v);
    opt.value = i ? v : "";
  });
  const modoEtiqueta = crear(barraFiltros, "select", null,
    ESTILO_CONTROL + "flex:1.5;min-width:160px;");
  [
    ["cerca", "Etiquetas al acercar"],
    ["siempre", "Etiquetas siempre"],
    ["ninguna", "Ocultar etiquetas"]
  ].forEach(([v, s]) => {
    const opt = crear(modoEtiqueta, "option", s);
    opt.value = v;
  });
  modoEtiqueta.title = "Las etiquetas muestran N° + código de poste.";

  const lista = crear(controles, "select", null,
    ESTILO_CONTROL + "display:block;width:100%;margin:5px 0 8px 0;");
  lista.setAttribute("aria-label", "Ir a un punto");

  const resumen = crear(controles, "div", "",
    "font-size:12px;margin:7px 0;line-height:1.6;");

  const agrupacion = crear(controles, "div", null,
    "background:#f3f6f9;border:1px solid #dbe3ea;border-radius:7px;" +
    "padding:9px;margin:8px 0;");
  crear(agrupacion, "strong", "Asignar bloque a los seleccionados",
    "display:block;font-size:12px;margin-bottom:7px;");
  const coloresFila = fila(agrupacion);
  const botonesColores = {};
  for (const bloque of BLOQUES) {
    const b = btn(coloresFila, bloque,
      "background:" + COLORES[bloque] + ";border-color:" + COLORES[bloque] +
      ";color:white;font-weight:bold;flex:1;min-width:80px;");
    botonesColores[bloque] = b;
  }
  const filaSeleccion = fila(agrupacion, "margin-top:7px;justify-content:space-between;");
  const nSel = crear(filaSeleccion, "span", "0 seleccionados", "font-size:12px;");
  const limpiarSel = btn(filaSeleccion, "Deseleccionar", "padding:5px 8px;");

  const tablaWrap = crear(controles, "div", null,
    "border:1px solid #ccd5df;border-radius:6px;overflow:auto;" +
    "max-height:min(38vh,350px);background:#fff;");
  const tabla = crear(tablaWrap, "table", null,
    "width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;table-layout:fixed;font:12px Arial,sans-serif;");
  const thead = crear(tabla, "thead");
  const trh = crear(thead, "tr", null, "background:#eaf0f5;");
  const th = (texto, ancho) => crear(trh, "th", texto,
    "position:sticky;top:0;z-index:2;background:#eaf0f5;" +
    "padding:7px 4px;text-align:left;border-bottom:1px solid #c4cdd8;" +
    (ancho ? "width:" + ancho + ";" : ""));
  const celdaCheck = th("", "31px");
  const checkTodo = crear(celdaCheck, "input");
  checkTodo.type = "checkbox";
  checkTodo.title = "Seleccionar todos los puntos visibles en la tabla";
  th("N°", "36px");
  th("Código de poste", "auto");
  th("Bloque", "100px");
  const tbody = crear(tabla, "tbody");
  const pieTabla = crear(controles, "div", "",
    "font-size:11px;color:#566575;margin:5px 0 9px 0;");

  const acciones = fila(panel, "margin-top:10px;");
  const verTodos = btn(acciones, "Ver todos", "flex:1;");
  const verSeleccion = btn(acciones, "Ver selección", "flex:1;");
  const quitar = btn(acciones, "Quitar puntos", "flex:1;");
  const acciones2 = fila(panel, "margin-top:7px;");
  const exportar = btn(acciones2, "Guardar reparto CSV", "flex:1;");
  const importar = btn(acciones2, "Recuperar CSV", "flex:1;");
  const cerrar = btn(acciones2, "Cerrar", "flex:0.6;");
  const archivoCSV = crear(panel, "input", null, "display:none;");
  archivoCSV.type = "file";
  archivoCSV.accept = ".csv,text/csv";
  crear(panel, "div", "El reparto se mantiene en esta sesión. Usa CSV para guardarlo.",
    "font-size:11px;color:#657181;margin-top:8px;");
  doc.body.appendChild(panel);

  function mensaje(s, error = false) {
    textoAviso = s;
    estado.textContent = s;
    estado.style.color = error ? "#b42318" : "#314355";
  }

  // Mantener el nombre original para no invalidar los CSV ya guardados.
  function puntoCodigoOriginal(f) {
    return String(f.get("codigo") || "Sin código");
  }

  // Si el KML trae "15 - F51755", no mostrar dos veces la numeración.
  function puntoCodigo(f) {
    const codigo = puntoCodigoOriginal(f).trim();
    const numero = String(f.get("numero"));
    const prefijo = new RegExp("^" + numero + "(?:\\s*[-–—·.:#/]\\s*|\\s+)");
    return codigo.replace(prefijo, "").trim() || codigo;
  }

  function puntosFiltrados() {
    const q = busqueda.value.trim().toLocaleLowerCase("es");
    const grupo = bloqueFiltro.value;
    return puntos.filter(f => {
      const coincideGrupo = !grupo || f.get("bloque") === grupo;
      const valor = String(f.get("numero")) + " " + puntoCodigo(f);
      return coincideGrupo && valor.toLocaleLowerCase("es").includes(q);
    });
  }

  function estiloPunto(f, resolution) {
    const bloque = BLOQUES.includes(f.get("bloque")) ? f.get("bloque") : "Azul";
    const activo = enfocado === Number(f.get("numero")) - 1;
    const marcado = seleccionado.has(Number(f.get("numero")) - 1);
    const conTexto = etiquetas === "siempre" || (etiquetas === "cerca" && resolution < 42);
    const clave = [bloque, activo, marcado, conTexto].join("|");
    let cache = cacheEstilos.get(f);
    if (!cache) {
      cache = new Map();
      cacheEstilos.set(f, cache);
    }
    if (cache.has(clave)) return cache.get(clave);
    const estilo = new ol.style.Style({
      image: new ol.style.Circle({
        radius: activo ? 10 : (marcado ? 8.5 : 7),
        fill: new ol.style.Fill({ color: COLORES[bloque] }),
        stroke: new ol.style.Stroke({
          color: activo ? "#17212b" : "#ffffff",
          width: activo ? 3 : marcado ? 3 : 2
        })
      }),
      text: conTexto ? new ol.style.Text({
        text: f.get("numero") + " · " + puntoCodigo(f),
        font: "bold 12px Arial,sans-serif",
        offsetY: activo ? -23 : -20,
        fill: new ol.style.Fill({color: "#ffffff"}),
        stroke: new ol.style.Stroke({color: "#142332", width: 3.5})
      }) : undefined,
      zIndex: activo ? 100 : marcado ? 50 : 0
    });
    cache.set(clave, estilo);
    return estilo;
  }

  function redibujarMapa() {
    if (capa) capa.changed();
  }

  function mostrarResumen() {
    const cuentas = Object.fromEntries(BLOQUES.map(b => [b, 0]));
    puntos.forEach(f => { cuentas[f.get("bloque")]++; });
    resumen.replaceChildren();
    crear(resumen, "span", `${puntos.length} puntos  ·  `);
    BLOQUES.forEach((b, i) => {
      const dato = crear(resumen, "strong", `${b}: ${cuentas[b]}`,
        `color:${COLORES[b]};`);
      if (i < BLOQUES.length - 1) dato.after(doc.createTextNode("  ·  "));
    });
    nSel.textContent = seleccionado.size + " seleccionados";
  }

  function reconstruirLista() {
    const actual = lista.value;
    lista.replaceChildren();
    const inicial = crear(lista, "option", "Ir a un punto...");
    inicial.value = "";
    puntos.forEach((f, i) => {
      const opt = crear(lista, "option", `${f.get("numero")} · ${puntoCodigo(f)}`);
      opt.value = String(i);
    });
    lista.value = (actual !== "" && Number(actual) < puntos.length) ? actual : "";
  }

  // Reserva un espacio visual a la derecha para que el panel NO tape el poste.
  function margenDerechoMapa() {
    const tamano = map.getSize();
    const mapaEl = map.getTargetElement?.();
    if (!tamano?.[0] || !mapaEl) return 0;
    const m = mapaEl.getBoundingClientRect();
    const p = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
    const anchoSolapado = Math.max(0,
      Math.min(m.right, p.right) - Math.max(m.left, p.left));
    const panelALaDerecha = (p.left + p.right) / 2 > (m.left + m.right) / 2;
    return panelALaDerecha
      ? Math.min(anchoSolapado + 16, Math.max(0, tamano[0] - 130))
      : 0;
  }

  function navegar(indice) {
    const f = puntos[indice];
    if (!f) return;
    enfocado = indice;
    lista.value = String(indice);
    const view = map.getView();
    const actual = view.getZoom();
    const zoom = Math.max(Number.isFinite(actual) ? actual : 0, 17.5);
    const xy = f.getGeometry().getCoordinates();
    const res = view.getResolutionForZoom?.(zoom);
    const margen = margenDerechoMapa();
    const centro = Number.isFinite(res) && margen
      ? [xy[0] + margen * res / 2, xy[1]]
      : xy;
    view.animate({ center: centro, zoom, duration: 350 });
    redibujarMapa();
    dibujarTabla();
  }

  function dibujarTabla() {
    const visibles = puntosFiltrados();
    tbody.replaceChildren();
    for (const f of visibles) {
      const indice = Number(f.get("numero")) - 1;
      const grupo = f.get("bloque");
      const tr = crear(tbody, "tr", null,
        "cursor:pointer;border-bottom:1px solid #e5e9ee;" +
        (indice === enfocado ? "background:#e8f0fb;" : "") +
        `border-left:4px solid ${COLORES[grupo]};`);
      tr.title = "Ir a este punto en el mapa";
      const td = (texto, css = "") => crear(tr, "td", texto,
        "padding:6px 4px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;" + css);
      const tCheck = td("");
      const check = crear(tCheck, "input");
      check.type = "checkbox";
      check.checked = seleccionado.has(indice);
      check.setAttribute("aria-label", `Seleccionar punto ${f.get("numero")}`);
      check.addEventListener("click", e => e.stopPropagation());
      check.addEventListener("change", () => {
        if (check.checked) seleccionado.add(indice);
        else seleccionado.delete(indice);
        refrescarSeleccion();
      });
      td(String(f.get("numero")), "font-weight:bold;");
      td(puntoCodigo(f), "font-weight:600;word-break:break-word;");
      const tBloque = td("");
      const s = crear(tBloque, "select", null,
        "font:11px Arial,sans-serif;width:100%;max-width:100%;" +
        "padding:5px 2px;border:1px solid #c4cdd8;border-radius:4px;");
      BLOQUES.forEach(b => {
        const op = crear(s, "option", b);
        op.value = b;
      });
      s.value = grupo;
      s.style.color = COLORES[grupo];
      s.style.fontWeight = "bold";
      s.title = "Cambiar solo este punto de bloque";
      s.addEventListener("click", e => e.stopPropagation());
      s.addEventListener("change", () => {
        asignar([indice], s.value);
      });
      tr.addEventListener("click", () => navegar(indice));
    }
    const n = visibles.length;
    pieTabla.textContent = `Mostrando ${n} de ${puntos.length} puntos. ` +
      "Marca casillas para asignar varios; pulsa una fila para ir al mapa.";
    const todos = n > 0 && visibles.every(f =>
      seleccionado.has(Number(f.get("numero")) - 1));
    const algunos = visibles.some(f => seleccionado.has(Number(f.get("numero")) - 1));
    checkTodo.checked = todos;
    checkTodo.indeterminate = algunos && !todos;
    mostrarResumen();
  }

  function refrescarSeleccion() {
    const visibles = puntosFiltrados();
    checkTodo.checked = visibles.length > 0 && visibles.every(f =>
      seleccionado.has(Number(f.get("numero")) - 1));
    checkTodo.indeterminate = !checkTodo.checked && visibles.some(f =>
      seleccionado.has(Number(f.get("numero")) - 1));
    nSel.textContent = seleccionado.size + " seleccionados";
    redibujarMapa();
  }

  function asignar(indices, bloque) {
    if (!BLOQUES.includes(bloque)) return;
    if (!indices.length) {
      mensaje("Primero selecciona uno o más puntos en la tabla.", true);
      return;
    }
    for (const i of indices) {
      if (puntos[i]) puntos[i].set("bloque", bloque);
    }
    seleccionado.clear();
    dibujarTabla();
    redibujarMapa();
    mensaje(`${indices.length} punto(s) asignado(s) al bloque ${bloque}.`);
  }

  function retirarCapa() {
    if (capa) {
      map.removeLayer(capa);
      capa = null;
    }
    puntos = [];
    seleccionado.clear();
    enfocado = -1;
    busqueda.value = "";
    bloqueFiltro.value = "";
    lista.replaceChildren();
    const op = crear(lista, "option", "Ir a un punto...");
    op.value = "";
    dibujarTabla();
  }

  // Lee el KML contenido en un KMZ (ZIP) sin llamar a servidores.
  async function leerKMZ(file) {
    const ab = await file.arrayBuffer();
    if (ab.byteLength > 30000000) throw Error("KMZ demasiado grande (>30 MB).");
    const v = new DataView(ab);
    let end = -1;
    for (let i = ab.byteLength - 22; i >= Math.max(0, ab.byteLength - 65557); --i) {
      if (v.getUint32(i, true) === 0x06054b50) { end = i; break; }
    }
    if (end < 0) throw Error("No se encontró el índice ZIP.");
    const total = v.getUint16(end + 10, true);
    let p = v.getUint32(end + 16, true);
    const encontrados = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < total; i++) {
      if (p + 46 > ab.byteLength || v.getUint32(p, true) !== 0x02014b50) {
        throw Error("Índice ZIP inválido.");
      }
      const metodo = v.getUint16(p + 10, true);
      const comprimido = v.getUint32(p + 20, true);
      const descomprimido = v.getUint32(p + 24, true);
      const nl = v.getUint16(p + 28, true);
      const el = v.getUint16(p + 30, true);
      const cl = v.getUint16(p + 32, true);
      const loc = v.getUint32(p + 42, true);
      const nombre = new TextDecoder().decode(new Uint8Array(ab, p + 46, nl));
      if (nombre.toLowerCase().endsWith(".kml") &&
          !nombre.startsWith("__MACOSX/")) {
        encontrados.push({ nombre, metodo, comprimido, descomprimido, loc });
      }
      p += 46 + nl + el + cl;
    }
    encontrados.sort((a, b) =>
      Number(b.nombre.toLowerCase().endsWith("doc.kml")) -
      Number(a.nombre.toLowerCase().endsWith("doc.kml")));
    const a = encontrados[0];
    if (!a) throw Error("El KMZ no contiene archivos KML.");
    if (a.descomprimido > 30000000) throw Error("KML interno demasiado grande.");
    if (a.loc + 30 > ab.byteLength || v.getUint32(a.loc, true) !== 0x04034b50) {
      throw Error("Entrada ZIP inválida.");
    }
    const inicio = a.loc + 30 + v.getUint16(a.loc + 26, true) +
      v.getUint16(a.loc + 28, true);
    if (inicio + a.comprimido > ab.byteLength) throw Error("Entrada ZIP incompleta.");
    const bytes = new Uint8Array(ab, inicio, a.comprimido);
    if (a.metodo === 0) return new TextDecoder().decode(bytes);
    if (a.metodo !== 8) throw Error("Compresión ZIP no admitida.");
    if (typeof DecompressionStream !== "function") {
      throw Error("Tu navegador no permite descomprimir KMZ; prueba con KML.");
    }
    const flujo = new Blob([bytes]).stream().pipeThrough(
      new DecompressionStream("deflate-raw"));
    const salida = await new Response(flujo).arrayBuffer();
    if (salida.byteLength > 30000000) {
      throw Error("KML descomprimido demasiado grande.");
    }
    return new TextDecoder().decode(salida);
  }

  function extraerPuntos(g) {
    if (!g) return [];
    const tipo = g.getType();
    if (tipo === "Point") return [g.getCoordinates()];
    if (tipo === "MultiPoint") return g.getCoordinates();
    if (tipo === "GeometryCollection") return g.getGeometries().flatMap(extraerPuntos);
    return [];
  }

  async function cargar(file) {
    if (!file) return;
    const token = ++cargaId;
    mensaje("Leyendo " + file.name + "...");
    try {
      const kml = /\.kmz$/i.test(file.name) ? await leerKMZ(file) : await file.text();
      if (!abierto || token !== cargaId) return;
      const xml = new DOMParser().parseFromString(kml, "application/xml");
      if (xml.querySelector("parsererror")) throw Error("El KML no es XML válido.");
      const formato = new ol.format.KML({ extractStyles: false, showPointNames: false });
      const entidades = formato.readFeatures(kml, {
        dataProjection: "EPSG:4326",
        featureProjection: map.getView().getProjection()
      });
      const nuevos = [];
      for (const original of entidades) {
        const codigo = String(original.get("name") || "").trim();
        for (const xy of extraerPuntos(original.getGeometry())) {
          if (!xy.every(Number.isFinite)) continue;
          const punto = new ol.Feature({
            geometry: new ol.geom.Point(xy),
            codigo: codigo || "Sin código",
            numero: nuevos.length + 1,
            bloque: "Azul"
          });
          nuevos.push(punto);
        }
      }
      if (!nuevos.length) throw Error("El archivo no contiene puntos.");
      const nuevaCapa = new ol.layer.Vector({
        source: new ol.source.Vector({features: nuevos}),
        zIndex: 100000,
        style: estiloPunto
      });
      retirarCapa();
      puntos = nuevos;
      capa = nuevaCapa;
      nombreArchivo = file.name.replace(/\.(kmz|kml)$/i, "");
      map.addLayer(capa);
      reconstruirLista();
      dibujarTabla();
      mensaje(`Cargados ${puntos.length} puntos. Todos comienzan en Azul; ` +
        "selecciona los demás para repartirlos.");
      console.log("Centrality: puntos cargados", puntos.length);
    } catch (e) {
      if (abierto && token === cargaId) {
        mensaje("Error: " + e.message, true);
        console.error("Centrality: error al cargar archivo", e);
      }
    }
  }

  function encuadrar(indices) {
    if (!capa) { mensaje("Carga un KMZ o KML primero.", true); return; }
    if (!indices.length) { mensaje("Selecciona al menos un punto.", true); return; }
    if (indices.length === 1) { navegar(indices[0]); return; }
    const coords = indices.map(i => puntos[i]?.getGeometry().getCoordinates()).filter(Boolean);
    if (!coords.length) return;
    const xs = coords.map(c => c[0]);
    const ys = coords.map(c => c[1]);
    const extent = [Math.min(...xs), Math.min(...ys), Math.max(...xs), Math.max(...ys)];
    map.getView().fit(extent, {
      size: map.getSize(),
      padding: [55, margenDerechoMapa() + 20, 55, 45],
      maxZoom: 17.5, duration: 350
    });
  }

  // CSV con separador ; para abrir cómodamente en Excel en Chile.
  const entreComillas = v => '"' + String(v).replace(/"/g, '""') + '"';
  function guardarCSV() {
    if (!puntos.length) { mensaje("Carga puntos antes de exportar.", true); return; }
    const lineas = ["Numero;CodigoPoste;Bloque;Longitud;Latitud"];
    for (const f of puntos) {
      const xy = f.getGeometry().getCoordinates();
      let lng = "", lat = "";
      if (ol.proj?.toLonLat) {
        [lng, lat] = ol.proj.toLonLat(xy, map.getView().getProjection());
      }
      lineas.push([
        f.get("numero"), entreComillas(puntoCodigoOriginal(f)), f.get("bloque"), lng, lat
      ].join(";"));
    }
    const blob = new Blob(["\ufeff" + lineas.join("\r\n")],
      {type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8"});
    const url = w.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = doc.createElement("a");
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `${nombreArchivo}_reparto_supervisores.csv`;
    doc.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    a.remove();
    w.setTimeout(() => w.URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 2000);
    mensaje("CSV guardado localmente con el reparto actual.");
  }

  function leerFilaCSV(linea) {
    const partes = [];
    let valor = "", comillas = false;
    for (let i = 0; i < linea.length; i++) {
      const c = linea[i];
      if (c === '"') {
        if (comillas && linea[i + 1] === '"') { valor += '"'; i++; }
        else comillas = !comillas;
      } else if (c === ";" && !comillas) {
        partes.push(valor);
        valor = "";
      } else valor += c;
    }
    if (comillas) throw Error("CSV con comillas incompletas.");
    partes.push(valor);
    return partes;
  }

  async function recuperarCSV(file) {
    if (!file || !puntos.length) {
      if (!puntos.length) mensaje("Carga primero el KMZ/KML original.", true);
      return;
    }
    try {
      const texto = (await file.text()).replace(/^\ufeff/, "");
      const lineas = texto.split(/\r?\n/).filter(x => x.trim() !== "");
      if (lineas.length < 2 ||
          leerFilaCSV(lineas[0]).slice(0, 3).join(";") !==
          "Numero;CodigoPoste;Bloque") {
        throw Error("Selecciona un CSV exportado por este panel.");
      }
      const cambios = new Map();
      for (const linea of lineas.slice(1)) {
        const [n, codigo, bloque] = leerFilaCSV(linea);
        const i = Number(n) - 1;
        if (!Number.isInteger(i) || !puntos[i] ||
            puntoCodigoOriginal(puntos[i]) !== codigo || !BLOQUES.includes(bloque) ||
            cambios.has(i)) {
          throw Error("El CSV no corresponde a los mismos puntos y orden del KMZ/KML.");
        }
        cambios.set(i, bloque);
      }
      if (cambios.size !== puntos.length) {
        throw Error("La cantidad de puntos del CSV no coincide con el archivo cargado.");
      }
      for (const [i, bloque] of cambios) puntos[i].set("bloque", bloque);
      seleccionado.clear();
      dibujarTabla();
      redibujarMapa();
      mensaje(`Recuperado reparto de ${cambios.size} puntos desde CSV.`);
    } catch (e) {
      mensaje("Error al recuperar CSV: " + e.message, true);
    } finally {
      archivoCSV.value = "";
    }
  }

  archivo.addEventListener("change", () => cargar(archivo.files?.[0]));
  busqueda.addEventListener("input", dibujarTabla);
  bloqueFiltro.addEventListener("change", dibujarTabla);
  modoEtiqueta.addEventListener("change", () => {
    etiquetas = modoEtiqueta.value;
    redibujarMapa();
  });
  lista.addEventListener("change", () => {
    if (lista.value !== "") navegar(Number(lista.value));
  });
  checkTodo.addEventListener("change", () => {
    const visibles = puntosFiltrados();
    const marcar = checkTodo.checked;
    for (const f of visibles) {
      const i = Number(f.get("numero")) - 1;
      if (marcar) seleccionado.add(i);
      else seleccionado.delete(i);
    }
    dibujarTabla();
    redibujarMapa();
  });
  limpiarSel.addEventListener("click", () => {
    seleccionado.clear(); dibujarTabla(); redibujarMapa();
  });
  for (const bloque of BLOQUES) {
    botonesColores[bloque].addEventListener("click", () =>
      asignar([...seleccionado], bloque));
  }
  verTodos.addEventListener("click", () => {
    encuadrar(puntos.map((_, i) => i));
  });
  verSeleccion.addEventListener("click", () => {
    encuadrar([...seleccionado]);
  });
  quitar.addEventListener("click", () => {
    ++cargaId;
    retirarCapa();
    archivo.value = "";
    mensaje("Puntos retirados.");
  });
  exportar.addEventListener("click", guardarCSV);
  importar.addEventListener("click", () => {
    if (!puntos.length) { mensaje("Carga primero el KMZ/KML original.", true); return; }
    archivoCSV.click();
  });
  archivoCSV.addEventListener("change", () => recuperarCSV(archivoCSV.files?.[0]));

  // Contraído: solo botón flotante 36 x 36, sin encabezado ni menú.
  // Guardar los display originales, incluido el input CSV oculto.
  const secciones = [...panel.children].filter(el => el !== titulo);
  const displayOriginal = new Map(secciones.map(el => [el, el.style.display]));
  let oculto = false;
  ocultar.addEventListener("click", () => {
    oculto = !oculto;
    secciones.forEach(el => {
      el.style.display = oculto ? "none" : displayOriginal.get(el);
    });
    tituloTexto.style.display = oculto ? "none" : "";
    titulo.style.marginBottom = oculto ? "0" : "8px";
    titulo.style.gap = oculto ? "0" : "7px";
    panel.style.width = oculto ? "36px" : "450px";
    panel.style.padding = oculto ? "0" : "14px";
    panel.style.background = oculto ? "transparent" : "#fff";
    panel.style.border = oculto ? "none" : "1px solid #bdc7d0";
    panel.style.boxShadow = oculto ? "none" : "0 7px 30px #0004";
    ocultar.textContent = oculto ? "☰" : "Contraer";
    ocultar.title = oculto ? "Mostrar panel de supervisores" : "Ocultar panel";
    ocultar.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(!oculto));
    ocultar.style.width = oculto ? "36px" : "";
    ocultar.style.height = oculto ? "36px" : "";
    ocultar.style.padding = oculto ? "0" : "5px 8px";
    ocultar.style.fontSize = oculto ? "21px" : "12px";
  });
  function cerrarPanel() {
    abierto = false;
    ++cargaId;
    retirarCapa();
    panel.remove();
    if (w.__superponerKMZ?.cerrar === cerrarPanel) delete w.__superponerKMZ;
  }
  cerrar.addEventListener("click", cerrarPanel);
  w.__superponerKMZ = { cerrar: cerrarPanel };
  retirarCapa();
  console.log("Panel de supervisores listo: selecciona KMZ o KML.");
})();
````

/*
 * CENTRALITY V4.1 - KMZ / SUPERVISORES / DETALLE MT-BT / PLANOS A1
 * Ejecutar en la consola del navegador con el mapa de Centrality abierto.
 * Lee archivos locales. No envia archivos ni cambia datos de Centrality.
 * Los colores son temporales; puedes exportar/importar el reparto como CSV.
 * La exportación PDF usa solo la cartografía renderizada a zoom fijo.
 * Las capas WMTS no se convierten en líneas vectoriales.
 */
(() => {
  "use strict";

  const frame = document.querySelector("iframe#content");
  if (!frame) {
    console.error("Abre primero el modulo Mapas de Centrality.");
    return;
  }

  let w, map, ol, doc;
  try {
    w = frame.contentWindow;
    map = w.webMap_?.map;
    ol = w.ol;
    doc = w.document;
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("No es posible acceder al mapa de Centrality:", e);
    return;
  }

  if (!map || !ol?.layer?.Vector || !ol?.source?.Vector ||
      !ol?.format?.KML || !ol?.Feature || !ol?.geom?.Point ||
      !ol?.style?.Style || !ol?.style?.Circle ||
      !ol?.style?.Text || !ol?.style?.Fill || !ol?.style?.Stroke) {
    console.error("El mapa no expone los componentes necesarios de OpenLayers.");
    return;
  }

  // Si viene de V1/V2, copiar puntos ANTES de cerrar su panel para no perder
  // la carga ni los bloques ya trabajados en esta sesión.
  const anteriores = [];
  try {
    function visitarAnteriores(collection) {
      for (const layer of collection?.getArray?.() || []) {
        const features = layer.getSource?.()?.getFeatures?.();
        if (layer.getZIndex?.() >= 99999 && Array.isArray(features)) {
          for (const f of features) {
            const geom = f.getGeometry?.();
            const numero = Number(f.get?.('numero'));
            if (geom?.getType?.() !== 'Point' || !Number.isInteger(numero) || numero < 1) continue;
            const xy = geom.getCoordinates();
            if (!xy?.slice(0,2).every(Number.isFinite)) continue;
            anteriores.push({numero, xy:xy.slice(0,2),
              codigo: String(f.get('codigo') || f.get('nombre') || f.get('name') || 'Sin código'),
              bloque: String(f.get('bloque') || 'Sin asignar')});
          }
        }
        if (layer.getLayers) visitarAnteriores(layer.getLayers());
      }
    }
    visitarAnteriores(map.getLayers());
    anteriores.sort((a,b) => a.numero - b.numero);
    if (new Set(anteriores.map(x => x.numero)).size !== anteriores.length) anteriores.length = 0;
  } catch(e) { anteriores.length = 0; console.warn('No fue posible conservar la carga anterior:',e); }

  // Cerrar paneles anteriores para evitar ediciones simultáneas de la malla WMTS.
  try { w.__pruebaDetalleCentrality?.cerrar?.(); } catch (e) { console.warn(e); }
  try { w.__centralityAtlasPDF?.destruir?.(); } catch (e) { console.warn(e); }
  try { w.__superponerKMZ?.cerrar?.(); } catch (e) {
    console.warn("No se pudo retirar el panel anterior:", e);
  }

  const SIN_BLOQUE = "Sin asignar";
  const COLORES = Object.freeze({
    [SIN_BLOQUE]: "#e53935", // rojo por defecto, sin supervisor asignado
    Verde: "#198754",
    Naranjo: "#ef8b16",
    Azul: "#2469d8"
  });
  const BLOQUES = ["Verde", "Naranjo", "Azul"];
  const OPCIONES_BLOQUE = [SIN_BLOQUE, ...BLOQUES];
  let puntos = [];
  let capa = null;
  let seleccionado = new Set();
  let enfocado = -1;
  let etiquetas = "cerca";
  let contenidoEtiqueta = "numero";
  // El primer visor mostraba los números únicamente cerca (resolución <25).
  const UMBRAL_ETIQUETA = 25;
  let atlasActivo = false;
  let atlasCancelar = false;
  let cargaId = 0;
  let abierto = true;
  let nombreArchivo = "puntos";
  let textoAviso = "";
  const cacheEstilos = new WeakMap();

  // Una sola interfaz, dentro del iframe que contiene el mapa.
  const panel = doc.createElement("div");
  panel.style.cssText = [
    "position:fixed", "top:12px", "right:12px",
    "z-index:2147483647", "width:450px",
    "max-width:calc(100vw - 24px)", "max-height:calc(100vh - 24px)",
    "overflow:auto", "box-sizing:border-box", "padding:14px",
    "background:#fff", "color:#17212b", "font:13px Arial,sans-serif",
    "border:1px solid #bdc7d0", "border-radius:10px",
    "box-shadow:0 7px 30px #0004"
  ].join(";");

    if (css) el.style.cssText = css;

  const ESTILO_CONTROL =
    "font:12px Arial,sans-serif;box-sizing:border-box;border:1px solid #abb6c1;" +
    "border-radius:5px;padding:7px;background:#fff;color:#17212b;";
  const ESTILO_BOTON = ESTILO_CONTROL + "cursor:pointer;background:#f5f7fa;";
  const btn = (padre, titulo, cssExtra = "") => {
    const el = crear(padre, "button", titulo, ESTILO_BOTON + cssExtra);
    el.type = "button";

  const fila = (padre, cssExtra = "") =>

  const titulo = fila(panel, "justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:8px;");
  const tituloTexto = crear(titulo, "strong", "Puntos KMZ · Supervisores", "font-size:15px;");
  const ocultar = btn(titulo, "Contraer", "padding:5px 8px;");

  crear(panel, "div", "Importa tu archivo KMZ o KML:", "margin-bottom:5px;");
  const archivo = crear(panel, "input", null,
    "display:block;width:100%;margin:4px 0 7px 0;font-size:12px;");
  archivo.type = "file";
  archivo.accept = ".kmz,.kml";

  const estado = crear(panel, "div", "Esperando archivo...",
