````
# GNAT research inspector

## Objective and authorized scope

Build and validate a local Node CDP observer, then perform bounded, authorized GNAT read-only research for independent Centrality Local integration, geolocation and electrical topology. Preserve all existing application files and prior research. No Centrality Local changes, global inventories, bulk extractions, credential capture, operational controls or unknown-effect widgets. Authorized browser session: existing Chrome, loopback 127.0.0.1:9222 if available; GNAT origin already identified in this conversation. No ambient remote credentials may be inspected.

## Design and constraints

- Native Node 24 fetch/WebSocket and node:test; no dependencies or proxy.
- Passive CDP Network observer only; no request interception, injection, storage reads, headers/ExtraInfo/cookie commands or browser configuration changes. Discard sensitive header structures at protocol ingress; never persist raw events.
- Exact-origin plus endpoint/action allowlists for bounded bodies, conservative redaction and size limits before persistence. Unknown actions: metadata only, never automatic invocation. Private evidence ignored by Git and access restricted/checked on this Windows host.
- Incremental JSONL summaries/schemas and explicit correlation markers; no models for log summarization. Distinguish observed bodies, loaded-client evidence and inference.
- Use known codes only; geometry tests use a few known IDs. Batch tests maximum two known IDs; never search maximum server limits by load. No area enumeration without a verified read contract and bounded user-authorized area.
- Strict TDD: enabled by supplied AGENTS.md; native runner source confirmed by tests/diagnostic.test.js. New runner: `node --test gnat-research/tests/*.test.cjs` once created. Require observed RED, GREEN and final checks.

## Tasks and routes

- [ ] T1 — Implement passive bounded inspector, redaction/schema summary, fixtures and tests; validate offline before live attachment. Route: delegated writer (multiple non-trivial files; preparation and implementation together). Verify strict TDD, privacy failure cases, safe CDP command allowlist, loopback/target bounds, response limits and cleanup. Baseline source: read-only mapper completed. Independent verification required if native review is unavailable/high risk.
- [ ] T2 — Attach only to authorized target if loopback CDP exists; use original UI for new evidence, bounded geometry/batch cases only. Route: parent browser orchestration with delegated local execution as needed. Verify inspector runtime first; no bypass of prior full-header inspector refusal. Stop affected live work if CDP unavailable; do not change Chrome settings. Capture provenance and explicit evidence gaps.
- [ ] T3 — Generate research catalogs, geolocation specification, GNAT_TOPOLOGIA.md, coverage and implementation guidance with evidence links. Route: delegated documentation writer (multiple non-trivial artifacts). Verify links, fixture provenance and no unsupported claims. Preserve earlier documents and distinguish visual raster, vector geometry and electrical connectivity.

## Delivery and checks

- Feature branch already `feat/gnat-integration`; repository has no commits and all application files are untracked. Never stage unrelated originals or bootstrap them implicitly. Isolated worktree creation is unavailable without a base; work only under gnat-research/ plus this task file and explicitly requested research document.
- RDD: on, global deciding source; review candidate consent is separate. Unborn base may make native review unavailable; report honestly, do not infer low risk.
- Delivery strategy: ask-on-risk. Forecast roughly 700–1100 authored lines across inspector/tests and research docs; no PR authorized. Chain strategy unresolved and must be asked before any over-budget commit. No silent initial commit or unrelated staging.
- Each work unit needs a Conventional Commit and evidence before closure under ODD; commit closure pending repository/review/chain constraints, not falsely completed.
- Checks: scoped node:test; native CLI dry/local fixture run; privacy review; live startup/clean stop if endpoint exists; documentation structural checks; parent spot check.
- Rollback: remove only newly created gnat-research/ and this task file; no application behavior changed. Private capture deletion requires explicit separately scoped safe handling.

## Progress

- Read user attachment and previous findings via bounded mapper. Confirmed Node v24.21.0 supports native fetch/WebSocket; no root dependency manifest.
- Existing injected diagnostic loses business values and changes browser globals; do not reuse its injection mechanism.
- Prior network-detail inspector was refused because it exposes cookies. New observer must exclude header data from retained records; it is not permission to use the refused tool.
- Skill registry missing in Engram and .atl; exact installed skill paths supplied directly to workers.
- T1 offline implementation observed: core.cjs, cli.cjs, two tests, synthetic geometry fixture, README and scoped .gitignore; 763 authored lines. Writer strict TDD RED/GREEN sequences observed and reported; MIME, scalar array/schema and duplicate/conflicting action regressions fixed before final freeze.
- Exact checks: `node --test gnat-research/tests/*.test.cjs` 26/26; `node --test tests/diagnostic.test.js` 3/3; both inspector files pass `node --check`. Independent verifier reran all checks and found no remaining reproducible defect. Actual-host Windows ACL tests passed. Parent spot check observed initial 20/20; final rerun pending parent readback.
- T2 blocked before any endpoint probe or live attachment: CDP transiently receives complete frames that may contain sensitive headers before ingress filtering. The user forbids capturing cookies/tokens; explicit clarification is needed before proceeding. No live GNAT queries, batch probes, area inventory or line geometry requests performed in this phase.
- Saved-source mapper found WMTS PNG MT/BT layers and ImageWMS elsewhere; separate vector selection overlay, line-capable highlighting and bounded point-identify client logic. These are saved-source findings, not live vector/topology responses. One source/config snippet unexpectedly exposed sensitive configuration fields to a worker tool context; further config inspection stopped. Values were not copied into new files or memory; source config excluded from deliverables.
- Native assessment returned high/unassessable because all files are untracked; do not treat as low risk. Native preflight/commit still pending, no staging or commits and no review receipt.
- T1 outcome is offline verified, not checklist-closed: live interoperability and commit/review closure remain pending. Parent final spot check observed 26/26 and both syntax checks passing.
- T3 produced root GNAT_TOPOLOGIA.md and nine research catalogs/specs, plus README navigation. Writer validated 35 relative links, two JSON examples and synthetic fixture; parent read back topology, geolocation and coverage documents. Added 401 document lines plus six README lines, 1170 authored lines total under new research deliverables (task document excluded). No real-response geometry fixtures or new live contracts claimed.
- Native selectorless preflight was attempted once after source freeze. It returned operation_failed, mutation_outcome=not_started, authority_applicability=not_evaluated, with access denied while freezing target. No native continuation/consent binding was issued; no retry, acknowledgement, receipt or fabricated approval. State preserved; ordinary repository delivery remains pending. No commit or staging was performed.
- Automated UI-event capture and JavaScript exception observation are not implemented; stdin markers are manual, HTML/business text is intentionally omitted. These limitations and the saved-config privacy incident are explicit in PENDIENTES.md and COBERTURA.md.

## Next step

### Authorized follow-up

- User explicitly approved transient CDP reception in the existing authorized browser session; authorization recorded in Engram #696. Retaining/displaying credentials, application writes, operational tools and global extraction remain excluded.
- Delegated standalone observer startup attempted once with duration 2 seconds. Private ACL initialization succeeded; zero records and exit 1. The exact loopback /json/list route returned non-success HTTP; numeric status was not retained. No target attachment or live shutdown verified. Empty private capture remains ignored; no browser settings changed or alternate ports probed.
- Chrome DevTools MCP remained available. Recovered missing bodies from already-performed requests without repeating searches. Confirmed singleton geometry success/data/Feature, two-ID batch success/data/FeatureCollection, Feature.id, geometry and bbox. Three observed points; OpenLayers reader default EPSG:4326 and actual transform matches prior EPSG:3857 FAP view center.
- Confirmed definition success/data/title/widget_definition/message_error. Definition exposition read-only/read-write is applied through initializeTab/setReadOnly; generator flag remains separate. Activated only verified read-only IDs widget 5 after inspecting actual tab/component handlers; list_object_external_ids success/data response and object_id paging observed. Partes response data/total and location_id paging confirmed. Widget-content success/data array contains widget_id/content string/array/null.
- Controlled two-ID geometry test via original map client method used existing pole/DBC IDs only. One original point-identify on visible F514139 segment returned line 32327210, F514139 - F514140, Tramo aéreo MT. One original geometry lookup returned a LineString with two vertices; original line technical view is open, class Cable, representation Lineal. Raw geometry body has no endpoint node/terminal IDs. Labels and circuit display are not electrical connectivity proof.
- Live source-class checks confirmed visible MT/BT/infrastructure WMTS image/png plus vector selection overlay and ImageWMS elsewhere, without retaining source URLs/credentials. An initial broader WMS parameter read surfaced session fields transiently in tool context; stopped that projection and retained only explicit safe class/format fields. No credential values go into deliverables.
- Electrical widget 142's loader has scripts:true and exposition read-write; not executed. No global extraction, area query, capacity probe, new negative test or code change in Local.
- Newly observed contracts need anonymized fixtures, contract tests and reconciled catalogs; existing earlier partial reports remain historical evidence. No more browser recollection needed for these confirmed responses.
- Follow-up DC and DF views were selected from cached F514139 candidates without a repeated search; class/type/hierarchy and distinct map centers observed. DF breadcrumb has intermediate F66023. Current DF view left open; no operational widget activated.
- T3 follow-up completed as a verified draft: EVIDENCIA_CONFIRMADA.md, observed/anonymized fixture catalog and three schema/projection tests added; nine catalogs, README and GNAT_TOPOLOGIA.md reconciled. Writer observed three missing-fixture RED assertions, then GREEN29/29 research tests and 3/3 existing diagnostic. Parent independently reran29/29 and read back evidence ledger, topology and geolocation specs. Writer verified43 links, JSON and scoped secret patterns. No production inspector or Local code changed.
- Current inspector limitations remain explicit: external-ID/point-identify bodies not in standalone allowlist; bbox/content/exposition values not retained by projection; no automatic UI/JS exception capture. No claim its automatic discovery is complete. Network truth was recovered through authorized MCP, not the failed standalone attachment.
- No capacity/rate-limit, nonexistent/error/auth, area inventory, terminal graph or independent authentication contract was established. No repeat of the earlier failed native preflight, no review approval, staging, commit or PR. Commit/review/chain closure remains pending and checkboxes are intentionally not presented as finished feature delivery.

Next bounded work: restore a compatible explicitly authorized standalone CDP endpoint, then add only verified consultation adapters/projections; obtain explicit terminal/node relationships before topology or electrical routes. Do not recollect the confirmed sample requests. Keep chain strategy/initial commit and prior failed native review separate and pending.
````

````
const {test}=require('node:test');
const assert=require('node:assert/strict');
const fs=require('node:fs');const vm=require('node:vm');
function environment(){
  let requests=0;let clones=0;let responseBody=JSON.stringify({success:true,data:{type:'Feature',id:8659987,geometry:{type:'Point',coordinates:[-72.06376236,-37.780086409]},properties:{display_name:'PRIVATE_NAME',token:'SECRET'}}});
  class El{constructor(){this.children=[];this.style={};this.listeners={};} append(...x){this.children.push(...x);} appendChild(x){this.children.push(x);return x;} addEventListener(k,f){this.listeners[k]=f;} remove(){} click(){} set innerHTML(x){throw Error('Unsafe HTML');}}
  class XHR{constructor(){this.listeners={};this.status=200;this.responseType='';} open(){} send(){this.responseText=responseBody;this.listeners.loadend?.();} addEventListener(k,f){this.listeners[k]=f;} removeEventListener(k){delete this.listeners[k];}}
  const originalFetch=async()=>{requests++;const text=responseBody;return {status:200,clone(){clones++;let delivered=false;return {body:{getReader(){return {async read(){if(delivered)return {done:true};delivered=true;return {done:false,value:new TextEncoder().encode(text)};},async cancel(){}};}}};}};};
  const doc={body:new El(),createElement(){return new El();},getElementById(){return null;}};
  const context={window:null,location:{href:'https://official.invalid/maps/index.php',origin:'https://official.invalid'},document:doc,fetch:originalFetch,XMLHttpRequest:XHR,URL,URLSearchParams,FormData,TextDecoder,TextEncoder,Blob,console:{warn(){}},performance:{now(){return 1;}},setTimeout(){return 1;},clearTimeout(){}};
  context.window=context;vm.createContext(context);
  const path='bridge/diagnostico_contrato_gnat.js';if(fs.existsSync(path))vm.runInContext(fs.readFileSync(path,'utf8'),context);
  return {context,originalFetch,XHR,requests:()=>requests,clones:()=>clones,setBody(v){responseBody=v;}};
}
const tick=()=>new Promise(r=>setImmediate(r));
test('diagnostic is passive, same origin, bounded and never exports payloads or credentials',async()=>{
  const e=environment();const diag=e.context.centralityGNATDiagnostic;assert.ok(diag,'Missing diagnostic');assert.equal(e.requests(),0);
  await e.context.fetch('/maps/svc_wmc.php?token=TOP_SECRET',{method:'POST',body:'action=get_objects_geojson&object_ids=8659987&csrf=SUPER_SECRET'});await tick();
  let data=diag.report();assert.equal(data.samples.length,1);
  const serialized=JSON.stringify(data);for(const value of ['official.invalid','TOP_SECRET','SUPER_SECRET','8659987','PRIVATE_NAME','-72.063','csrf','token'])assert.equal(serialized.includes(value),false,value);
  assert.equal(data.samples[0].action,'get_objects_geojson');assert.equal(data.samples[0].geometry_type,'Point');assert.equal(data.cors_localhost,'NO_VERIFICADO');
  await e.context.fetch('https://unrelated.invalid/svc_wmc.php',{body:'action=textual_search'});
  await e.context.fetch('/unrelated.php',{body:'action=textual_search'});await tick();assert.equal(e.clones(),1);
  diag.stop();assert.equal(e.context.fetch,e.originalFetch);
});
test('XHR shape observer restores hooks and sample cap; raw large or bad responses omitted',async()=>{
  const e=environment();const diag=e.context.centralityGNATDiagnostic;assert.ok(diag,'Missing diagnostic');
  for(let i=0;i<40;i++){const xhr=new e.XHR();xhr.open('POST','/maps/svc_wmc.php');xhr.send('action=get_objects_geojson&object_ids=1');}
  assert.ok(diag.report().samples.length<=24);assert.ok(JSON.stringify(diag.report()).length<=64000);
  const replacement=()=>{};e.context.fetch=replacement;diag.stop();assert.equal(e.context.fetch,replacement);
  const before=diag.report().samples.length;const xhr=new e.XHR();xhr.open('POST','/maps/svc_wmc.php');xhr.send('action=textual_search');assert.equal(diag.report().samples.length,before);
  const f=environment();f.setBody('SECRET'.repeat(30000));await f.context.fetch('/maps/svc_wmc.php',{body:'action=textual_search'});await tick();assert.equal(f.context.centralityGNATDiagnostic.report().samples[0].response_state,'oversize');
  f.context.centralityGNATDiagnostic.stop();
});

test('JSON-typed XHR bodies are not traversed without a byte-size bound; malformed text is reported safely',async()=>{
  const e=environment();const diag=e.context.centralityGNATDiagnostic;
  const xhr=new e.XHR();xhr.open('POST','/maps/svc_object_view.php');xhr.responseType='json';
  xhr.response={success:true,data:{secret:'PRIVATE_VALUE'}};xhr.send('action=get_object_definition');
  assert.equal(diag.report().samples[0].response_state,'unavailable_json_object');
  assert.equal(diag.report().samples[0].response_shape,null);
  e.setBody('{PRIVATE_BROKEN_JSON');await e.context.fetch('/maps/svc_wmc.php',{body:'action=textual_search'});await tick();
  assert.equal(diag.report().samples[1].response_state,'invalid_json');assert.equal(JSON.stringify(diag.report()).includes('PRIVATE'),false);
  diag.stop();
});
````

````
const {test}=require('node:test');
const assert=require('node:assert/strict');
const fs=require('node:fs');
const load=async()=>{if(!fs.existsSync('web/gnat-client.js'))return null;return import('data:text/javascript;base64,'+fs.readFileSync('web/gnat-client.js').toString('base64'));};
class Element{
  constructor(){this.children=[];this.textContent='';this.handlers={};this.classList={toggle(){}};}
  append(...values){this.children.push(...values);} appendChild(v){this.children.push(v);return v;}
  replaceChildren(...values){this.children=values;} addEventListener(k,fn){this.handlers[k]=fn;}
  set innerHTML(v){throw Error('Unsafe HTML boundary');}
}
function dom(){const elements=new Map();global.document={createElement(){return new Element();},getElementById(id){if(!elements.has(id))elements.set(id,new Element());return elements.get(id);}};return elements;}
function allText(el){return el.textContent+' '+el.children.map(allText).join(' ');}
test('asset fiche uses inert text, provenance and does not create HTML',async()=>{
  const module=await load();assert.ok(module,'GNAT UI module absent');dom();const host=new Element();
  module.renderAsset(host,{id:'8659987',code:'<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>',asset_type:'poste',geometry:{type:'Point'},metadata:'<script>alert(2)</script>',source:'MOCK',consulted_at:'2026-10-20T20:00:00+00:00',synthetic:false});
  const text=allText(host);assert.ok(text.includes('<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>'));assert.ok(text.includes('MOCK'));assert.ok(text.includes('2026-10-20'));assert.ok(text.includes('Point'));
});
test('disconnected real query disabled; explicit mock load and clear only touch optional overlay',async()=>{
  const module=await load();assert.ok(module,'GNAT UI module absent');const elements=dom();const calls=[];const overlays=[];
  const asset={id:'8659987',code:'F5353',asset_type:'poste',geometry:{type:'Point',coordinates:[-72,-38]},metadata:'MOCK',source:'MOCK',consulted_at:'now'};
  const api=async(path)=>{calls.push(path);return path.endsWith('/status')?{validated:false,state:'disconnected',message:'Pendiente'}:{ok:true,data:{state:'found',matches:[asset]}};};
  await module.initGNAT(api,{setAssetGeometry(v){overlays.push(v);},fitAssetGeometry(){}});
  assert.equal(document.getElementById('gnat-consult').disabled,true);assert.deepEqual(calls,['/api/gnat/status']);
  document.getElementById('gnat-query').value='F5353';document.getElementById('gnat-type').value='';
  await document.getElementById('gnat-mock').handlers.click();assert.deepEqual(calls,['/api/gnat/status','/api/gnat/mock']);
  document.getElementById('gnat-overlay').checked=true;document.getElementById('gnat-overlay').handlers.change();
  assert.deepEqual(overlays.at(-1),asset.geometry);
  document.getElementById('gnat-clear').handlers.click();assert.equal(overlays.at(-1),null);
});

test('MOCK failure stays local and leaves panel usable while live query remains disabled',async()=>{
  const module=await load();dom();const overlays=[];
  await module.initGNAT(async(path)=>{if(path.endsWith('/status'))throw Error('local status unavailable');return {ok:false,error:{message:'Tiempo de espera'}};},{setAssetGeometry(v){overlays.push(v);}});
  document.getElementById('gnat-query').value='F5353';document.getElementById('gnat-type').value='';
  await document.getElementById('gnat-mock').handlers.click();
  assert.ok(document.getElementById('gnat-status').textContent.includes('Tiempo de espera'));
  assert.equal(document.getElementById('gnat-mock').disabled,false);assert.equal(document.getElementById('gnat-consult').disabled,true);assert.equal(overlays.at(-1),null);
});
````

````
const {test}=require('node:test');
const assert=require('node:assert/strict');
const fs=require('node:fs');
const moduleMap=import('data:text/javascript;base64,'+fs.readFileSync('web/map.js').toString('base64'));
test('blocks filter shared openings and independent labels preserve display gaps',async()=>{
  const m=await moduleMap;
  assert.equal(typeof m.matchesBlock,'function');
  const p={id:72,numero:21,codigo:'F5353',bloques:['B1','B2'],clase:'apertura'};
  assert.equal(m.matchesBlock(p,'B2'),true);assert.equal(m.matchesBlock(p,'B3'),false);assert.equal(m.matchesBlock(p,''),true);
  assert.equal(m.pointLabel(p,true,false),'21');assert.equal(m.pointLabel(p,false,true),'F5353');assert.equal(m.pointLabel(p,true,true),'21 · F5353');assert.equal(m.pointLabel(p,false,false),'');
});
test('default offline renderer never loads OSM, selects by ID and draws X at point footprint',async()=>{
  const {LocalMap}=await moduleMap;
  global.window={devicePixelRatio:1};global.ResizeObserver=class{observe(){}};
  global.requestAnimationFrame=()=>{};global.document={getElementById(){return null;}};
  const canvas={getContext(){return {};},parentElement:{getBoundingClientRect(){return {width:500,height:400};}},style:{},addEventListener(){}};
  const map=new LocalMap(canvas);map.render=()=>{};
  assert.equal(map.showOSM,false);
  const events=[];map.ctx=new Proxy({}, {get(target,key){return (...args)=>events.push([key,...args]);},set(){return true;}});
  const opening={id:900,numero:21,codigo:'F5353',clase:'apertura',supervisor:'Azul',longitud:-72.6,latitud:-38.74};
  map.setPoints([opening]);assert.equal(map.hit(250,200).id,900);map.draw();
  assert.equal(events.some(e=>e[0]==='arc'),false);
  assert.equal(events.some(e=>e[0]==='moveTo' && e[1]===244 && e[2]===194),true);
  assert.equal(events.some(e=>e[0]==='lineTo' && e[1]===256 && e[2]===206),true);
});

test('optional synthetic line overlay draws all segments without adding or changing points',async()=>{
  const {LocalMap}=await moduleMap;
  const map=Object.create(LocalMap.prototype);map.render=()=>{};map.points=[{id:7,numero:83}];
  const calls=[];map.ctx=new Proxy({}, {get(t,k){return (...v)=>calls.push([k,...v]);},set(){return true;}});map.toScreen=(x,y)=>[x,y];
  assert.equal(typeof map.setAssetGeometry,'function');
  map.setAssetGeometry({type:'MultiLineString',coordinates:[[[1,2],[3,4]],[[5,6],[7,8]]]});map.drawAssetOverlay();
  assert.deepEqual(calls.filter(x=>x[0]==='moveTo'),[['moveTo',1,2],['moveTo',5,6]]);
  assert.deepEqual(calls.filter(x=>x[0]==='lineTo'),[['lineTo',3,4],['lineTo',7,8]]);
  assert.deepEqual(map.points,[{id:7,numero:83}]);map.setAssetGeometry(null);assert.equal(map.assetGeometry,null);
});
````

````
import io
from contextlib import closing
import json
import sqlite3
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch
from app import db, importers

ROUTE = "ID;Numero;Subestacion;Longitud;Latitud;SupervisorColor;BloqueID\nr-9;9;SED_9;-72,59;-38,735;Verde;B1\nr-21;21;SED_21;-72,60;-38,74;Sin asignar;B2\n"
OPENING = "ID;Numero;Codigo;Longitud;Latitud;SupervisorColor;Bloques\na-4;4;F5353;-72,59;-38,735;Azul;B1|B2\n"

class CompatibilityTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.path = Path(self.tmp.name) / 'work.sqlite3'
        self.patch = patch.object(db, 'DB_PATH', self.path)
        self.patch.start()
        db.init_db()

    def tearDown(self):
        self.patch.stop()
        self.tmp.cleanup()

    def load(self, text):
        rows, coordinates = importers.from_csv(text.encode(), 'prueba.csv')
        self.assertTrue(coordinates)
        db.replace_points(rows)

    def test_route_and_shared_openings_coexist_with_gaps(self):
        self.load(ROUTE)
        route_ids = [p['id'] for p in db.list_points()]
        self.load(OPENING)
        points = db.list_points()
        self.assertEqual([p['numero'] for p in points], [4, 9, 21])
        self.assertEqual(points[0]['clase'], 'apertura')
        self.assertEqual(points[0]['bloques'], ['B1', 'B2'])
        self.assertEqual(points[1]['import_id'], 'r-9')
        self.assertEqual([p['id'] for p in points[1:]], route_ids)
        self.assertEqual(points[2]['supervisor'], '')
        self.assertAlmostEqual(points[1]['longitud'], -72.59)

    def test_replacing_one_kind_never_deletes_other(self):
        self.load(ROUTE)
        self.load(OPENING)
        opening = next(p for p in db.list_points() if p['clase'] == 'apertura')
        self.load(ROUTE.replace('SED_9', 'SED_NEW'))
        self.assertIn(opening, db.list_points())
        self.assertEqual(len(db.list_points()), 3)

    def test_csv_invalid_coordinates_are_atomic_and_locate_row_column(self):
        self.load(OPENING)
        before = db.list_points()
        for value in ('-72590000', 'NaN', 'Infinity', '-72.59.00', '', '1e999'):
            with self.subTest(value=value):
                with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, r'Fila 2.*Longitud'):
                    self.load(ROUTE.replace('-72,59', value))
                self.assertEqual(db.list_points(), before)

    def test_duplicate_identity_number_and_invalid_supervisor_rejected(self):
        for text in (ROUTE.replace('r-21', 'r-9'), ROUTE.replace(';21;', ';9;'),
                     ROUTE.replace(';Verde;', ';Rojo;'), ROUTE.replace(';9;', ';0;'),
                     ROUTE.replace(';B1\n', ';B1|B2\n')):
            with self.subTest(text=text):
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    self.load(text)
        self.assertEqual(db.list_points(), [])

    def test_invalid_kmz_is_validation_error(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            importers.parse_kmz(b'not zip', 'mal.kmz')

    def test_legacy_schema_migrates_once_with_backup_and_no_loss(self):
        self.path.unlink()
        schema = """CREATE TABLE puntos (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, numero INTEGER NOT NULL, codigo TEXT NOT NULL, codigo_busqueda TEXT NOT NULL, tipo TEXT DEFAULT 'sin clasificar', categoria TEXT DEFAULT '', supervisor TEXT DEFAULT '', latitud REAL NOT NULL, longitud REAL NOT NULL, descripcion TEXT DEFAULT '', origen TEXT DEFAULT '', actualizado TEXT NOT NULL);"""
        with closing(sqlite3.connect(self.path)) as conn, conn:
            conn.executescript(schema)
            conn.execute("INSERT INTO puntos (id,numero,codigo,codigo_busqueda,latitud,longitud,actualizado,supervisor) VALUES (83,91,'F_83','F_83',-38,-72,'old','Azul')")
        db.init_db()
        db.init_db()
        point = db.list_points()[0]
        self.assertEqual((point['id'], point['numero'], point['codigo'], point['supervisor']), (83, 91, 'F_83', 'Azul'))
        self.assertEqual(point['clase'], 'recorrido')
        backups = list(self.path.parent.glob('work.sqlite3.pre-*.bak'))
        self.assertEqual(len(backups), 1)
        with closing(sqlite3.connect(backups[0])) as conn:
            self.assertEqual(conn.execute('SELECT id,numero,codigo FROM puntos').fetchone(), (83,91,'F_83'))
            self.assertNotIn('clase', [r[1] for r in conn.execute('PRAGMA table_info(puntos)')])

    def test_legacy_attributes_preserve_added_fields(self):
        self.load(OPENING)
        db.merge_attributes([{'numero':4, 'codigo':'F5353', 'supervisor':'Naranjo'}])
        point = db.list_points()[0]
        self.assertEqual((point['clase'], point['bloques'], point['supervisor']), ('apertura',['B1','B2'],'Naranjo'))

    def test_malformed_kml_point_rejects_whole_import(self):
        from test_core import KML
        for bad in (b'not-number',b'-7259000'):
            with self.subTest(bad=bad):
                with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'Longitud'):
                    importers.from_kml(KML.replace(b'-72.590',bad),'bad.kml')

    def test_legacy_clase_header_remains_asset_type_not_point_kind(self):
        text='Numero;CodigoPoste;Clase;Bloque;Latitud;Longitud\n5;SED_LEGACY;subestacion;Verde;-38,74;-72,60\n'
        self.load(text)
        point=db.list_points()[0]
        self.assertEqual((point['tipo'],point['clase'],point['codigo']),('subestacion','recorrido','SED_LEGACY'))
````

````
"""python -m unittest discover -s tests -v"""
import io
import json
import tempfile
import unittest
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch
from app import importers,db,captures

KML=b'''<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<kml xmlns="http://www.opengis.net/kml/2.2"><Document>
<Placemark><name>1 - F697480</name><description>Postes c.a. 10 m</description><Point><coordinates>-72.590,-38.735,0</coordinates></Point></Placemark>
<Placemark><name>2 - SED_113</name><description>Subestaci&#243;n</description><Point><coordinates>-72.600,-38.740,0</coordinates></Point></Placemark>
<Placemark><name>Ruta no punto</name><LineString><coordinates>-72.5,-38.1 -72.4,-38.2</coordinates></LineString></Placemark>
</Document></kml>'''

class CoreTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp=tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.override=patch.object(db,'DB_PATH',Path(self.temp.name)/'puntos.sqlite3')
        self.override.start()
        self.override_capdir=patch.object(captures,'CAPTURE_DIR',Path(self.temp.name)/'capturas')
        self.override_capdir.start()
        captures.CAPTURE_DIR.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        self.override_manifest=patch.object(captures,'MANIFEST',captures.CAPTURE_DIR/'indice.json')
        self.override_manifest.start()
        db.init_db()
    def tearDown(self):
        self.override_manifest.stop();self.override_capdir.stop();self.override.stop();self.temp.cleanup()
    def test_kml_points_only(self):
        result=importers.from_kml(KML,'test.kml')
        self.assertEqual(len(result),2)
        self.assertEqual(result[0]['codigo'],'F697480')
        self.assertEqual(result[0]['tipo'],'poste')
        self.assertEqual(result[1]['tipo'],'subestacion')
        self.assertAlmostEqual(result[0]['longitud'],-72.59)
    def test_kmz(self):
        stream=io.BytesIO()
        with zipfile.ZipFile(stream,'w',zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as archive:archive.writestr('doc.kml',KML)
        self.assertEqual(len(importers.parse_kmz(stream.getvalue(),'prueba.kmz')),2)
    def test_csv_v2_and_exact_search(self):
        db.replace_points(importers.from_kml(KML,'test'))
        csv_data='Numero;CodigoPoste;Bloque;Longitud;Latitud\n1;F697480;Verde;-72.590;-38.735\n2;SED_113;Naranjo;-72.600;-38.740\n'
        rows,with_coords=importers.from_csv(csv_data.encode(),'reparto.csv')
        self.assertTrue(with_coords)
        # Existing V2 CSV has coordinates and can stand alone as a fresh import.
        self.assertEqual(len(rows),2)
        db.replace_points(rows)
        self.assertEqual(db.list_points()[0]['supervisor'],'Verde')
        result=db.search_exact(['F697480','F6974800','SED_113'],[])
        self.assertEqual([r['estado'] for r in result],['encontrado','no encontrado','encontrado'])
    def test_merge_preserves_type_when_not_provided(self):
        db.replace_points(importers.from_kml(KML,'test'))
        report=db.merge_attributes([{'numero':'1','codigo':'F697480','supervisor':'Azul'}])
        self.assertEqual(report['actualizados'],1)
        self.assertEqual(db.list_points()[0]['tipo'],'poste')
        self.assertEqual(db.list_points()[0]['supervisor'],'Azul')
    def test_duplicate_exact_is_ambiguous(self):
        a=importers.from_kml(KML,'test')
        a.append({**a[0],'numero':3})
        db.replace_points(a)
        self.assertEqual(db.search_exact(['F697480'],['poste'])[0]['estado'],'ambiguo')
    def test_csv_semicolon_and_code_exact(self):
        raw='Numero;CodigoPoste;Tipo;Categoria;Bloque;Latitud;Longitud\n1;F697480;poste;MT;Azul;-38.735;-72.590\n'
        rows,hascoords=importers.from_csv(raw.encode(),'a.csv')
        self.assertEqual(rows[0]['codigo'],'F697480');self.assertTrue(hascoords)
    def test_bad_kmz(self):
        with self.assertRaises(Exception):importers.parse_kmz(b'bad data','a.kmz')

if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()
````

````
import importlib
import json
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

try:
    gnat=importlib.import_module('app.integrations.gnat')
except ModuleNotFoundError:
    gnat=None

POINT={"success":True,"data":{"type":"Feature","geometry":{"type":"Point","coordinates":[-72.06376236,-37.780086409]},"id":8659987,"properties":{"display_name":"F5353"}}}

class GNATTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.assertIsNotNone(gnat,'Falta el adaptador GNAT aislado')
    def test_disconnected_adapter_truthful_no_network(self):
        with patch('socket.create_connection',side_effect=AssertionError('No network')):
            adapter=gnat.DisconnectedAdapter()
            self.assertFalse(adapter.get_connection_status()['validated'])
            for value in (adapter.search_assets('F5353'),adapter.get_asset_geometry('8659987'),
                          adapter.get_asset_metadata('8659987'),adapter.get_asset_by_id('8659987')):
                self.assertFalse(value['ok']);self.assertEqual(value['error']['code'],'disconnected')
            self.assertEqual(adapter.list_available_layers()['data'],[])
    def test_mock_exact_search_types_and_ambiguity(self):
        adapter=gnat.FixtureAdapter()
        self.assertEqual(adapter.search_assets(' f5353 ')['data']['state'],'found')
        self.assertEqual(adapter.search_assets('F535')['data']['state'],'not_found')
        self.assertEqual(adapter.search_assets('F5353', ['subestacion'])['data']['state'],'not_found')
        row=adapter.assets[0]
        duplicate={**row,'id':'another-id'}
        adapter.assets.append(duplicate)
        self.assertEqual(adapter.search_assets('F5353')['data']['state'],'ambiguous')
        self.assertEqual(adapter.get_connection_status()['mode'],'MOCK')
        self.assertFalse(adapter.get_connection_status()['validated'])
    def test_point_normalized_wgs84_provenance_and_raw_in_memory_only(self):
        out=gnat.normalize_response(json.dumps(POINT),source='diagnostico autorizado')
        self.assertTrue(out['ok'])
        asset=out['data'];self.assertEqual(asset['id'],'8659987')
        self.assertEqual(asset['geometry']['coordinates'],[-72.06376236,-37.780086409])
        self.assertEqual(asset['source'],'diagnostico autorizado')
        self.assertTrue(asset['consulted_at'].endswith('+00:00'))
        self.assertEqual(asset['raw_response'],POINT)
        public=gnat.public_result(out)
        self.assertNotIn('raw_response',public['data'])
        self.assertEqual(out['data']['raw_response'],POINT)
    def test_mock_line_and_multiline_are_synthetic(self):
        adapter=gnat.FixtureAdapter()
        for identity,kind in [('SYN-LINE','LineString'),('SYN-MULTI','MultiLineString')]:
            result=adapter.get_asset_by_id(identity)
            self.assertTrue(result['ok']);self.assertEqual(result['data']['geometry']['type'],kind)
            self.assertTrue(result['data']['synthetic']);self.assertIn('MOCK',result['data']['source'])
        self.assertFalse(adapter.get_asset_geometry('8659985')['ok'])
    def test_malformed_geojson_is_rejected_not_guessed(self):
        for geometry in ({'type':'Point','coordinates':[-181,0]}, {'type':'Point','coordinates':[True,1]},
                         {'type':'Point','coordinates':[1,float('nan')]}, {'type':'LineString','coordinates':[[1,2]]},
                         {'type':'MultiLineString','coordinates':[]}, {'type':'Polygon','coordinates':[]},
                         {'type':'Point','coordinates':['-72','-38']}):
            with self.subTest(geometry=geometry):
                response={**POINT,'data':{**POINT['data'],'geometry':geometry}}
                self.assertEqual(gnat.normalize_response(response)['error']['code'],'invalid_geometry')
    def test_errors_http_timeout_and_invalid_payloads(self):
        for status in (401,403):
            self.assertEqual(gnat.normalize_response(POINT,http_status=status)['error']['code'],'not_authorized')
        self.assertEqual(gnat.normalize_response(POINT,transport_error=TimeoutError())['error']['code'],'timeout')
        self.assertEqual(gnat.normalize_response(POINT,transport_error=ConnectionError())['error']['code'],'connection_error')
        for body,code in [('', 'empty_response'),('{','invalid_json'),({},'invalid_response'),
                          ({'success':False},'service_error'),({'success':True},'invalid_response'),
                          ({'success':True,'data':None},'invalid_response')]:
            with self.subTest(body=body):self.assertEqual(gnat.normalize_response(body)['error']['code'],code)
    def test_metadata_html_scripts_widgets_never_become_executable(self):
        raw='<div><b>Cable:</b> 6 AWG</div><script>alert(1)</script><img src=x onerror=alert(2)><style>body{}</style>'
        text=gnat.metadata_text(raw)
        self.assertIn('Cable:',text);self.assertIn('6 AWG',text)
        self.assertNotIn('<',text);self.assertNotIn('alert',text);self.assertNotIn('body{}',text)
        self.assertEqual(gnat.metadata_text([{'widget_id':3,'content':'<b>Dato</b>'}]),'Dato')
    def test_mock_api_does_not_modify_points(self):
        adapter=gnat.FixtureAdapter()
        with patch('app.db.replace_points',side_effect=AssertionError('No point mutations')):
            self.assertTrue(adapter.get_asset_metadata('8659987')['ok'])
            self.assertTrue(adapter.get_asset_geometry('8659987')['ok'])
````

````
import base64
import http.client
import json
import tempfile
import threading
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch
from http.server import ThreadingHTTPServer
from app import server, db, captures
from test_compat import ROUTE, OPENING

class HTTPTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp=tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        root=Path(self.tmp.name)
        cap=root/'captures'; cap.mkdir()
        self.patches=[patch.object(db,'DB_PATH',root/'points.sqlite3'), patch.object(captures,'CAPTURE_DIR',cap),
                      patch.object(captures,'MANIFEST',cap/'indice.json'),patch.object(server,'CAPTURE_DIR',cap)]
        for item in self.patches:item.start()
        db.init_db()
        self.http=ThreadingHTTPServer(('127.0.0.1',0),server.Handler)
        self.patches.append(patch.object(server,'PORT',self.http.server_port));self.patches[-1].start()
        self.thread=threading.Thread(target=self.http.serve_forever,daemon=True);self.thread.start()
    def tearDown(self):
        self.http.shutdown();self.http.server_close();self.thread.join()
        for item in reversed(self.patches):item.stop()
        self.tmp.cleanup()
    def request(self, method, path, body=None, headers=None):
        conn=http.client.HTTPConnection('127.0.0.1',self.http.server_port,timeout=3)
        try:
            conn.request(method,path,body,headers or {})
            response=conn.getresponse();data=response.read()
            return response.status, data, dict(response.getheaders())
        finally:conn.close()
    def upload(self,text,confirm=False):
        return self.request('POST','/api/import?filename=test.csv'+('&confirm_replace=1' if confirm else ''),text.encode())
    def test_import_confirms_only_replaced_kind(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.upload(ROUTE)[0],200)
        self.assertEqual(self.upload(OPENING)[0],200)
        before=db.list_points()
        status,body,_=self.upload(ROUTE)
        self.assertEqual(status,409)
        self.assertTrue(json.loads(body)['requires_confirmation'])
        self.assertEqual(db.list_points(),before)
        self.assertEqual(self.upload(ROUTE,True)[0],200)
        self.assertIn(before[0],db.list_points())
    def test_export_two_schemas_decimal_comma_roundtrip(self):
        self.upload(ROUTE);self.upload(OPENING)
        for kind,header in [('route','ID;Numero;Subestacion;Longitud;Latitud;SupervisorColor;BloqueID'),
                            ('opening','ID;Numero;Codigo;Longitud;Latitud;SupervisorColor;Bloques')]:
            status,data,headers=self.request('GET','/api/export.csv?kind='+kind)
            text=data.decode('utf-8-sig')
            self.assertEqual(status,200);self.assertEqual(text.splitlines()[0],header)
            self.assertIn('-72,59',text)
            self.assertEqual(self.upload(text,True)[0],200)
        self.assertEqual(len(db.list_points()),3)
    def test_bad_upload_and_foreign_origin_do_not_mutate(self):
        self.upload(ROUTE);before=db.list_points()
        self.assertEqual(self.request('POST','/api/import?filename=bad.kmz',b'not zip')[0],400)
        self.assertEqual(self.request('POST','/api/import?filename=bad.csv',ROUTE.replace('-72,59','-725900').encode())[0],400)
        self.assertEqual(self.request('POST','/api/supervisores',b'{}',{'Origin':'https://foreign.invalid'})[0],403)
        self.assertEqual(db.list_points(),before)
    def test_health_static_editor_batch_and_captures_remain_local(self):
        self.upload(ROUTE)
        self.assertEqual(self.request('GET','/')[0],200)
        self.assertEqual(self.request('GET','/api/health')[0],200)
        point=db.list_points()[0]
        status,body,_=self.request('PATCH','/api/points/'+str(point['id']),json.dumps({'supervisor':'Azul'}))
        self.assertEqual(status,200);self.assertEqual(json.loads(body)['point']['import_id'],'r-9')
        status,body,_=self.request('POST','/api/batch',json.dumps({'codes':['SED_9','SED_90'],'types':[]}))
        self.assertEqual([x['estado'] for x in json.loads(body)['items']],['encontrado','no encontrado'])
        png=b'\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n'+b'\x00'*8+(100).to_bytes(4,'big')*2
        payload={'format':'centrality-local-capture-v1','bounds3857':[0,0,100,100],'imageDataUrl':'data:image/png;base64,'+base64.b64encode(png).decode()}
        status,body,_=self.request('POST','/api/captures/import',json.dumps(payload))
        self.assertEqual(status,200);capture=json.loads(body)['capture']
        self.assertEqual(self.request('GET','/capturas/'+capture['archivo'])[1],png)
        self.assertEqual(self.request('GET','/api/captures')[0],200)
        self.assertEqual(self.request('DELETE','/api/captures/'+capture['id'])[0],200)

    def test_gnat_status_mock_isolation_and_no_raw_html(self):
        self.upload(ROUTE);before=db.list_points()
        status,body,_=self.request('GET','/api/gnat/status')
        self.assertEqual(status,200);self.assertFalse(json.loads(body)['validated'])
        status,body,_=self.request('POST','/api/gnat/mock',json.dumps({'query':' f5353 '}))
        self.assertEqual(status,200);result=json.loads(body)
        self.assertEqual(result['data']['state'],'found')
        self.assertIn('MOCK',result['data']['matches'][0]['source'])
        self.assertNotIn('raw_response',body.decode())
        self.assertEqual(db.list_points(),before)

    def test_default_and_legacy_export_roundtrip_preserves_both_point_kinds(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.upload(ROUTE)[0],200)
        self.assertEqual(self.upload(OPENING)[0],200)
        expected=[
            ('apertura','a-4',4,'F5353','Azul',['B1','B2'],-72.59,-38.735),
            ('recorrido','r-9',9,'SED_9','Verde',['B1'],-72.59,-38.735),
            ('recorrido','r-21',21,'SED_21','',['B2'],-72.60,-38.74),
        ]
        for path in ('/api/export.csv','/api/export.csv?kind=legacy'):
            with self.subTest(path=path):
                status,data,_=self.request('GET',path)
                self.assertEqual(status,200)
                status,body,_=self.request('POST','/api/import?filename=roundtrip.csv&confirm_replace=1',data)
                self.assertEqual(status,200,body.decode())
                actual=[tuple(p[k] for k in ('clase','import_id','numero','codigo','supervisor','bloques','longitud','latitud')) for p in db.list_points()]
                self.assertEqual(actual,expected)
````

````
import os
import subprocess
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

@unittest.skipUnless(os.name=='nt','Windows CMD launcher')
class LauncherTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def run_launcher(self,with_py):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            root=Path(directory)
            log=root/'called.txt'
            for name in (('python','py') if with_py else ('python',)):
                (root/(name+'.cmd')).write_text('@echo off\necho '+name+' %* > "'+str(log)+'"\nexit /b 0\n')
            env={**os.environ,'PATH':str(root)+';'+os.environ['SystemRoot']+'\\System32'}
            subprocess.run([os.environ['COMSPEC'],'/d','/c',str(Path('INICIAR.bat').resolve())],input='\n',text=True,capture_output=True,env=env,timeout=10)
            return log.read_text().strip() if log.exists() else ''
    def test_python_fallback_when_py_missing(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.run_launcher(False),'python iniciar.py')
    def test_py_launcher_when_available(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.run_launcher(True),'py -3 iniciar.py')
````

````
/*
 * CENTRALITY V4 - KMZ / SUPERVISORES / DETALLE MT-BT / PLANOS A1
 * Ejecutar en la consola del navegador con el mapa de Centrality abierto.
 * Lee archivos locales. No envia archivos ni cambia datos de Centrality.
 * Los colores son temporales; puedes exportar/importar el reparto como CSV.
 * La exportación PDF usa solo la cartografía renderizada a zoom fijo.
 * Las capas WMTS no se convierten en líneas vectoriales.

(() => {
  "use strict";

  const CSV_TABLES = {
    route: { columns: ["ID", "Numero", "Subestacion", "Longitud", "Latitud", "SupervisorColor", "BloqueID"],
      keys: ["id", "numero", "subestacion", "longitud", "latitud", "supervisorColor", "bloqueId"], tipo: "PUNTO" },
    opening: { columns: ["ID", "Numero", "Codigo", "Longitud", "Latitud", "SupervisorColor", "Bloques"],
      keys: ["id", "numero", "codigo", "longitud", "latitud", "supervisorColor", "bloques"], tipo: "APERTURA" }
  };

  function normalizeTableRows(rows, kind, reservedIds = []) {
    const ids = new Set(reservedIds), numbers = new Set();
    for (const row of rows) {
      const id = String(row.id ?? "").trim();
      if (id && (!/^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]*$/.test(id) || ids.has(id))) {
        throw Error("ID inválido o duplicado: " + id);
      }
      if (id) ids.add(id);
    }
    let nextId = 1;
    return rows.map(row => {
      let id = String(row.id ?? "").trim();
      if (!id) {
        while (ids.has("marker-" + nextId)) nextId++;
        id = "marker-" + nextId++;
        ids.add(id);
      }
      const numero = Number(row.numero);
      if (!Number.isSafeInteger(numero) || numero < 1 || numbers.has(numero)) {
        throw Error("Numero inválido o duplicado: " + row.numero);
      }
      numbers.add(numero);
      const coordinate = (value, limit, name) => {
        const text = String(value ?? "").trim();
        const number = Number(text.replace(",", "."));
        if (!/^[+-]?(?:\d+(?:[.,]\d*)?|[.,]\d+)(?:e[+-]?\d+)?$/i.test(text) ||
            !Number.isFinite(number) || Math.abs(number) > limit) throw Error(name + " inválida.");
        return number;
      };
      const supervisorColor = String(row.supervisorColor ?? "").trim();
      if (!["Verde", "Naranjo", "Azul", "Sin asignar"].includes(supervisorColor)) {
        throw Error("SupervisorColor inválido: " + supervisorColor);
      }
      const result = { id, numero, longitud: coordinate(row.longitud, 180, "Longitud"),
        latitud: coordinate(row.latitud, 90, "Latitud"), supervisorColor };
      if (kind === "route") {
        result.subestacion = String(row.subestacion ?? "");
        result.bloqueId = String(row.bloqueId ?? "").trim();
        if (result.bloqueId.includes("|")) throw Error("BloqueID admite un solo bloque.");
      } else {
        result.codigo = String(row.codigo ?? "");
        const blocks = Array.isArray(row.bloques) ? row.bloques : String(row.bloques ?? "").split("|");
        result.bloques = [...new Set(blocks.map(value => String(value).trim()).filter(Boolean))];
        if (result.bloques.some(value => value.includes("|"))) throw Error("Bloques inválidos.");
      }
      return result;

  function parseTableCSV(text, kind, reservedIds = []) {
    const records = [];
    let record = [], value = "", quoted = false, closed = false;
    text = String(text).replace(/^\ufeff/, "");
    for (let i = 0; i <= text.length; i++) {

        if (c === undefined) throw Error("CSV con comillas incompletas.");
        if (c === '"') {
          if (text[i + 1] === '"') { value += '"'; i++; }
          else { quoted = false; closed = true; }
        } else value += c;
      } else if (c === ";" || c === "\r" || c === "\n" || c === undefined) {

        value = ""; closed = false;
````
