/* Optional GNAT panel. Only local status/MOCK routes; no corporate transport. */
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const textElement=(tag,text)=>{const el=document.createElement(tag);el.textContent=String(text??'');return el;};
export function renderAsset(host,asset){
  host.replaceChildren();
  const heading=textElement('h3',asset.synthetic?'MOCK · geometría SINTÉTICA':'MOCK · activo de ejemplo');
  const fields=[['Código',asset.code],['ID interno',asset.id],['Tipo',asset.asset_type],
    ['Geometría',asset.geometry?.type||'No disponible'],['Metadatos',asset.metadata||'Sin datos'],
    ['Procedencia',asset.source],['Consulta local del fixture',asset.consulted_at]];
  const list=document.createElement('dl');
  for(const [label,value] of fields)list.append(textElement('dt',label),textElement('dd',value));
  host.append(heading,list);
}
export async function initGNAT(api,map){
  let asset=null;
  const status=$('gnat-status'),host=$('gnat-asset'),overlay=$('gnat-overlay');
  $('gnat-consult').disabled=true;
  overlay.disabled=true;
  function clear(){asset=null;overlay.checked=false;overlay.disabled=true;map.setAssetGeometry(null);host.replaceChildren();}
  function show(next){
    clear();asset=next;renderAsset(host,asset);overlay.disabled=!asset.geometry;
  }
  overlay.addEventListener('change',()=>{
    map.setAssetGeometry(overlay.checked?asset?.geometry:null);
    if(overlay.checked)map.fitAssetGeometry();
  });
  $('gnat-clear').addEventListener('click',()=>{clear();status.textContent='Ficha y capa MOCK eliminadas de esta sesión; GNAT sigue desconectado.';});
  $('gnat-mock').addEventListener('click',async()=>{
    const button=$('gnat-mock');button.disabled=true;clear();status.textContent='Cargando fixture MOCK local…';
    try{
      const query=$('gnat-query').value;
      const type=$('gnat-type').value;
      const result=await api('/api/gnat/mock',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({query,asset_types:type?[type]:[]})});
      if(!result.ok)throw Error(result.error?.message||'No se pudo cargar el fixture');
      const matches=result.data.matches;
      if(matches.length===1){show(matches[0]);status.textContent='MOCK cargado. GNAT desconectado; ningún punto de trabajo fue modificado.';}
      else if(matches.length>1){
        status.textContent='MOCK ambiguo: seleccione el ID deseado.';
        for(const item of matches){const pick=textElement('button',item.code+' · ID '+item.id);pick.addEventListener('click',()=>show(item));host.appendChild(pick);}
      } else status.textContent='Sin coincidencia exacta en fixtures MOCK. Pruebe F5353, SINTETICO_LINEA o SINTETICO_MULTILINEA.';
    }catch(error){status.textContent='Error MOCK: '+error.message+' Los puntos locales siguen disponibles.';}
    finally{button.disabled=false;}
  });
  try{
    const connection=await api('/api/gnat/status');
    // No live adapter is implemented, so a remote-query button must never become active.
    status.textContent=connection.message;
  }catch(error){status.textContent='No se pudo comprobar el estado local de GNAT: '+error.message;}
}
````

<!doctype html>
<html lang="es"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
<title>Centrality Local V5 · Visor modular</title><link rel="stylesheet" href="/styles.css"></head>
<body>
<header class="topbar"><div class="brand"><span class="brand-symbol">⌁</span><div><strong>Centrality Local</strong><small>Visor cartográfico independiente · Piloto</small></div></div>
<div class="top-actions"><span id="status-top" class="subtle">Servidor local</span><button id="toggle-sidebar" class="secondary">☰ Panel</button></div></header>
<div class="layout" id="layout">
  <aside class="sidebar" id="sidebar">
    <nav class="tabs" aria-label="Módulos"><button data-tab="puntos" class="active">Puntos</button><button data-tab="masiva">Masiva</button><button data-tab="capturas">Mosaico</button><button data-tab="capas">Capas</button><button data-tab="gnat">GNAT</button></nav>
    <section class="tab-section" id="tab-puntos">
      <h2>Puntos y supervisores</h2>
      <p class="muted">Importa KMZ, KML o CSV. Los datos quedan en SQLite local.</p>
      <label class="upload">Importar KMZ / KML / CSV <input id="import-file" type="file" accept=".kmz,.kml,.csv" hidden></label>
      <label class="field"><span>Formato de exportación</span><select id="export-kind"><option value="legacy">Todos (CSV compatible)</option><option value="route">Recorrido (v6)</option><option value="opening">Aperturas (v6)</option></select></label>
      <div class="toolbar"><button id="download-csv" class="secondary">Exportar CSV</button><button id="fit-all" class="secondary">Ver todos</button></div>
      <div id="counters" class="counters">0 puntos</div>
      <label class="field"><span>Buscar por número o código</span><input id="find" type="search" placeholder="Ej: 68, F61663"></label>
      <div class="toolbar"><select id="filter-supervisor" aria-label="Filtrar supervisor"><option value="">Todos los supervisores</option><option value="_none">Sin supervisor</option><option>Verde</option><option>Naranjo</option><option>Azul</option></select><button id="check-all" class="secondary">Seleccionar visibles</button></div>
      <label class="field"><span>Bloque de trabajo</span><select id="filter-block"><option value="">Todos los bloques</option></select></label>
      <div class="table-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th>✓</th><th>N°</th><th>Código</th><th>Bloque</th></tr></thead><tbody id="point-rows"></tbody></table></div>
      <div class="assignment"><span id="selected-count">0 seleccionados</span><div class="toolbar"><button data-assign="" class="tag-red">Sin asignar</button><button data-assign="Verde" class="tag-green">Verde</button><button data-assign="Naranjo" class="tag-orange">Naranjo</button><button data-assign="Azul" class="tag-blue">Azul</button></div></div>
      <div id="point-editor" class="editor hidden"><h3>Editar punto <span id="editor-num"></span></h3>
        <label class="field"><span>Código</span><input id="edit-code" maxlength="180"></label>
        <div class="toolbar"><label class="field grow"><span>Tipo</span><select id="edit-type"><option value="sin clasificar">Sin clasificar</option><option value="poste">Poste</option><option value="subestacion">Subestación</option><option value="otro">Otro</option></select></label><label class="field grow"><span>Supervisor</span><select id="edit-supervisor"><option value="">Sin supervisor</option><option>Verde</option><option>Naranjo</option><option>Azul</option></select></label></div>
        <label class="field"><span>Categoría</span><input id="edit-category"></label>
        <label class="field"><span>Descripción</span><textarea id="edit-description" rows="2"></textarea></label>
        <button id="save-point" class="primary">Guardar cambios</button>
      </div>
    </section>
    <section class="tab-section hidden" id="tab-masiva"><h2>Buscar varios códigos</h2>
      <p class="muted">Coincidencia exacta en tu base local. No consulta automáticamente GNAT/Centrality.</p>
      <label class="field"><span>Un código por línea (máx. 500)</span><textarea id="batch-codes" rows="7" placeholder="F697480&#10;F61663&#10;F51757"></textarea></label>
      <div class="toolbar"><label class="checkline"><input type="checkbox" id="type-poste" checked> Poste</label><label class="checkline"><input type="checkbox" id="type-sed" checked> Subestación</label><label class="checkline"><input type="checkbox" id="type-unclassified"> Sin clasificar</label></div>
      <button id="batch-search" class="primary wide">Buscar coincidencias exactas</button>
      <div id="batch-summary" class="muted spacing"></div><div id="batch-results" class="batch-results"></div>
    </section>
    <section class="tab-section hidden" id="tab-capturas"><h2>Mosaico local</h2>
      <p class="muted">Importa capturas georreferenciadas de Centrality. Se superponen sobre el mapa sin depender de su carga en vivo.</p>
      <label class="upload">Importar captura JSON <input type="file" accept=".json" id="capture-file" hidden></label>
      <p class="hint">Usa el snippet de <strong>bridge/exportar_vista_centrality.js</strong> dentro de Centrality para generar el archivo.</p>
      <div class="notice">Piloto: capturas manuales verificables. Aún <strong>no</strong> recorre automáticamente 100 km ni descarga toda la red.</div>
      <div id="capture-list" class="capture-list"></div>
    </section>
    <section class="tab-section hidden" id="tab-capas"><h2>Capas visibles</h2>
      <label class="switch"><input id="toggle-osm" type="checkbox"><span>Mapa base OpenStreetMap</span></label>
      <p class="hint">Desactivado al iniciar. Al activarlo solicita teselas externas; requiere Internet para visualizar las teselas normales de navegación.</p>
      <label class="switch"><input id="toggle-captures" type="checkbox" checked><span>Capturas eléctricas locales</span></label>
      <label class="switch"><input id="toggle-numbers" type="checkbox" checked><span>Mostrar números</span></label>
      <label class="switch"><input id="toggle-codes" type="checkbox"><span>Mostrar códigos</span></label>
      <label class="field"><span>Visibilidad de etiquetas</span><select id="label-mode"><option value="cerca">Número al acercar</option><option value="siempre">Siempre números</option><option value="ninguna">Ocultar etiquetas</option></select></label>
      <div class="notice"><strong>Estado MT/BT:</strong> aún no hay conexión directa a las capas WMTS privadas de Centrality. El visor muestra únicamente capturas que tú importes, sin trasladar credenciales.</div>
      <h3>Arquitectura</h3><p class="muted">Python (servidor) · SQLite (una tabla de puntos) · JavaScript por módulos · canvas cartográfico.</p>
    </section>
    <section class="tab-section hidden" id="tab-gnat">
      <h2>GNAT · consulta opcional</h2>
      <p id="gnat-status" role="status">Desconectado · pendiente de configurar.</p>
      <p class="muted">La ficha de activo oficial es independiente de los puntos de trabajo. No se vincula ni actualiza SQLite automáticamente.</p>
      <button id="gnat-consult" disabled title="Requiere contrato, autorización y conexión validados">Consultar GNAT</button>
      <h3>Prueba local sin GNAT</h3>
      <p class="hint">Solo MOCK. F5353 reproduce el Point del informe; las líneas son SINTÉTICAS y no representan un tramo GNAT confirmado.</p>
      <label class="field"><span>Código exacto del fixture</span><input id="gnat-query" value="F5353" maxlength="180" list="gnat-examples"></label>
      <datalist id="gnat-examples"><option value="F5353"><option value="SINTETICO_LINEA"><option value="SINTETICO_MULTILINEA"></datalist>
      <label class="field"><span>Tipo de activo MOCK</span><select id="gnat-type"><option value="">Todos</option><option value="poste">Poste</option><option value="subestacion">Subestación</option><option value="tramo">Tramo sintético</option></select></label>
      <button id="gnat-mock" class="primary">Buscar fixture MOCK</button>
      <button id="gnat-clear" class="secondary">Borrar ficha MOCK</button>
      <label class="switch"><input id="gnat-overlay" type="checkbox" disabled><span>Mostrar geometría MOCK en el mapa</span></label>
      <div id="gnat-asset" class="asset-detail" aria-live="polite"></div>
      <p class="hint">Datos efímeros en memoria, sin caché oficial. Consulte docs/CONTRATO_GNAT.md para el diagnóstico manual.</p>
    </section>
    <div class="sidebar-footer" id="message" role="status">Listo para importar datos.</div>
  </aside>
  <main class="map-area"><canvas id="map" aria-label="Mapa interactivo"></canvas><div class="map-top-right"><button class="zoom-btn" id="zoom-in" title="Acercar">+</button><button class="zoom-btn" id="zoom-out" title="Alejar">−</button></div><div class="map-hint">Arrastra para mover · Rueda para zoom · Clic en un punto para editarlo</div><div class="map-footer"><span id="map-coordinates">Zoom —</span><span class="attribution">© OpenStreetMap contributors (cuando está activo)</span></div></main>
</div>
<script type="module" src="/app.js"></script>
</body></html>

/* Independent canvas WebMercator viewer. No mapping SDK/API key required.
   Ordinary interactive OSM tiles only (no offline bulk download).
   Authorized georeferenced captures are loaded from this local server. */
const MAX_LAT = 85.05112878;
const HALF_MERC = 20037508.342789244;
export const clamp = (n,a,b) => Math.min(b,Math.max(a,n));
const RAD=Math.PI/180;
function mercatorFromGeo(lon,lat) {
  lat=clamp(lat,-MAX_LAT,MAX_LAT);
  const s=Math.sin(lat*RAD);
  return [(lon+180)/360, 0.5-Math.log((1+s)/(1-s))/(4*Math.PI)];
}
function geoFromMercator(x,y) {
  return [x*360-180, Math.atan(Math.sinh(Math.PI*(1-2*y)))/RAD];
}
function mercatorMetresToWorld(x,y) {
  return [(x+HALF_MERC)/(2*HALF_MERC), 0.5-y/(2*HALF_MERC)];
}

const COLORS={'':'#e24040','Verde':'#198754','Naranjo':'#ed8915','Azul':'#2775df'};
export const matchesBlock = (point, block) => !block || (point.bloques||[]).includes(block);
export const pointLabel = (point, numbers, codes) => [numbers?String(point.numero):'', codes?point.codigo:''].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
export const layerProviders = [
  {id:'captures',name:'Capturas locales',available:true},
  {id:'wmts',name:'WMTS autenticado',available:false,reason:'Contrato y acceso no validados'}
];
export class LocalMap {
  constructor(canvas, {onPick}={}) {
    this.canvas=canvas;
    this.ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
    this.view={lon:-72.6,lat:-38.74,zoom:10};
    this.points=[];this.captures=[];this.active=null;
    this.showOSM=false;this.showNumbers=true;this.showCodes=false;this.showCaptures=true;this.labels='cerca';
    this.onPick=onPick||(()=>{});
    this.tiles=new Map();this.captureImages=new Map();
    this.pixelRatio=1;this.width=1;this.height=1;
    this.pendingDraw=false;
    this.drag=null;
    this.installEvents();
    const observer = new ResizeObserver(()=>this.resize());
    observer.observe(canvas.parentElement);
    this.observer=observer;
    this.resize();
  }
  resize(){
    const rect=this.canvas.parentElement.getBoundingClientRect();
    this.width=Math.max(100,Math.floor(rect.width));
    this.height=Math.max(100,Math.floor(rect.height));
    this.pixelRatio=Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio||1,2);
    this.canvas.width=Math.round(this.width*this.pixelRatio);
    this.canvas.height=Math.round(this.height*this.pixelRatio);
    this.canvas.style.width=this.width+'px';this.canvas.style.height=this.height+'px';
    this.render();
  }
  worldSize(){return 256*Math.pow(2,this.view.zoom);}
  centerWorld(){return mercatorFromGeo(this.view.lon,this.view.lat);}
  toScreen(lon,lat){
    const [x,y]=mercatorFromGeo(lon,lat),[cx,cy]=this.centerWorld(),scale=this.worldSize();
    return [this.width/2+(x-cx)*scale,this.height/2+(y-cy)*scale];
  }
  screenToGeo(sx,sy){
    const [cx,cy]=this.centerWorld(),scale=this.worldSize();
    return geoFromMercator(cx+(sx-this.width/2)/scale,cy+(sy-this.height/2)/scale);
  }
  setView(lon,lat,zoom=this.view.zoom){
    this.view={lon:clamp(lon,-180,180),lat:clamp(lat,-MAX_LAT,MAX_LAT),zoom:clamp(zoom,2,20)};
    this.render();
  }
  setZoomAt(nextZoom,sx,sy){
    const before=this.screenToGeo(sx,sy);
    this.view.zoom=clamp(nextZoom,2,20);
    const after=this.screenToGeo(sx,sy);
    const centerWorld=this.centerWorld();
    const [bx,by]=mercatorFromGeo(...before),[ax,ay]=mercatorFromGeo(...after);
    const corrected=geoFromMercator(centerWorld[0]+bx-ax,centerWorld[1]+by-ay);
    this.setView(...corrected,this.view.zoom);
  }
  fitGeo(points, padding=55) {
    if(!points.length)return;
    const xy=points.map(p=>mercatorFromGeo(p.longitud,p.latitud));
    this.fitWorld(xy,padding);
  }
  fitBounds3857(bounds,padding=50){
    const a=mercatorMetresToWorld(bounds[0],bounds[1]),b=mercatorMetresToWorld(bounds[2],bounds[3]);
    this.fitWorld([a,b],padding);
  }
  fitWorld(xy,padding=55){
    const xs=xy.map(v=>v[0]), ys=xy.map(v=>v[1]);
    const [minx,maxx,miny,maxy]=[Math.min(...xs),Math.max(...xs),Math.min(...ys),Math.max(...ys)];
    const maxAvailableX=Math.max(100,this.width-padding*2),maxAvailableY=Math.max(100,this.height-padding*2);
    const available=Math.min(maxAvailableX/Math.max(0.000001,maxx-minx),maxAvailableY/Math.max(0.000001,maxy-miny));
    const zoom=clamp(Math.log2(available/256),2,18.5);
    const center=geoFromMercator((minx+maxx)/2,(miny+maxy)/2);
    this.setView(...center,zoom);
  }
  setPoints(points){this.points=points;this.render();}
  setAssetGeometry(geometry){this.assetGeometry=geometry;this.render();}
  fitAssetGeometry(){
    const g=this.assetGeometry;if(!g)return;
    const positions=g.type==='Point'?[g.coordinates]:g.type==='LineString'?g.coordinates:g.coordinates.flat();
    this.fitGeo(positions.map(([longitud,latitud])=>({longitud,latitud})));
  }
  drawAssetOverlay(){
    const g=this.assetGeometry;if(!g)return;
    const ctx=this.ctx;ctx.save();ctx.strokeStyle='#783eaf';ctx.fillStyle='#783eaf';ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.setLineDash([7,4]);
    if(g.type==='Point'){
      const [x,y]=this.toScreen(...g.coordinates);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,12,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();
    } else {
      const lines=g.type==='LineString'?[g.coordinates]:g.coordinates;
      for(const line of lines){ctx.beginPath();line.forEach((p,i)=>{const xy=this.toScreen(...p);if(i)ctx.lineTo(...xy);else ctx.moveTo(...xy);});ctx.stroke();}
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }
  setCaptures(captures){this.captures=captures;this.render();}
  setOptions(opts){Object.assign(this,opts);this.render();}
  highlight(id){this.active=id;this.render();}
  installEvents(){
    this.canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
      if(e.button!==0)return;
      this.canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
      this.drag={x:e.offsetX,y:e.offsetY,origin:this.centerWorld(),moved:false};
      this.canvas.classList.add('dragging');
    });
    this.canvas.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
      if(!this.drag)return;
      const dx=e.offsetX-this.drag.x,dy=e.offsetY-this.drag.y;
      if(Math.abs(dx)+Math.abs(dy)>5)this.drag.moved=true;
      const scale=this.worldSize();
      const [cx,cy]=this.drag.origin;
      const geo=geoFromMercator(cx-dx/scale,cy-dy/scale);
      this.view.lon=clamp(geo[0],-180,180);this.view.lat=clamp(geo[1],-MAX_LAT,MAX_LAT);
      this.render();
    });
    this.canvas.addEventListener('pointerup',e=>{
      const moved=this.drag?.moved;
      this.drag=null;this.canvas.classList.remove('dragging');
      if(!moved){
        const p=this.hit(e.offsetX,e.offsetY);
        if(p)this.onPick(p);
      }
    });
    this.canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel',()=>{this.drag=null;this.canvas.classList.remove('dragging');});
    this.canvas.addEventListener('wheel',e=>{
      e.preventDefault();
      this.setZoomAt(this.view.zoom+(e.deltaY<0?0.5:-0.5),e.offsetX,e.offsetY);
    },{passive:false});
    this.canvas.addEventListener('dblclick',e=>{
      e.preventDefault();this.setZoomAt(this.view.zoom+1,e.offsetX,e.offsetY);
    });
  }
  hit(x,y){
    let chosen=null,best=15*15;
    for(const p of this.points){
      const [px,py]=this.toScreen(p.longitud,p.latitud);
      const dist=(x-px)**2+(y-py)**2;
      if(dist<best){best=dist;chosen=p;}
    }
    return chosen;
  }
  getTile(z,x,y){
    const key=z+'/'+x+'/'+y;
    if(this.tiles.has(key))return this.tiles.get(key);
    const img=new Image();const record={img,status:'loading'};
    this.tiles.set(key,record);
    img.onload=()=>{record.status='ready';this.render();};
    img.onerror=()=>{record.status='error';this.render();};
    // This is ordinary interactive tile access, not batch/offline prefetch.
    img.src=`https://tile.openstreetmap.org/${z}/${x}/${y}.png`;
    if(this.tiles.size>350){
      const old=[...this.tiles.keys()].slice(0,100);
      for(const k of old)this.tiles.delete(k);
    }
    return record;
  }
  render(){
    if(this.pendingDraw)return;
    this.pendingDraw=true;
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>{this.pendingDraw=false;this.draw();});
  }
  draw(){
    const ctx=this.ctx,dpr=this.pixelRatio,w=this.width,h=this.height;
    ctx.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);
    ctx.fillStyle='#eef3f0';ctx.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
    const [cx,cy]=this.centerWorld(); const scale=this.worldSize();
    if(this.showOSM){
      const z=Math.floor(this.view.zoom),num=2**z,factor=2**(this.view.zoom-z),tile=256*factor;
      const tx=cx*num,ty=cy*num;
      const startX=Math.floor(tx-w/(2*tile))-1,stopX=Math.ceil(tx+w/(2*tile))+1;
      const startY=Math.max(0,Math.floor(ty-h/(2*tile))-1),stopY=Math.min(num-1,Math.ceil(ty+h/(2*tile))+1);
      for(let y=startY;y<=stopY;y++)for(let x=startX;x<=stopX;x++){
        const wrap=(x%num+num)%num;
        const screenX=w/2+(x-tx)*tile,screenY=h/2+(y-ty)*tile;
        const rec=this.getTile(z,wrap,y);
        if(rec.status==='ready')ctx.drawImage(rec.img,screenX,screenY,tile+.7,tile+.7);
      }
    } else {
      ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.strokeStyle='#dce7e1';
      for(let x=0;x<w;x+=60){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,0);ctx.lineTo(x,h);ctx.stroke();}
      for(let y=0;y<h;y+=60){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,y);ctx.lineTo(w,y);ctx.stroke();}
    }
    if(this.showCaptures){
      const sorted=[...this.captures].sort((a,b)=>{
        const sa=(a.bounds3857[2]-a.bounds3857[0])/a.width;
        const sb=(b.bounds3857[2]-b.bounds3857[0])/b.width;
        return sb-sa;
      });
      for(const capture of sorted){
        const b=capture.bounds3857;
        const [ax,ay]=mercatorMetresToWorld(b[0],b[3]);
        const [bx,by]=mercatorMetresToWorld(b[2],b[1]);
        const left=w/2+(ax-cx)*scale,top=h/2+(ay-cy)*scale;
        const cw=(bx-ax)*scale,ch=(by-ay)*scale;
        if(left>w||top>h||left+cw<0||top+ch<0)continue;
        let image=this.captureImages.get(capture.id);
        if(!image){
          image=new Image();this.captureImages.set(capture.id,image);
          image.onload=()=>this.render();
          image.src='/capturas/'+encodeURIComponent(capture.archivo);
        }
        if(image.complete&&image.naturalWidth>0){ctx.drawImage(image,left,top,cw,ch);}
      }
    }
    this.drawAssetOverlay();
    ctx.textBaseline='middle';ctx.textAlign='center';
    for(const p of this.points){
      const [x,y]=this.toScreen(p.longitud,p.latitud);
      if(x< -100||y< -100||x>w+100||y>h+100)continue;
      const selected=this.active===p.id;
      const radius=selected?9:6;
      ctx.beginPath();
      if(p.clase==='apertura'){
        ctx.moveTo(x-radius,y-radius);ctx.lineTo(x+radius,y+radius);
        ctx.moveTo(x+radius,y-radius);ctx.lineTo(x-radius,y+radius);
        ctx.strokeStyle='#fff';ctx.lineWidth=selected?6:5;ctx.stroke();
        ctx.strokeStyle=COLORS[p.supervisor]||COLORS[''];ctx.lineWidth=selected?4:3;ctx.stroke();
      } else {
        ctx.arc(x,y,radius,0,Math.PI*2);
        ctx.fillStyle=COLORS[p.supervisor]||COLORS[''];ctx.fill();
        ctx.strokeStyle=selected?'#20334a':'#fff';ctx.lineWidth=selected?3:1.6;ctx.stroke();
      }
      if(this.labels==='siempre'||(this.labels==='cerca'&&this.view.zoom>=13.2)){
        const label=pointLabel(p,this.showNumbers,this.showCodes);
        ctx.font='bold 12px Arial';ctx.lineJoin='round';ctx.lineWidth=3.8;
        ctx.strokeStyle='#102239';ctx.strokeText(label,x,y-15);
        ctx.fillStyle='#fff';ctx.fillText(label,x,y-15);
      }
    }
    const z=this.view.zoom.toFixed(1);
    const el=document.getElementById('map-coordinates');
    if(el)el.textContent=`Zoom ${z}  ·  ${this.view.lat.toFixed(5)}, ${this.view.lon.toFixed(5)}`;
  }
}

# GNAT V6 portable handoff

A **private, source-preserving coding handoff**, not a finished GNAT integration or authorized public release. Start here, not in the historical snapshots.

## Quick path

1. Read [start here](START_HERE.md), [provenance](V6_PROVENANCE.md), then [current state and gaps](STATE_AND_GAPS.md).
2. For an AI without repository tools, upload [GNAT_V6_CHAT_CONTEXT.md](GNAT_V6_CHAT_CONTEXT.md). It contains the full inventoried text corpus, not just summaries. Prefer upload to pasting its large contents; no particular provider's context/file limits are assumed.
3. For coding tools or a private repository, use `snapshot/` as the project root. [AI working guide](AI_WORKING_GUIDE.md) defines safe scope; [code map](CODE_MAP.md) locates original files.
4. Check [validation](VALIDATION.md), [source manifest](SOURCE_MANIFEST.json), [checksums](SHA256SUMS.txt) and [exclusions](EXCLUSIONS.md) before sharing.

## Contents

- Byte-exact `snapshot/v6.txt`: user-designated V6 browser-console IIFE. Its internal title/log still say CENTRALITY V4.
- Historical V4/legacy references; separate Python/SQLite/web Local application; diagnostic/export snippets; demo CSVs; offline tests.
- Complete allowlisted research corpus: current ledger, response-contract/coverage catalogs, historical investigations, transformed observed and synthetic fixtures, observer source/tests and three historical ODD task records.
- New English orientation guides and recovered interrupted-sector findings. Original files retain their languages and bytes.

The manifest is the exact inclusion list: 66 copied originals, 585,420 source bytes. No universal completeness claim is made. Saved portal/config assets, real data, credentials, private captures, caches and full raw conversation are not included. Originals were not deleted or rewritten.

## Two different execution contexts

| Context | Status |
| --- | --- |
| `snapshot/v6.txt` | Host-dependent console JavaScript; NOT standalone HTML, bookmarklet or Local application. No browser execution performed. |
| `snapshot/iniciar.py`, `app/`, `web/` | Existing Local prototype. GNAT disconnected, explicit MOCK fixtures only. Later research does not mean integration was implemented. |

Read the [original README](snapshot/README.md) for existing Local usage and tests. Running it may create local data; OSM opt-in sends external tile requests. This handoff does not authorize corporate requests, observer attachment, browser changes, portal execution, uploads or remote publishing.

## Private GitHub preparation

The directory can enter a separately authorized **private** repository after privacy/rights review. No repository initialized, staging/commit/remote operation or license added. [Publication boundary](EXCLUSIONS.md#publication-boundary) is mandatory. Original ignore policies are archived inertly because they hide included source; the new active `.gitignore` does not hide V6/V4 source.

# Resume GNAT without repeating the investigation

## Original objective

The user needs a portable, technically detailed baseline to continue GNAT/Centrality coding with ChatGPT or Gemini without this chat or browser tooling. The larger project concerns a Windows Local map/work-point workflow, exact asset-code consultation/geolocation, technical fiches and eventually trustworthy network/sector analysis. This delivery preserves the knowledge needed to resume; it does not implement future integration.

## First reading order

1. [V6 provenance](V6_PROVENANCE.md): identify exact base before editing.
2. [State and gaps](STATE_AND_GAPS.md): distinguish working code, bounded evidence and proposals.
3. Current ledger: [EVIDENCIA_CONFIRMADA](snapshot/gnat-research/EVIDENCIA_CONFIRMADA.md), [CONTRATOS](snapshot/gnat-research/CONTRATOS.md), [COBERTURA](snapshot/gnat-research/COBERTURA.md), [PENDIENTES](snapshot/gnat-research/PENDIENTES.md).
4. [Recovered sector observations](RECOVERED_SECTOR_OBSERVATIONS.md): later findings absent from those original files; no raw response export available.
5. [Code map](CODE_MAP.md), [AI working guide](AI_WORKING_GUIDE.md), then complete source files actually needed for the requested change.

## Source-of-truth rules

- Original source bytes/hashes establish what code exists, not correctness in every case.
- Current ledger supersedes old search/selection report gaps only for covered samples. Preserve historical reports rather than flattening them into universal current facts.
- Recovered sector findings supplement, not silently rewrite, the ledger. They are not a machine-readable electrical graph.
- Transformed observed fixtures preserve selected contracts, not raw bodies. Synthetic fixtures are invented. Neither is authoritative inventory.
- Implementation/geolocation/model documents are proposals. Source/tests determine implemented behavior.
- Empty cached tabs, dropped fields, omitted payloads and failed collection are not negative business evidence.

## Context that must survive

S/E means substation. Transformer **CD of S/E** differs from pole **COD**; do not invent an expansion of CD. Physical hosting, UI hierarchy and electrical connectivity are distinct relations. Codes can return several asset kinds/IDs: preserve ambiguity and identity roles. A line, shared label, crossing or nearby coordinate is not proof of electrical connection.

There is enough evidence to design consultation/maps/fiches with honest uncertainty. There is NOT enough for rigorous sector/isolation conclusions or maneuver guidance. Read permission, retention rights, authentication, graph relations and state quality/freshness remain feature-specific gates.

## Next step

Select one explicitly requested behavior, preserve unrelated working behavior and test locally using fixtures. Start with bounded consultation/geometry/fiches and non-destructive UI. Never auto-enable corporate transport or infer session availability grants permission. Blocked electrical/operational scope stays blocked.
````

# Exact V6 baseline and provenance

| Field | Verified value |
| --- | --- |
| Original path | `v6.txt` |
| Packaged path | `snapshot/v6.txt` |
| Size | 67,852 bytes |
| SHA256 | `48383BB3991DC5C6CD5DA246292C6EA9CC5807741068E517F71F2DDF178CBE66` |
| Version interpretation | User-designated V6 filename; internal header/title/log retain CENTRALITY V4. Not a verified internal semantic release. |

No source label, newline, encoding, export or algorithm changed. In the master Markdown, UTF-8 text is embedded verbatim; snapshot/manifest remain authoritative for exact bytes. Fences/metadata outside originals are not source content.

## Runtime boundary

The base is a browser-console JavaScript IIFE expecting existing same-origin `iframe#content`, `frame.contentWindow.webMap_.map`, `w.ol` and host map/layer/source resources. Native browser Canvas, Blob, KML/KMZ and `DecompressionStream('deflate-raw')` are platform assumptions. No JSZip/CDN dependency was found in the verified base. Static findings are not a supported-browser guarantee.

It is not standalone HTML, a bookmarklet or the Local app. Local Python/web does not host/replace the IIFE automatically. Do not rename `v6.txt` to historical `v4.js` to make old tests appear to cover it.

The base has CommonJS exports for six CSV helpers and an early no-DOM return; this alone does not prove browser behavior. Packaging performs syntax-only `vm.Script` parsing without running the IIFE. Existing `v4.test.js` reads `v4.js`; label its results V4 coverage.

## Preservation chain

`SOURCE_MANIFEST.json` maps each original to copy path, size, SHA256, role, evidence class and private-handoff classification. Source hashes are checked pre/post copy and against ZIP members. Generated guides have final checksums in `SHA256SUMS.txt`, excluding itself. ZIP checksum is outside ZIP, avoiding recursion.

Original root/research `.gitignore` files are unchanged under `original-ignore-policies/` with inert `.original` suffixes. This is the only exception to `snapshot/<original path>`. Their old exclusions would otherwise hide V6/V4 sources. Mapping is explicit in the manifest.

# Current state, chronology and safe roadmap

## Implemented versus researched

| Area | Actual state |
| --- | --- |
| Browser base | Existing user-designated `v6.txt`, host-dependent console code; not executed for delivery. |
| Independent Local app | Existing Windows/Python/SQLite/web prototype; GNAT disconnected, explicit MOCK only. |
| Search/geometry/fiches research | Bounded successful real samples and UI/client contracts, not production adapter. |
| Standalone inspector | Offline verified; one discovery failure, zero records; no live attachment/clean-stop proof. |
| Graph/sector/isolation | Relations and temporal state quality unverified; NOT operationally ready. |

## Chronology

1. Browser tooling and Local established work-point CSV/import/map behavior; legacy references remain separate.
2. Search/selection reports covered known codes and host/client behavior, with raw-body gaps at that time.
3. Passive inspector was built/tested offline. One live attempt failed at exact loopback discovery. MCP is a separate evidence channel.
4. Later bounded MCP research confirmed Point and one MT LineString wrappers, a two-ID FeatureCollection, definition/widget/parts/external-ID responses, sample CRS-consumer behavior. Current ledger records sequence; old reports remain preserved.
5. Interrupted sector investigation added inert electrical-response headings/client candidates, not graph rows or valid state data. See [recovered observations](RECOVERED_SECTOR_OBSERVATIONS.md).
6. This handoff copies safe actual corpus and supplements available conversation context. Integration source was not changed.

## Confirmed sample facts

- Search can be ambiguous: F514139 returned pole/DC/DBC/DF candidates; F56822 longer codes and duplicate labels with distinct IDs. Code is not a global key.
- `object_id` and `surrogate_in_map_id` remain distinct roles, even when sample values match.
- Geometry success/data can be singleton Feature or two-ID FeatureCollection. Observed line has two vertices; source bbox differs from vertex extent. Preserve both, do not invent endpoints.
- Consumer interprets tested GeoJSON as EPSG:4326; real point transform matched EPSG:3857 view. Raw bodies do not declare a universal CRS contract.
- Successful batch of two is NOT server maximum, order/partial-error/rate guarantee.
- Widget content string/array/null can reorder; join by widget_id. Parts has data/total without success. External-ID success/data has no total.
- UI exposition, generator metadata, row read_only and server authorization are different constraints.

## Domain constraints

S/E is substation; transformer CD of S/E differs from pole COD. Do not invent CD expansion. DC/DF/DBC labels and DF breadcrumb `F514139 -> F66023 -> F514139 [F66023]` are bounded UI evidence, not physical hosting/formal classes/field truth/electrical connectivity.

Raster WMTS PNG MT/BT/infrastructure is visualization. Vector selection and object geometry do not imply global inventory. Coordinates, crossings, shared codes and line labels never authorize inferred electrical edges.

## First safe increments (explicit behavior request required)

1. Preserve current work-point behavior; adapt only requested consultation/fixture path with source/time/status and exact ambiguity handling.
2. Define adapter boundary for observed variants with offline tests. Do not enable transport until permissions/independent access contract confirmed.
3. Add requested geometry/fiches as non-destructive overlays, keeping user data separate. Metadata is text; never execute widget HTML/scripts.
4. Separately scope inspector gaps: bbox/display_name/content/exposition values omitted, point-identify/external-ID bodies not allowlisted, automatic UI correlation and JavaScript exceptions absent.

## Blocked scope

Owner-approved independent auth, read fields, cache/retention/rate rights, negative/error/paging/partial behavior and backend type semantics remain unresolved. Session/port availability is not permission. Area inventory needs an explicitly authorized bounded read service and area, not a sweep/load probe.

Electrical sector/isolation needs node/terminal/edge relations, compatible phase/voltage, circuit semantics, time-valid states, event/record timestamps, freshness/quality/reliability, coverage and independent authorization. Unknown state is not closed/connected. No maneuver/opening instructions or operational widget execution belong here.

## Delivery and evidence gaps

Full raw conversation, original raw responses, portal/configuration, credentials/private captures and field datasets are absent intentionally or unavailable. No receipt, commit, PR or publication claimed. Prior native assessment was high/unassessable; selectorless preflight failed access denied before mutation. This handoff does not repair/bypass that lifecycle.

# Recovered interrupted sector investigation

**Provenance:** parent-session handoff of inert response and loaded-client observations after the original current ledger. Original raw bodies and full transcript unavailable here. These notes distinguish observed content, candidates and unknown behavior, not independently replayed evidence.

## Electrical consultation candidate

Known DF `28557371`, widget `142`, original loader `/backend/saesa/elec_web_object_view.php`, parameter `object_id`. Metadata reported `scripts:true` and read-write exposition. Widget NOT activated; returned scripts NOT executed.

Parent issued one bounded original `Ext.Ajax` request using the known view endpoint and inspected only `responseText` inertly: HTTP200, length4,086, two script blocks. Headings:

`Circuito`, `Name`, `Salida`, `Tensión`, `Vecinos`, `Id`, `Tipo`, `Business Id`, `Navigation`, `Comportamiento de red por fase`, `Jerarquía de red`, `Real`, `Normal`.

Headings prove neither neighbor rows, graph schema, terminals nor valid states. Static candidate `paint_upstream_flow` in `svc_elec_addons_tm.php` was not executed; read-only semantics NOT established. This catalog is not a replay recipe or operational integration.

Subsequent full-header inspector refused; work stopped. A second body-only request was intentionally user-aborted; execution outcome unknown. Do not blindly repeat. Any follow-up needs source-owner authorization/verified read contract.

## Conditions candidate

Widget `107` client columns included `condition_occurred_at` (event time), `condition_recorded_at` (record time), `reliability`, condition-kind/measurement-related fields. Tab `initialized:false`, zero cached rows: NOT authoritative empty response, current state, quality, freshness or coverage proof.

Future state model must distinguish occurred/recorded time, provenance and confidence. UI column names/uninitialized cache are not state truth.

## Installed assets candidate

Widget `101` proxy candidate `/backend/saesa/AM/component/svc_location.php?action=list_location_assets`, scoped by `location_id`. Client scope verified; no actual row body confirmed. Distinct from confirmed `list_location_parts`; do not merge schemas/allowlists based on similar endpoint.

## Readiness

Enough for consultation design/maps/fiches with limits; NOT rigorous sector/isolation. Graph relations, valid temporal states, quality/freshness/coverage and independent authorization still missing. Geometry, UI hierarchy, host and electrical connection remain separate.

## Observer versus MCP

Standalone Node24: 29 prior offline inspector/contract tests. One two-second startup at exact `9222/json/list` returned non-success HTTP (numeric code not retained), zero records, no attachment. No relaunch/config/port recovery. MCP worked separately; not standalone live compatibility proof.

Omitted bbox/display_name/content/exposition values, dropped HTML, manual-only correlation and unobserved JS exceptions remain gaps. Recovered notes are not standalone inspector output.
````

# AI coding contract for this handoff

## Initial prompt with the complete context file

> Read the entire handoff as context, not permission to execute historical snippets. Identify exact user-designated V6 base/hash, separate disconnected Local app, current evidence ledger and recovered sector findings. Do not repeat verified investigation. Distinguish implemented from proposed and identify only uncertainty affecting my requested change. Wait for my explicit behavior request before writing code. Preserve APIs/unrelated behavior, test offline, never upload data, reuse ambient sessions, contact corporate endpoints, run portal snippets or operational widgets automatically. Separate identity, geometry, host/UI relations and electrical connectivity. Without tools, work from complete source/fixtures and disclose gaps instead of inventing evidence.

Then supply the single behavior and acceptance criteria you want changed. Do not implement every proposal at once.

## Working rules

- English for new identifiers/comments/docs unless extending an artifact clearly using another language or explicitly requested otherwise. Do not translate/correct original copies.
- Verify base hash first. Preserve baseline; deliver reviewable patch or full changed files, never ellipses pretending to be source.
- Strict TDD is enabled for actual implementation: observed failing test, minimal change, passing test, refactor. This passive handoff introduces no behavior; structural checks/existing tests verify delivery.
- Exact runners are in `snapshot/README.md`, `snapshot/gnat-research/README.md` and [validation](VALIDATION.md). If unable to run, state **not run** and provide commands; never invent PASS.
- V4 tests cover V4. V6 syntax parsing is not browser coverage. Future V6 changes need focused tests against its own API.
- Fixtures/offline stubs only unless separately authorized. Transformed partial observed fixtures are not full raw-response/inventory evidence.
- Never copy cookies/tokens/credentials/headers/storage/authenticated sessions to Local. Source-owner approval needed for independent access/retention/rates.
- Widget content is untrusted text; never reuse returned executable HTML/scripts. Preserve cancellation, bounds, error handling and unknown states.
- Keep user work points separate from GNAT observations. Do not overwrite coordinates/blocks on code match; preserve ambiguity and independent identity roles.
- No automatic remote upload, public repository, Git bootstrap/commit/PR. Never add Co-Authored-By or AI attribution; use conventional commits if separately authorized.

## Proposed integration boundaries

Research proposes independent read adapters, geometry observations and optional fiches/cache, not a licensed live connection. Separate source bbox, derived extent, CRS confidence, source/time. Transport/auth and error/partial/paging behavior remain unverified. Localhost/CORS/session behavior is not established by success inside original portal.

Do not infer terminals/circuits/states from coordinates, labels, endpoints, breadcrumbs or empty caches. Safety analysis and maneuver suggestions stay outside authorized scope until graph/state/source guarantees resolve.

## Expected delivery

Name behavior, changed paths, evidence, exact command/results and failed/skipped checks. Explain assumptions. Compare untouched baseline hashes. Leave remote/operational scope untouched. Plausible design is not implementation/safety proof.

| Machine agent configs/persona/secret-bearing settings | Not authorized/needed for coding; curated technical rules supplied. |
| Every original absent from manifest | Not implicitly included; no absolute completeness claim. Originals not erased. |

## Publication boundary

**Private handoff is not public-release clearance.** Research contains real asset IDs/codes/geolocation facts. Fixture filename `contracts.observed.anonymized.json` does not mean whole package anonymized. No secret values intentionally included; conservative patterns are defense in depth, not comprehensive security/rights audit.

Before public GitHub or AI-provider upload, obtain permission covering destination/data/operation/account; review identifiers/coordinates/rights. A later public derivative must be separately reviewed with referentially consistent anonymization and evidence labels. Never silently modify preservation snapshot. No license/rights invented.

Original ignore policies inert; new policy protects runtime/private caches without hiding V6/V4. Never blind `git add .` in original workspace.
````

# Validation record

Date: 2026-10-21. No production behavior or reusable executable tooling introduced. Checks ran against the packaged snapshot without installing dependencies, contacting corporate systems or executing V6/portal scripts. Python HTTP tests use local loopback only.

## Existing functional checks: observed passing

Run from `gnat-v6-handoff/snapshot/`:

| Exact command | Observed result |
| --- | --- |
| `node --test gnat-research/tests/*.test.cjs` | 29 passed, 0 failed/skipped. Offline inspector and transformed-contract fixtures, not live handshake. |
| `python -B -m unittest discover -s tests -v` | 32 passed. Temporary local DB/captures and ephemeral loopback HTTP; no corporate transport. |
| `node --test (Get-ChildItem tests/*.test.js \| ForEach-Object FullName)` | 9 passed, 0 failed/skipped. Existing Local DOM/map/diagnostic stubs. PowerShell command; remove Markdown table escape before running. |
| `node --test v4.test.js` | 13 passed, 0 failed/skipped; V4 ONLY, not V6 coverage. |
| `node --check web/app.js` | Exit0. |
| `node --check web/map.js` | Exit0. |
| `node --check web/gnat-client.js` | Exit0. |
| `node --check bridge/diagnostico_contrato_gnat.js` | Exit0. |

83 existing tests passed across the four suites. Separate V6 syntax-only parse, exit0, no execution:

```powershell
node -e "new(require('node:vm').Script)(require('node:fs').readFileSync('v6.txt','utf8')); console.log('V6 syntax parsed only; not executed')"
```

For an unescaped Local JS test command:

```powershell
node --test (Get-ChildItem tests/*.test.js | ForEach-Object FullName)
```

## Structural and preservation checks

- 66 allowlisted originals, 585,420 bytes, read/copy equality and SHA256 asserted. Baseline V6 exact size/hash matches provenance. Original sources remain untouched.
- Explicit package ignore-policy exceptions keep copied V6, V4 and example CSVs visible even beneath the workspace's inherited historical exclusions; all copied original paths were checked using `git check-ignore --stdin` without staging.
- Complete full-text master includes each copied original plus nine generated explanatory Markdown guides, source manifest and new active ignore policy: 77 uniquely anchored appendices. Fences are longer than any backtick run inside their text, not a fixed fence vulnerable to embedded snippets. Source sections are verified against decoded original bytes, not summaries.
- Root and packaged master are identical byte-for-byte. All relative Markdown link paths in explanatory/original documents resolve; final sealing checks the generated master/checksum targets too. External URLs are not fetched or claimed valid. Source document fragment anchors/language rendering are not rewritten or exhaustively checked.
- Conservative credential patterns over allowlisted source text found zero matches: private-key delimiters, AWS access-key shape, long GitHub tokens, long Bearer values and quoted long secret assignments. No excluded private file was opened for that scan. This is not comprehensive security/rights clearance; real IDs/codes remain private.
- No unexpected runtime files remained after tests. Archive entries are generated from explicit copied/generated paths only; reject symlinks, traversal, private/cache/Git paths and compare every decompressed entry byte/hash with the corresponding file.
- `SHA256SUMS.txt` covers all final package files except itself; its hash cannot contain itself. Outer ZIP checksum is external in `GNAT_V6_HANDOFF.zip.sha256`. ZIP decompression checks verify member bytes without writing extracted files.

The final sealing command asserts these invariants before reporting success. No blind staging/commit or original cleanup is performed.

## Skipped, unavailable and pending

- No V6 browser/runtime, portal visual/manual QA, live GNAT integration, independent auth/CORS, real data/capture import or standalone live CDP attachment test. These require distinct authorization/evidence and cannot be inferred from syntax/offline PASS.
- Native review receipt, commit/PR/push/publication not created. Existing repository unborn state, unresolved delivery chain choice and previous preflight access failure remain parent-owned constraints; no gates bypassed.
- No original full transcript/raw response bodies included. Recovered sector findings retain explicit provenance and uncertain aborted request outcome.
- Conservative privacy scan is not an independent exhaustive security audit or public publication license. User/source-owner review required before uploads/sharing.
``

<a id="guide-011"></a>

### .gitignore

- Origin: new handoff artifact, not original GNAT source
- Original locator: `.gitignore`
- Package locator: `.gitignore`
- Role: generated handoff explanation or metadata
- Original/artifact bytes: 251
- SHA256: `0B1DB5AD1D428EF3DF203C081D4FA368EAABB6CFF8561328BB5DC7ED5FDED0E5`

``
# Protect runtime data without hiding preserved source.
!snapshot/v6.txt
!snapshot/v4.js
!snapshot/examples/*.csv
**/__pycache__/
**/*.pyc
**/.pytest_cache/
**/node_modules/
snapshot/datos/*
!snapshot/datos/.keep
snapshot/gnat-research/private/
*.log
``
